# Building Art And Assets

_building, UI, art, animation, audio, asset pipelines, UGC assets._

Evidence base: 1764 extracted items from 548 videos by #7Phantom, 1Coal, AlvinBlox, ArTheDev, Arow, B Ricey, BloxianCode, BloxxedDev, BubuTheDev, ByteBlox, CallMeDogey, Chase Higgins, Creators on the Blox, Crusherfire, Cumin, Daiorg, Deconstructor of Fun, DitchyDevelopments, Duckable, DudeGuy, Emancyphur, Erthlord, FrenkyRBLX, GameMakers, GnomeCode, ItsZyonic, JustDucksey, Leif, LovJak, MonarchDev, Mulch, PineappleDev, PolarBear, Possible Panda, Quez, Raul, RoBuilder, RoDev, Roblox, Roblox Creator Docs, Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Rool-Studio, Roox4, RoyalDev, SimTek Game Development, SmartyRBX, Stewiepfing, Suphi Kaner, TheDankDeveloper, TheDevKing, TheNexusAvenger, VuukStudios, XTB7, Xaiyne, Yann, bubDEV, janisjanis01, mats-dept2, rasdev, studio rook. (2014-12-27 to 2026-10-30). Roblox changes quickly: check the date on each source and see Date context below.

## Overview
Building, art and asset guidance now has two layers. Roblox DevForum and indie creators (2015-2025) give performance tricks, manual welds, Moon Animator, Shadowmap lighting and Gaea terrain. Official Creator Docs (stamped 2026-10) supersede them where they conflict: WeldConstraint, the Animation Editor, LightingStyle, the Generate terrain tool, the Importer, Reimport, MaterialVariant and SurfaceAppearance. The shared themes are cutting draw calls and memory (size textures to surface area, upload each mesh once, strip decor physics and shadows, limit glass and neon), building kits on a snap grid, lighting with local sources and tuned ambient, and passing import, caging and fitting checks before shipping. Indie figures are self-reported.

## Rules and findings
- **Graybox before adding art** (multiple-creators): Block out levels in plain grey parts and playtest the layout before art, so changes stay cheap. Pipeline: concept art, graybox, art build, finished map. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-08 30:55](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=1855), [Roblox Creators 2022-03 30:51](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1851), [Roblox Creators 2022-03 30:51](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1851), [Roblox 2024-09 10:08](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=608). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Research, mood-board and sketch first** (multiple-creators): Spend about a week researching real locations and building a mood board, taking the best parts of several references. Sketch concept art to lock details and get approvals faster. Draw your own references to cut revisions. Check references for AI-generated images. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-03 23:39](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1419), [Roblox Creators 2022-03 23:39](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1419), [Roblox 2024-09 3:53](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=233), [Roblox 2024-09 8:00](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=480), [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 24:37](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=1477). Newest source 2026-03-14.
- **Write an art bible and style sheet; decide style early** (multiple-creators): Document shapes, colours, UI, fonts, padding and character design. Pick palette, texture style (photoreal vs cartoon) and baked vs real-time lighting early, and apply them to UI, decals and assets. Add your own personal style to stand out. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-09 14:21](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=861), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 17:48](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=1068), [Roblox 2018-07 21:36](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1296), [Roblox 2024-09 14:14](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=854). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Keep a few unifying elements consistent; check silhouettes and colour focus** (multiple-creators): Keep 4-5 things consistent (lighting, materials, palette, detail level, silhouette). Games can break other visual rules if shared lighting and materials hold. Keep mesh detail and volume consistent between characters and environment. Make enemies match the map theme. Fill models flat black to check each character or class is recognisable. Use similar hue and saturation across the game and reserve high contrast or complementary colours for focal points. Two or three colours read softer and calmer; one colour is more dramatic. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 22:17](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1337), [Roblox 2018-07 38:53](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=2333), [Roblox 2018-07 13:53](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=833), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-07 0:41](https://youtu.be/fkT8nMnomb8?t=41), [Roblox 2026-10 24:53](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1493). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Treat lighting and atmosphere as foundation** (multiple-creators): Do not leave lighting to last-minute polish. Adjust light direction, intensity and skybox together, and match the skybox to the lighting. Keep the main light off the focal point so it does not flatten it. Atmosphere, clouds and post-processing are underused and high impact. Use sun rays and depth of field for cinematic separation. Counterbalance strong skybox tints when colours must stay accurate. Study a movie still to match its light. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 23:21](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1401), [Roblox 2018-07 23:21](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1401), [Roblox 2018-07 24:58](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1498), [Roblox 2018-07 29:00](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1740), [Roblox Creators 2023-03 1:27](https://youtu.be/CmXyiJw5N_Y?t=87). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Cheap cartoon lighting preset (low-advice, indie)** (one-creator-repeated): Add Atmosphere, Blur, ColorCorrection and SunRays under Lighting. The creator used Blur size 4, Atmosphere density 0.269, a Toolbox skybox, and ColorCorrection brightness 0.1, contrast 0.1, saturation 0.8 with a blue tint. Add strokes or outlines to characters and decorations for a more cartoonish look. Sources: [TheDankDeveloper 2023-06 0:22](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=22), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-06 0:30](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=30), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-06 0:42](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=42), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-06 0:48](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=48), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-06 0:30](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=30). Newest source 2023-10-25.
- **Design UI mobile-first** (multiple-creators): Start from the smallest screen (phone portrait, 320 virtual pixels wide), prioritise essential content, then scale up. Cutting down is easier than fitting a desktop layout onto a phone. Keep UI consistent across phone and tablet and similar on desktop. Console can differ more. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 6:18](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=378), [Roblox 2018-07 22:49](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=1369), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:10:01](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=4201), [Roblox 2018-07 19:59](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=1199). Newest source 2021-09-09.
- **Use Scale, constraints and layouts instead of fixed pixels** (multiple-creators): Use UI layouts and constraints (UIAspectRatioConstraint, UISizeConstraint, UITextSizeConstraint) with Scale sizing so UI works across hundreds of aspect ratios. Use UIListLayout or flex so repeated items stay aligned. UISizeConstraint can enlarge touch hit targets and allow smaller mouse targets. Pair UIGridLayout with a ScrollFrame and script the canvas size. Build each element responsive from the start. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 17:55](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=1075), [Roblox Creators 2023-02 11:08](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=668), [Roblox Creators 2023-02 12:34](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=754), [Roblox Creators 2023-03 0:30](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=30), [Roblox Creators 2023-03 1:10](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=70). Newest source 2025-09-26.
- **Keep text legible and easy to read** (multiple-creators): Enable TextScaled with a min/max (UITextSizeConstraint, example 24-64); longer translations such as German or Spanish shrink scaled text. Do not shrink fonts past legibility; use two lines or a scroll bar. Use bold for thin fonts. Break long text into short paragraphs, use rich text for key terms, use icons when referencing UI. Sans-serif is generally easier (low-advice). For floating labels: BillboardGui on a transparent part, TextScaled, Scale not Offset, plus UIStroke and UIGradient (indie). Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 22:18](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1338), [Roblox 2025-09 22:18](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1338), [Roblox Creators 2023-05 8:12](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=492), [Roblox 2018-07 14:12](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=852), [Roblox 2018-07 14:53](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=893). Newest source 2025-09-26.
- **Respect mobile thumb zones; test in the device emulator** (multiple-creators): Put main actions in bottom-corner thumb zones (right side), keep few controls, show context buttons only when needed, and avoid red corners and the Roblox top menu, bottom-left move and bottom-right jump areas. Make touch targets big with spacing. Test every UI in the emulator. Keep core UI in a safe central layout and move overflow (e.g. leaderboards) to a 3D panel. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 7:42](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=462), [Roblox 2018-07 7:11](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=431), [Roblox 2018-07 12:05](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=725), [Roblox Creators 2023-02 19:43](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1183), [Roblox Creators 2023-02 14:12](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=852). Newest source 2023-03-02.
- **Favour readable, simple UI** (multiple-creators): Understandable matters more than stylised. Standardise padding (about 15px), corner radius (6px) and a limited palette with gradients. Fix consistency first, then remove clutter. Use clear icons. Keep font, spacing and colour consistent within a hierarchy. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-03 6:40](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=400), [Roblox Creators 2023-03 6:40](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=400), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 10:34](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=634), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 10:34](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=634), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 11:48](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=708). Newest source 2023-05-25.
- **Support gamepad, console and TV** (multiple-creators): Lay out buttons in a grid, enable Selectable, and place frequent buttons near the starting focus. Support d-pad, select and back. On TV, scale interactive UI about 1.5x and simplify. Use a live context-sensitive controls HUD, map buttons by convention (A jump, B secondary, X main) and allow customisation. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 12:39](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=759), [Roblox Creators 2023-03 7:59](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=479), [Roblox 2024-09 15:04](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=904), [Roblox 2024-09 15:04](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=904), [Roblox 2024-09 16:27](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=987). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Use style system for adaptive and themed UI** (multiple-creators): Swap style tags instead of duplicating instances for orientation. Use the viewport display size API (small/medium/large) to decide content. Define themes for light/dark, seasonal and high-contrast packs. Structure UI as a top-level initializer module with per-feature init modules. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 13:39](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=819), [Roblox 2025-09 14:06](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=846), [Roblox 2025-09 23:43](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1423), [Leif 2025-08 8:10](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=490). Newest source 2025-09-26.
- **Plan localization from the start** (multiple-creators): Use string tables and localization infrastructure. Export the table, do a rough machine translation (Google Sheets), fix broken parameter tokens, re-import CSV and test. Use icons for weapons and currency, and localization format strings for numbers. Russian has 3 plural forms. Write a helper module and plugin. Leave room for longer text. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 4:40](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=280), [Roblox 2018-07 4:19](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=259), [Roblox 2018-07 9:56](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=596), [Roblox 2018-07 12:02](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=722), [Roblox 2018-07 21:28](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=1288). Newest source 2023-05-25.
- **Add real cultural flavour beyond translation** (multiple-creators): Translation is baseline. Add local art, music, references and events, tailor art per region, and stay authentic to the IP. Humour tends to translate. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 18:03](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=1083), [Roblox 2024-09 15:11](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=911), [Roblox 2024-09 22:33](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1353), [Roblox 2024-09 35:49](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=2149), [Roblox 2024-09 23:14](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1394). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Design for colour and visual accessibility** (multiple-creators): Use contrast that follows WCAG, checked with a calculator. Avoid white text on yellow. Tweak shades rather than abandon the palette. Add outlines or secondary colours, use brightness differences (red-green is not the only deficiency), and replace colour-only mechanics with symbols. Offer a contrast slider rather than colourblind filters, which look muddy. Highlight objects with prompts or billboards instead of intense red neon. Offer UI scaling. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:09:02](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=4142), [Roblox Creators 2023-04 0:30](https://youtu.be/uumqDp020jk?t=30), [Roblox Creators 2023-04 0:07](https://youtu.be/syQcaeRqqc4?t=7), [Roblox Creators 2023-04 1:31](https://youtu.be/syQcaeRqqc4?t=91), [Roblox Creators 2023-04 2:06](https://youtu.be/syQcaeRqqc4?t=126). Newest source 2023-05-25.
- **Keep rendering cheap: parts, terrain, unions, transparency** (one-creator-repeated): Low-end mobile budget is tens of thousands of parts per area. Terrain is efficient, so do not export it to meshes or hollow it below 4-8 voxels thick. Unions do not fix performance: keep to dozens of parts. Use Roblox materials over SurfaceAppearance where possible. Avoid partial transparency; transparency 1 is cheapest, then 0. Do not hide parts with decals. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 24:29](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1469), [Roblox 2018-07 28:26](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1706), [Roblox 2018-07 28:26](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1706), [Roblox 2018-07 27:04](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1624), [Roblox 2018-07 31:22](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1882). Newest source 2018-07-24.
- **Use instancing, simple collision and split ScreenGuis** (one-creator-repeated): Cloned mesh parts keep memory roughly constant, and resizing or recolouring does not block instancing for mesh parts. Wedges were not instanced at the time (2018). Use Box or Hull collision fidelity. Split ScreenGuis by update frequency. Newer: Voronoi-based convex decomposition auto-picks collision balance. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 12:57](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=777), [Roblox 2018-07 12:57](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=777), [Roblox 2018-07 29:29](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1769), [Roblox 2018-07 17:06](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1026), [Roblox 2026-09 1:06:41](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=4001). Newest source 2026-09-17.
- **Manage texture, audio memory and mesh streaming** (multiple-creators): A 1024x1024 texture is about 5 MB regardless of PNG size (2018); one resident 1024 SurfaceAppearance set is about 2.8 MB, 100 sets about 300 MB (2024). Do not keep audio resident until needed. Prioritise memory over draw calls. Use uniform UV density, avoid atlases and heavy desktop UI textures. Enable mesh streaming via workspace property (opt-in; skinned meshes unsupported) and disable your own LODs to avoid LODs of LODs. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 16:25](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=985), [Roblox 2024-09 30:08](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1808), [Roblox 2024-09 30:08](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1808), [Roblox 2024-09 31:30](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1890), [Roblox 2026-10 27:38](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1658). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Reuse assets and keep art-only cadence updates** (multiple-creators): Recolour, reskin and tweak geometry of existing assets; reuse rigs and animations on similar shapes. Cadence releases should be mostly art, which is easiest to outsource. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-09 10:00](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=600), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 21:03](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1263), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 9:56](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=596), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 9:56](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=596). Newest source 2021-10-21.
- **Use modular, simple, low-detail environments** (multiple-creators): Use modular pieces to mix facades. Keep detail low around avatars and use chunky shapes. Use compact hubs and bottlenecks. Hide map edges with distant buildings, mountains or floating islands (paper-thin tall terrain for distant mountains). Simple part art reaches more devices. Part size is capped, so duplicate parts for large maps. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-03 38:11](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=2291), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 16:52](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=1012), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 46:39](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=2799), [Roblox Creators 2022-03 1:23:17](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=4997), [Daiorg 2025-12 33:52](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=2032). Newest source 2025-12-21.
- **Free and bought assets are fine; organise, credit, and sell packs properly** (multiple-creators): Toolbox/free assets build fast (one indie reported a 99%-free-asset map earning money, unverified). Sources: Unity Asset Store, RoDev Market, game-icons.net, Zapsplat; credit creators; keep imports in folders. Packs earn repeatedly versus commissions; sell pain-point assets. Set Creator to me, organise Workspace/Server Storage/Server Script Storage, add a ReadMe and a preview experience. Programmer art can become identity. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-12 9:13](https://youtu.be/TLRakuu4vd0?t=553), [ItsZyonic 2023-12 13:05](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=785), [TheDankDeveloper 2024-01 4:27](https://youtu.be/DzFVKqdX0VY?t=267), [CallMeDogey 2025-01 2:34](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=154), [CallMeDogey 2025-01 2:34](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=154). Newest source 2026-09-30.
- **UGC accessory modelling and export (Blender)** (one-creator-repeated): Keep triangles low (hat limit 4,000; petals use most of a flower's budget). Unwrap UVs early, build at origin with origin set to Volume, keep parts separate until late, apply array/curve modifiers before Ctrl+J. Fix flipped normals via back face culling, mark sharp after auto smooth. Test an early FBX, export selected objects with FBX unit scale, uncheck Upload to Roblox. Fit on Creator Hub reference avatars. Metalness 100 does not export. UGC applications were limited to selected creators (2023). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-07 4:47](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=287), [Roblox Creators 2025-07 6:08](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=368), [Roblox Creators 2025-07 10:07](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=607), [Roblox Creators 2025-07 11:29](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=689), [Roblox Creators 2025-07 16:17](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=977). Newest source 2025-08-01.
- **Layered clothing rules** (one-creator-repeated): Name meshes MeshName_InnerCage and MeshName_OuterCage with armature 'Armature'. Use Avatar > Accessory Fitting Tool. Stay under 4,000 triangles (example 51,136 to 3,196 after removing subdivision). Fix see-through neck by flipping normals. Auto smooth at 180. Unwrap before adding faces. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-08 5:41](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=341), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 6:22](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=382), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 8:24](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=504), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 11:08](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=668), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 13:48](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=828). Newest source 2025-08-12.
- **UV and texture workflow for items** (one-creator-repeated): Mark seams on hard edges, keep islands away from borders, and give the most UV area to the front. Texture in layers: base, multiply for shadows, overlay/soft light for highlights; 0% hardness brushes, 20-30% smudge. Highlights smaller and sharper than shadows. Keep text UVs straight. Use normal maps instead of geometry, grayscale metallic with inverted roughness, Non-Color for normal/metallic. Maps at 1024 or below. Upload via Surface Appearance. Add real imperfections. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-08 43:13](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=2593), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 46:51](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=2811), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 49:04](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=2944), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 1:00:22](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=3622), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 1:00:22](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=3622). Newest source 2025-08-15.
- **Use PBR and clean topology; low-poly tricks** (multiple-creators): PBR makes accessories realistic and can be published alongside non-PBR. Clean topology and triangle optimisation keep PBR reflections right and performance good; messy sculpt topology caused poor performance. Makeup UGC (planned) uses templates, max 1024x1024. Low-poly tricks: separate faces into parts for per-part colour, shared palette image with UV project from view, Import as One Mesh unchecked for separate parts, inverted-normal bottom-stroke outline, depth-fade mesh for endless pits. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 6:20](https://youtu.be/Tese1QLD3nU?t=380), [Roblox 2023-09 5:16](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=316), [Roblox 2023-09 10:30](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=630), [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 22:33](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=1353), [Roblox 2025-09 23:55](https://youtu.be/Tese1QLD3nU?t=1435). Newest source 2026-09-16.
- **Polish with sound, animation and feedback; use newer rig tooling** (multiple-creators): Use sound groups, layered ambience, real recordings, and 50 weapon sound variants. Make hit feedback unambiguous (directional hits, block sparkles, slowed parries, stagger pushback). Avoid robotic animation; give characters distinct gaits and convey emotion through posture; give items distinct click animations and spring buttons. Adaptive animations play one R15 clip on R6/R15/custom rigs; animation graphs let animators own logic; Auto Setup rigs raw meshes (fills only missing parts). Sources: [Roblox 2023-09 11:11](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=671), [Roblox 2023-09 11:11](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=671), [Roblox 2023-09 20:43](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=1243), [Daiorg 2024-08 2:34](https://youtu.be/Yku_51UE_GM?t=154), [Roblox 2018-07 22:56](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1376). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Use AI tools with references** (multiple-creators): Give AI a UI screenshot and icon packs, verify changes in-game, import PNGs via Asset Manager and set image scaling to Crop. Do not rely on AI for builds or UI without a visual reference. Blender MCP can model low-poly assets. Use AI for repeated simple props, bounding-box on graybox placeholders, and reserve artists for hero assets. Sources: [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 2:03](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=123), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 2:44](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=164), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 4:47](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=287), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 4:47](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=287), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 6:09](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=369). Newest source 2026-10-13.
- **Make ads and icons viewable** (multiple-creators): Image ads need to be viewable under IAB standards (most of the area visible for about a second), or earn nothing. Use Photo Booth for consistent item icons (stroke size 6). Sources: [Roblox 2023-09 28:51](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1731), [Roblox 2023-09 31:16](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1876), [Roblox 2023-09 28:35](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1715), [Leif 2026-09 10:42](https://youtu.be/xVtvDYvUTqc?t=642), [Leif 2026-09 11:23](https://youtu.be/xVtvDYvUTqc?t=683). Newest source 2026-09-16.
- **Check colour legibility in grayscale** (one-creator-repeated): View UI in monochrome. If elements blend in grey, colourblind players will likely miss them too. Keep your palette, then add outlines or tweak brightness on key elements instead of abandoning the look. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-05 28:53](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1733), [Roblox Creators 2023-05 29:34](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1774), [Roblox Creators 2023-05 29:34](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1774). Newest source 2023-05-18.
- **Pair colour with symbols or icons** (multiple-creators): Never convey meaning by colour alone. Add symbols or icons (e.g. team markers, as Super Doomspire does) alongside colour in gameplay and UI so colourblind players can tell states apart. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-05 32:59](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1979), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Pair every sound cue with a visual indicator** (multiple-creators): Do not signal game events only through audio. Add captions or on-screen indicators (e.g. a ringing phone) so hearing-impaired players get the same information. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-05 34:36](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2076), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Expose separate volume controls via sound groups** (single-mention): Offer separate sliders for music, effects and speech. Roblox sound groups make this easy to implement. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-05 35:56](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2156). Newest source 2023-05-18.
- **Ship an accessibility settings menu** (one-creator-repeated): Include in-game settings for volume, camera shake and controls, and make visual and audio options customisable. Examples cited: the Split game by EgoMoose and Minecraft's accessibility settings. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-05 38:00](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2280), [Roblox Creators 2023-05 21:52](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1312). Newest source 2023-05-18.
- **Honour OS text-size and transparency preferences** (one-creator-repeated): Read GuiService.PreferredTextSize and PreferredTransparency. Tag UI backgrounds 'TransparentBack' and multiply BackgroundTransparency by the preferred value. Avoid fixed sizes where text should grow, so larger preferences produce larger UI. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Replace TextScaled with AutomaticSize and TextWrapped** (one-creator-repeated): Official guidance says avoid TextScaled and fixed sizes where text should grow. Use AutomaticSize and TextWrapped so labels expand and wrap, and content-driven sizing prevents overflow as screen size changes. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Respect ReducedMotionEnabled** (single-mention): Check GuiService.ReducedMotionEnabled. When it is true, set tween time to 0 or replace positional motion with a fade-in. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Scale contrast requirements to font size** (multiple-creators): Keep high contrast between text/buttons and backgrounds; avoid light-on-light and dark-on-dark. WCAG-style guidance allows lower contrast ratios for larger or bolder text, so small text needs more contrast. Build hierarchy with size, colour and placement. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2023-05 26:50](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1610), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Choose fonts readable at the smallest mobile size** (single-mention): Size fonts for the smallest expected mobile screen and test on real devices. Avoid thin or decorative fonts. A 2023 panel opinion (not formal research) named Comic Sans and OpenDyslexic as dyslexia-friendly and said serifs are harder for some readers. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design), [Roblox Creators 2023-05 23:57](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1437). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build TV UI at low resolution first** (single-mention): Use relative sizes and positions plus UISizeConstraint, and develop for lower resolutions first so the UI scales up to large TVs. Typical viewing distance is 8-10 feet, so keep hierarchy strong. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep key UI in TV-safe areas and use ScrollingFrame for clutter** (one-creator-repeated): Some TVs crop content at the edges, so keep important UI inside TV-safe areas. As UI scales up on large displays, use ScrollingFrame to reduce on-screen clutter. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Add console shortcuts for essential actions** (single-mention): Provide shortcuts or supplemental buttons for essential in-game actions so controller navigation is faster. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Show device-matched button icons** (single-mention): Use InputActionLabel for Input Action System bindings. Otherwise use GetImageForKeyCode or GetStringForKeyCode so prompts match the player's device. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Add sound to controller-driven UI** (single-mention): Play sound effects to confirm selections and signal menu traversal so controller-driven menus feel responsive. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use haptics sparingly** (single-mention): Use haptic feedback for explosions, collisions, attention events and UI notifications. Avoid constant vibration; overuse annoys players. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Collapse secondary buttons and react to parent size** (one-creator-repeated): On small screens, fold less-important buttons into a menu button and show them in full on desktop. Build panels that react to parent size, e.g. split-view side panels that collapse on narrow screens and expand on wide ones. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Lock shape proportions with UIAspectRatioConstraint** (single-mention): Apply UIAspectRatioConstraint to frames and images so shapes keep their proportions across phone, tablet and PC resolutions. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2025-04 8:50](https://youtu.be/e48wgHQll2Y?t=530). Newest source 2025-04-27.
- **Format large numbers compactly** (one-creator-repeated): Show 5,000+ as compact values such as 1k. Unformatted large numbers are hard for players to read, and the bigger the number, the harder it is for many people. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2025-08 5:28](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=328), [Stewiepfing 2025-08 5:28](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=328). Newest source 2025-08-10.
- **Store UI item text in data tables with fallbacks** (single-mention): Keep pet or item info in a data table with name, rarity, add-on and generic keys. The script checks for add-on keys and falls back to generic text when they are missing. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2025-08 2:18](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=138). Newest source 2025-08-10.
- **Use a custom currency icon** (single-mention): Make your own currency icon rather than default toolbox icons. Low-advice, self-reported indie claim that custom icons look better. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159). Newest source 2024-04-23.
- **Build gradients that loop cleanly** (single-mention): Always set a transparency sequence, and end the colour sequence with the starting colour so an animated gradient loops without clipping. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2025-08 12:00](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=720). Newest source 2025-08-10.
- **Drive VFX with clamped time and bezier curves** (single-mention): Compute clamped elapsed time divided by duration, shape motion with bezier curves, and disconnect and tween parts out when the clamp reaches 1. Example orb random offset range: -3 to 20. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2025-08 15:02](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=902), [Stewiepfing 2025-08 15:02](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=902). Newest source 2025-08-10.
- **Budget for Quest at 72 FPS** (one-creator-repeated): Target 72 FPS on Quest; frame drops cause discomfort. Mobile VR is sensitive to draw call count, so add detail where it matters, use lower detail elsewhere and keep object counts conservative. If Auto Quality Mode looks poor, optimise the game rather than accept degraded visuals. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep SurfaceGuis few and simple** (single-mention): Numerous or complex SurfaceGuis are costly for rendering and CPU. Keep them few and simple, especially in VR and on mobile. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep 3D accessories simple, static and single-point** (one-creator-repeated): Avatar 3D accessories should be simple, non-animated items attached to one body point. A simple 2D t-shirt can be made in an image editor without Studio; use Blender plus Studio for basic 3D accessories (low-advice). Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/creating), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/creating). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use texture generation for variants and rebrands** (one-creator-repeated): Generate texture variants of one asset (e.g. lightning vs fire pillars), then attach an attribute and script to change gameplay per variant. Generative retexturing lets small teams ship branded updates faster. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 11:49](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=709), [Roblox 2024-09 23:10](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1390). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Build a personal asset pack for style consistency** (single-mention): A personal asset pack keeps maps original and visually consistent instead of relying on toolbox assets. Roblox official guidance still uses Toolbox for prototyping; see disagreements. Sources: [DitchyDevelopments 2026-03 4:10](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=250), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Prototype menu and input flow on paper** (single-mention): Prototype UI/UX early, especially menu interaction and input controls. Paper is well suited to exploring layouts before building in Studio. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep gameplay videos simple and truthful** (single-mention): Record the core loop with minimal editing. Curated highlight reels are fine if every shown part exists in the game; in-game UI is acceptable. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-we-are-improving-home-this-year/4502571). Newest source 2026-03-09.
- **Size textures to surface area** (multiple-creators): Match texture size to the stud footprint: 256x256 for 5x5-stud faces, 512 for 10x10, 1024 for 20x20. 1024 costs 4x the graphics memory of 512, so downscale where possible, use atlases and trim sheets, and compress PNG/JPG before upload. SurfaceAppearance maps stay at 1024 or smaller. Newer official sizing replaces the blanket 512 advice. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications), [Crusherfire 2025-07 14:23](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=863). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Texture only the faces players see** (multiple-creators): Don't put Textures or decals on all six faces. A brick textured on every side was about 7x as costly to draw as a plain material brick (2019). Texture is checked on all faces and hurts mobile. Prefer SurfaceAppearance or built-in MaterialService materials, and reuse one tileable material rather than unique maps. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059), [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345), [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use Texture to tile, Decal to stretch once** (one-creator-repeated): Use Texture when the image must repeat across a surface; Decal stretches to fit one face. Scaling a part doesn't change a Texture's tile size. Set StudsPerTileU/V instead (examples 2 and 4). Make tiles seamless, with no single distinctive patch that reveals repetition. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/textures-decals), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/textures-decals), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/textures-decals), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Upload each mesh once and reuse it** (multiple-creators): Identical meshes uploaded separately get separate asset IDs and break batching. After one creator fixed duplicate tree, door and tire IDs, shipping-container draw calls fell 50%. Upload once, duplicate in Studio, and use packages so one edit updates every instance. Clone templates from ServerStorage instead of calling Instance.new. Sources: [Crusherfire 2025-07 21:31](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=1291), [Crusherfire 2025-07 23:34](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=1414), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/optimize-your-experience), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Reimport instead of re-uploading** (one-creator-repeated): Use Reimport (Alt+Shift+R) to update models from Blender; it keeps existing properties. Matching is by mesh path and name, so a rename creates a new MeshPart. Keep the tree structure, name PBR files with recognised suffixes (_Color, _Normal, _Rough, _Metal, _Emissive), and expect CollisionFidelity and RenderFidelity to be preserved. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Clean the Blender file before export** (one-creator-repeated): Set Unit System to None and Rotation to Degrees. Apply all transforms and modifiers, delete lights, cameras and mannequins, and purge unused materials and orphan data. Export FBX with Path Mode Copy and Embed Textures. In Studio set Scale Unit to Stud and enable Insert Using Scene Position. Avoid Scene Unit Scale 0.01. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/blender), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/from-existing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/exporting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/exporting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/from-existing). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use the Importer for rigged and layered meshes** (one-creator-repeated): Use Studio's Importer (Home > Import) for FBX/glTF with rigs, skinning, animation or accessories. It previews and error-checks before import and rejects meshes with errors. Asset Manager suits bulk images and audio. Roblox's official Blender plugin sends models straight to Studio. Don't rely on the validation tool alone. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/meshes), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/meshes), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/roblox-blender-plugin), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/importing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/validation-tool). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep meshes inside polycount budgets** (multiple-creators): Ordinary rocks, crates, tables and barrels: 12 to 2,000 triangles (author's target, versus maxing out at 10k). Docs hard limits: under 10,000 triangles per MeshPart and under 2,000 units per axis; under 20,000 per avatar mesh. Meshes give more detail per triangle than unions; convert unions copied several times to MeshParts via Export Selection. Only import MeshParts when primitives can't do the job. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419), [Roblox DevForum 2021-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/custom-characters), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/specifications), [Roblox DevForum 2019-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Strip physics and shadows from decor** (multiple-creators): Set CollisionFidelity to Box on non-interactable MeshParts and Unions, but not on parts used with CanTouch. Disable CanCollide, CanTouch and CanQuery on plants, windows and unreachable tools. Disable CastShadow on small or rarely seen parts. For debug hitboxes use Neon, Transparency 0.5, CanCollide false and CastShadow false. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve), [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333), [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333), [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Anchor static and spawned models** (multiple-creators): Anchor all parts in placeable models and spawned props; unanchored models fall over and placement systems rely on anchored objects. Unanchor only for the physics action, then re-anchor. Group multi-part items into one Model with all parts anchored so harvestable items stay in place. Sources: [B Ricey 2024-03 2:44](https://youtu.be/SVBmZ3yIMRo?t=164), [janisjanis01 2025-11 9:33](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=573), [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Limit glass, neon and transparency** (multiple-creators): Glass and Neon are the costliest materials; semi-transparent parts need more render work than opaque plastic. Use transparency 0 or 1 where possible, merge several glass panes into one part, and avoid many distinct transparency values. Glass refraction isn't supported on mobile. Neon emits no light by itself; add a light object. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059), [Roblox DevForum 2019-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059), [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/materials), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Enable Expanded Terrain before custom terrain materials** (one-creator-repeated): Expanded Terrain is off by default and needs the place reopened. Override the 22 built-in terrain materials; per-part Material Variants have no such limit. Create several shades as separate materials rather than overriding one base. Replace a material before deleting it, or terrain using it turns bright purple. Custom physical properties aren't yet respected by terrain slots. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Generate base terrain, then sculpt** (multiple-creators): Use the Terrain Generate tool with biome selection for a large base, then refine with Draw and Sculpt. Sculpt Strength (0.1 to 1) gives gentle edits; Draw has no strength control. Auto Material matches nearby terrain. For large non-cave terrain, heightmap import is faster and higher-resolution than mesh conversion. Use Gaea only if the generator is unavailable. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/terrain), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor), [Roblox DevForum 2019-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Light with local sources matched to the source** (one-creator-repeated): Use PointLight for bulbs and torches, SpotLight for streetlamps and flashlights (Angle 30 to 75 for a focused cone), and SurfaceLight for screens and signs. Brightness doesn't extend coverage; adjust Range (about 8 to 12 studs for a PointLight). Insert PointLights into an Attachment rather than a BasePart. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/light-sources), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/light-sources), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Choose LightingStyle deliberately** (one-creator-repeated): Realistic gives the most advanced shadows, and ShadowSoftness only takes effect under Realistic. Soft gives a flat retro Roblox look. Set ShadowSoftness 0 for dramatic edges such as a tunnel entrance. ClockTime and TimeOfDay don't advance on their own; set only one, since they are linked. Adjust ExposureCompensation for perceived brightness. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Split ambient lighting by purpose** (one-creator-repeated): Use OutdoorAmbient for outdoor time-of-day colour. Ambient tints the whole game, including indoor areas, so it can't mimic sunlight alone. Tint Ambient, OutdoorAmbient and ColorShift_Top together to pull the scene onto one palette. Keep EnvironmentSpecularScale near 1 so smooth and metal surfaces reflect the environment. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set PBR baselines and keep metalness honest** (one-creator-repeated): Start glossy plastic at roughness 0.1, metalness 0; matte plastic 0.4/0; cotton and denim 0.8 to 0.9/0; pure gold or silver 0.2 to 0.3/1.0. Use Metalness 0 for non-metals; only true metals go near 1.0. Silk and satin sit around 0.4 to 0.6 metalness. Calibrate against Substance Painter renders. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/material-reference), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/material-reference), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/material-reference), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/material-reference), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/material-reference). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Weld with WeldConstraint; Motor6D only for joints** (multiple-creators): Prefer WeldConstraint over Weld: it needs no C0/C1 offsets and welds relative position. Use Motor6D only for animation joints. Weld in Studio, not on live servers, to avoid displaced parts. Check welds after import; mis-welded parts can disappear in play. Set PrimaryPart on welded models so the pivot and bounding box are correct. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/constraints/rigid), [Roblox DevForum 2019-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253), [janisjanis01 2026-06 9:25](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=565), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/models). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build tools around a Handle and a grip** (multiple-creators): A tool must contain a part named Handle, or it drops on equip. Make Handle the primary part: transparent, CanCollide false, with every other part welded to it. Set GripPos, GripForward, GripRight and GripUp to fix the hold; the Tool Grip Editor helps before animating. Play tool animations on Equipped and stop on Unequipped. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-player-tools), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-player-tools), [Roblox DevForum 2019-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-a-toolobject-with-a-dummy-in-the-animation-editor/232317), [Roblox DevForum 2019-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-a-toolobject-with-a-dummy-in-the-animation-editor/232317), [Roblox DevForum 2022-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tools-the-easiest-and-best-way-for-melees/1654589). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep first-person viewmodels separate** (multiple-creators): Keep the viewmodel out of Workspace and separate from the world gun; merge only when equipped. Add invisible reference parts (Handle, Right, Left, Aim) with consistent front and up faces so offsets stay predictable. Expect to tune Motor6D C0 per weapon. Scale weapons to real player height; oversized guns broke one author's animations. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318), [Roblox DevForum 2018-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434), [Roblox DevForum 2018-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434), [Roblox DevForum 2020-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-part-2-2020/581791), [Roblox DevForum 2018-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434). Newest source 2020-05-18.
- **Make animations loop-safe and event-driven** (one-creator-repeated): Make the first and last pose identical, and keyframe only poses (the editor interpolates). Keep the rig inside the white grid so it doesn't clip. Save after every edit, because unsaved changes don't show in playtests. Use OnceAndHold for fall and land clips, distinct transition Priority values, and BeforeFinished to avoid pauses. Trigger footsteps with animation event markers. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor-state-machine). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Respect the animation validation budget** (one-creator-repeated): Author clips on the standard R15 Block rig so they play on every avatar. Clips over 10 seconds fail validation, and body parts moving more than 1.5 studs per frame fail too. Keep every clip except Jump looping. For one-shot emotes use Action priority with Looped false so they override idle and walk. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Override default animations through the Animate script** (multiple-creators): Replace default character animations by setting AnimationId on Animate-script children (for example walk.WalkAnim) from a server Script on CharacterAppearanceLoaded. Idle choices are random, weighted by Weight divided by the total (Weights 5 and 10 give 1/3 and 2/3). Put shared animation code in one reusable animator module. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/animation/play-character-animations), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using), [Duckable 2026-08 6:52](https://youtu.be/3XX3Y74hzq0?t=412). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Ease motion with cubic or chained exponential curves** (multiple-creators): Cubic is the only curve interpolation mode that allows tangent editing, so use it for eased in and out motion. Use Generate Curve Bounce or Elastic to mimic easings curves lack. Set Rotation Type to Quaternions before opening the Curve Editor, since quaternion-to-Euler conversion is irreversible. An indie trick: exponential-in to halfway, copy the frame, then exponential-out to finish. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor), [DudeGuy 2026-03 0:29](https://youtu.be/k3id70yCMRY?t=29). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build layered clothing from cage templates and wrap in order** (one-creator-repeated): Start from Roblox's cage and clothing templates and never edit cage UVs or add cage vertices. Keep the garment between inner and outer cage, spaced evenly, with break lines at joints. Only edit cage regions the item needs. To stack layers, weld each new layer to the previous (Part0 = previous), share the target's UV layout, and set WrapLayer.Order. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Ship rigid accessories as one MeshPart and fit with AFT** (one-creator-repeated): A rigid accessory is a single MeshPart nested in a single Model, with textures on a child SurfaceAppearance. Make it watertight with no backfaces, one material, one UV set within 0 to 1, and quads rather than N-gons. Use the Accessory Fitting Tool to auto-generate attachments; judge fit in its preview, since the clothing mannequin doesn't show rigid attachment accurately. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/watertight), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/specifications), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Prompt generators precisely and segment the output** (multiple-creators): Prompt with the object name plus material, age and distinctive details; omit background, camera, lighting and generic quality words. Use /generate_procedural_model when size will change and /generate_mesh for a fixed piece. Segment into named parts (max five per run) so materials and scripts can target them. Indie: Meshy plus Blender worked for scenery; image-to-3D then remesh to low poly. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/prompt-engineering), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/guide), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/guide), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/prompt-engineering). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build modular kits on a snap grid with trim sheets** (one-creator-repeated): Make modular pieces at least 5 studs wide and tall, snapping on 5-stud increments, with max width and height divisible by the kit's smallest dimension. Split large pieces into named children (UpperTrim, Wall). Give props logical pivots: back for wall-mounted, bottom for floor items. Use trim sheets, square and at most 1024, with no contextual stains that repeat obviously. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep background music and sound limits in check** (one-creator-repeated): Parent background music Sound objects to SoundService so they play at the same volume everywhere; parts-parented sounds are positional. Load audio only for the area being played, since audio can be a surprising client memory cost. Imports: one track, mp3, ogg, wav or flac, under 20 MB, under 7 minutes, at most 48 kHz. Use AudioPlayer for dynamic playback. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/play-background-music), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/assets). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Export UI icons white and copy Figma styling into UI objects** (multiple-creators): Export Figma icons as PNG (Studio needs PNG) and make them white so ImageColor3 can recolour them per state. Copy Figma gradients into UIGradient and corner radii into UICorner, matching the Figma W and H values. Avoid unnecessary gradients, many open frames and glitter effects on low-end devices. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916), [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916), [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333). Newest source 2023-09-09.
- **Use one grid increment per task and never switch mid-build** (multiple-creators): Pick a fixed snap grid: 0.2 or 0.25 studs for fine work, 0.5 to 1 for detail, 5 to 10 for big walls. Changing increments causes gaps, overlaps and skewed parts. Sizes like 52.082 are hard to edit later. Avoid going below about 0.1 studs. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919), [GnomeCode 2021-07 16:26](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=986), [GnomeCode 2021-07 19:52](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1192), [GnomeCode 2021-07 23:17](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1397), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Offset added detail instead of stacking coplanar faces** (multiple-creators): Two surfaces at the same position cause z-fighting flicker. Offset added detail by 0.04 studs on a 0.2 grid (0.05 on a 0.25 grid) rather than leaving gaps or overlapping identical planes. Close seams with Gapfill-style tools. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919), [GnomeCode 2021-07 17:49](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1069), [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919). Newest source 2021-07-06.
- **Treat studs as real units and check human scale early** (multiple-creators): One stud is roughly 28 cm. Studio does not convert DCC units, so model in studs. Place the Build Rig dummy (Plugins > Build Rig) and confirm doorways and windows are human-scale before building further. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture), [GnomeCode 2021-07 11:46](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=706). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build curves from mixed primitives and solid modelling** (multiple-creators): Combine wedges, corner wedges, cylinders, spheres and blocks to make curves, bevels and ramps without overlap. Use Union and Negate for complex Studio shapes, and import Blender meshes for organic detail. CSG needs watertight meshes: closed and non-self-intersecting. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/modeling/create-neon-signs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/next-steps), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/use-case-tutorial-overview), [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Break up long flat walls with trim and offset parts** (one-creator-repeated): Avoid single long flat surfaces. Add blocks, trim, columns and small offset parts; a few extra parts can roughly triple visible detail. Do not rely on one texture such as brick: add slab supports, contrasting courses and skirting boards. Sources: [GnomeCode 2023-03 11:01](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=661), [GnomeCode 2023-03 11:44](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=704). Newest source 2023-03-11.
- **Keep detail level and style uniform across all assets** (multiple-creators): A low-detail dagger beside a high-detail sword breaks immersion. Set the style and detail tier once in the art bible, then make every built or imported model match it. Mismatched assets forced one project into a full rebuild to a blocky style. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2023-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571), [JustDucksey 2025-04 1:23](https://youtu.be/BbNlDGBROdg?t=83), [MonarchDev 2026-10 2:14](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=134), [Roblox DevForum 2020-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156). Newest source 2026-10-03.
- **Limit the palette to 2-3 base colours plus shades; keep accents rare** (multiple-creators): Start from base, dark and light versions of 2-3 colours, made by slight darkening or lightening instead of clashing brights. Cartoon: drop one step below pure neon; realism or post-apocalyptic: desaturate further. Keep vibrant colours rare so flowers or mushrooms stand out. Use contrast to direct the eye. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258), [GnomeCode 2023-03 13:50](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=830), [GnomeCode 2023-03 15:59](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=959), [Roblox DevForum 2020-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156), [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866). Newest source 2023-03-11.
- **Give each zone its own mood and write concrete art direction** (multiple-creators): Vary palette and lighting per area so each zone reads differently (warm desert vs swampy zone). Write specific direction: time of day, outline style for characters, named base colours. Research the cultural meaning of colours in the region you emulate rather than copying online colour-meaning charts. Sources: [GnomeCode 2023-03 17:22](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=1042), [MonarchDev 2026-10 3:06](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=186), [Roblox DevForum 2020-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156). Newest source 2026-10-26.
- **Learn core Studio hotkeys and place parts precisely** (multiple-creators): Ctrl+D duplicates in place (copy lands in the original spot), Ctrl+G groups, Ctrl+1-5 switch tools, F focuses on selection, and holding Shift slows camera. Move on a single axis, keep Collisions on so parts stop against neighbours, and snap to a part's orientation. Ctrl+R rotates 90 degrees; Ctrl+L toggles local and world orientation. Sources: [GnomeCode 2021-07 1:06](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=66), [GnomeCode 2021-07 2:29](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=149), [GnomeCode 2021-07 3:50](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=230), [GnomeCode 2021-07 5:32](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=332), [GnomeCode 2021-07 6:12](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=372). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use proven building plugins for speed (verify they still work)** (multiple-creators): Stravant ResizeAlign and GapFill fix alignment and gaps; Model Reflect builds symmetry; Light Editor and Atmos manage lights and skyboxes; Brick Counter measures part counts before optimising. For dressing, Brushtool, TerrainPainter, MeshVox and the Brush tool scatter props with variation. Creators say most builders only got good after using plugins. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919), [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919), [Roblox DevForum 2020-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156), [Roblox DevForum 2021-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301), [Roblox DevForum 2020-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156). Newest source 2025-02-09.
- **Smooth terrain ridges and blend materials gradually** (one-creator-repeated): Fix Smooth-tool ridges by running Grow or Erode at minimum strength over the seam. Blend two terrain materials by growing the opposite material at low strength on each side of the boundary. Use the Paint plane lock for layered strata such as canyon bands. Use Snap to Grid and Ignore Water with the Add tool. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017), [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017), [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017), [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017). Newest source 2016-04-08.
- **Build terrain palettes with MaterialColors and variants; never delete in-use materials** (multiple-creators): Recolour default materials through Workspace.Terrain MaterialColors to get a unique palette. Use Terrain Paint to change a level's look (snow, water, lava) without reshaping. Create MaterialVariants from the closest base material and enable Set as Override. Deleting an in-use material turns affected terrain bright purple. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/design-the-world), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/design-the-world), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/assemble-an-asset-library), [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-unlocking-massive-terrain-with-up-to-64-custom-materials/4703282), [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-unlocking-massive-terrain-with-up-to-64-custom-materials/4703282). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Match sky, fog and water tint to genre and time of day** (multiple-creators): Cartoony sky for cartoony games, dark fog for horror. For mild background fog set FogEnd to 1000-2000 (default 100000). Choose orange or yellow sky tones for sunrise or sunset realism, and tint water yellow or orange rather than the default colour. A bad sky tint can turn a whole map blue. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013), [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013), [BloxxedDev 2025-01 3:03](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=183), [BloxxedDev 2025-01 3:34](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=214), [MonarchDev 2026-10 2:14](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=134). Newest source 2026-10-03.
- **Use exposure compensation only for crisp daytime scenes** (one-creator-repeated): ExposureCompensation near -3 with Lighting Brightness about 65 gives crisper daytime light; lower exposure needs higher brightness. It needs brighter spot and point lights, removes neon glare, makes Bloom nearly invisible, and works poorly at night. Treat the values as starting points and tune each game. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033), [Roblox DevForum 2021-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033), [Roblox DevForum 2021-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033), [Roblox DevForum 2021-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033), [Roblox DevForum 2021-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033). Newest source 2021-08-02.
- **Use Lighting and Camera post-processing for global looks** (multiple-creators): Add ColorCorrection to Lighting for a cinematic or black-and-white grade. Keep SunRays subtle, and lower their intensity after playtesting because the first setting blinded players. Put effects on Camera for per-player looks, such as blur while a menu is open. Post effects change the whole look without rebuilding assets. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033), [Roblox DevForum 2021-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033), [BloxxedDev 2025-01 4:56](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=296), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/post-processing-effects), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/lights-and-polish). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use one main light; set environment scales so PBR reflections show** (multiple-creators): Use one main light rather than many overlapping lights, which add shadow and overlap cost. Set Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale and EnvironmentSpecularScale to 1 so PBR materials, MaterialVariants and SurfaceAppearance show real reflections. Set final lighting early to see which surface details are actually visible. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/enhance-outdoor-environments), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set RenderFidelity and merge static meshes late** (multiple-creators): Use RenderFidelity Automatic or Performance for distant MeshParts; Automatic drops detail past 250 and 500 studs. With StreamingEnabled set Model LevelOfDetail to Automatic. Near the end of development, merge static models into single assets and prefer fewer, larger meshes over many small pieces. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Prefer built-in Materials and SmoothPlastic over custom textures** (multiple-creators): Built-in materials are already on the device, so they save memory and load time. Use SmoothPlastic for best performance on simple surfaces. Spend the custom-texture budget on hero surfaces the player actually sees. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design), [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/next-steps). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use cutout or near-opaque transparency for foliage and glass** (multiple-creators): Set MeshPart Transparency to 0 with Transparency alpha mode for hard-edged cutouts such as foliage or lace, so depth occlusion renders correctly. For semi-transparent detail such as dirty glass or soft feathers, use at least 0.02. Keep other partial transparency rare because it causes overdraw. See the disagreement note. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Supply all four PBR maps and use albedo, not baked diffuse** (one-creator-repeated): Always supply albedo, metalness, roughness and normal maps; a missing metalness map defaults to plastic and a missing roughness map to smooth.
