Use an albedo map with no baked lighting. Author colour maps as near-white grayscale so SurfaceAppearance.Color tints strongly and one map serves many variants. Normal maps must be OpenGL tangent-space. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use texture atlases only for batchable parts** (one-creator-repeated): An atlas streams its whole texture at the mip level of its highest-resolution member, so one high-res texture drags the atlas. Atlas only textures that appear together on batchable parts. Give non-batchable parts unique textures so mips load independently, and keep texel density near-uniform across UV-mapped meshes. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855), [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855), [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855), [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855). Newest source 2025-12-12.
- **Build reusable and greybox assets as Packages and organise them** (one-creator-repeated): Wrap a single object in a Model before converting to a package so children can be added or scaled later. Enable AutoUpdate on PackageLink so one edit propagates to every copy. Start with greybox placeholder packages and swap in polished models later; replacements keep position and orientation. Organise folders by data type and mirror greybox level names. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/assemble-an-asset-library). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Name assets exactly and delete greybox last** (one-creator-repeated): Scripts often reference assets by name, so keep names such as 'Coin' exact inside their folder. Group related parts into one Model (Ctrl+G) and name them. Delete greybox placeholders only after every polished replacement is placed, so you can compare progress against the layout. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/modeling/create-neon-signs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Publish assets early and plan for moderation delay** (multiple-creators): Imported assets are private by default and unapproved assets stay invisible to players until Roblox approves them; review is generally within a few hours. Use rbxassetid rather than rbxgameasset when an asset must work across games. Reuse uploaded assets from the Toolbox inventory instead of duplicating uploads. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Vet third-party models for scripts, size and necessity** (multiple-creators): Check imported maps and models for hidden scripts and oversized geometry before placing them; large imports caused lag and blocked paths. Prefer Roblox-endorsed (orange badge) free models. Disable Upload To Roblox in the Importer to avoid unwanted copies. Spamming many free models can lag low-end machines. Sources: [BloxxedDev 2025-08 3:17](https://youtu.be/od95x-afmBU?t=197), [GnomeCode 2021-07 28:13](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1693), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar-setup), [GnomeCode 2023-05 8:18](https://youtu.be/TJnV2ycnXZc?t=498), [BloxxedDev 2025-08 0:42](https://youtu.be/od95x-afmBU?t=42). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Buy, commission or model missing art; keep defaults for simple games** (multiple-creators): For a simple game, a few dollars of Toolbox or template models can beat custom art, and default Roblox animations can stay. When free packs lack needed items (plants, for example), budget time to model them. Commissioning artists per UGC item is an option when your own modelling is slow. Block building and low-poly are both valid; choose by willingness to learn Blender. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-07 9:26](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=566), [VuukStudios 2026-07 10:06](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=606), [Raul 2026-07 7:13](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=433), [Raul 2026-07 7:13](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=433), [Erthlord 2026-07 10:14](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=614). Newest source 2026-07-29.
- **Source audio from licensed libraries and filter by length and licence** (multiple-creators): Search the Toolbox by mood words, not exact track names, and filter to tracks over 1 minute to exclude SFX. Check licences (TLDRLegal summary) and credit creators even under CC0. Prefer the Creator Store's free audio or the web audio dashboard. Filter Freesound or SoundCloud by licence, and filter by creator to avoid unlabelled AI-generated tracks. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio), [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490), [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490), [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Drive footsteps from animation events and group sounds by material** (multiple-creators): Disable default footsteps and add custom ones synced to animation events named 'footstep' with parameter 'left' or 'right' on each foot-contact frame. Group similar materials under one sound to cut download size, and tune per-material volume (e.g. 0.3 instead of default 0.5). Parent Sounds to a part or attachment so volume scales with distance. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943), [GnomeCode 2022-12 3:36](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=216), [GnomeCode 2022-12 9:03](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=543), [GnomeCode 2022-12 9:03](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=543), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/in-game-sounds). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set animation priority and rig type before animating** (multiple-creators): Use Core priority for idle, Movement for run and Action for one-shot actions, and set R6 or R15 in game settings so the rig matches what you publish. To speed up an animation, tighten keyframe spacing rather than changing speed in code. Make the first and last keyframes identical so loops transition smoothly. Sources: [Suphi Kaner 2021-11 21:12](https://youtu.be/JyMxrcqEzu8?t=1272), [ArTheDev 2025-07 0:50](https://youtu.be/csxJTfPVW8U?t=50), [SimTek Game Development 2025-07 1:17:39](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=4659), [SimTek Game Development 2025-07 4:09](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=249). Newest source 2025-07-28.
- **Customise NPCs with BodyParts, Accessories and discriminators** (multiple-creators): Customise NPC looks by modifying BodyParts and adding Accessory objects, not the base rig. Use discriminators to restrict assets to certain NPC types and the 'all' discriminator to share one asset across types. Set an accessory or hair slot to -1 to skip it. Do not rename Motor6D joints on NPC rigs, because Animator matches them by name. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/justice-the-awesome-npc-system/1026927), [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/justice-the-awesome-npc-system/1026927), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/npc-kit), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/npc-kit), [SimTek Game Development 2025-07 2:03](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=123). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Name accessory Handle and HatAttachment exactly** (one-creator-repeated): Make the most central part of a multi-part accessory the 'Handle' (capital H); other names won't equip. Name the positioning attachment 'HatAttachment' so the item fits R6 and R15 identically. Use the same names in Blender or Studio before export. Sources: [Emancyphur 2021-04 0:20](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=20), [Emancyphur 2021-04 3:46](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=226), [Emancyphur 2021-04 6:52](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=412), [Emancyphur 2021-04 6:52](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=412). Newest source 2021-04-23.
- **Weld every accessory part to the Handle** (one-creator-repeated): Unwelded parts are dropped when the accessory is added to a character; only the Handle transfers. The Weld button only joins intersecting parts, so add WeldConstraints manually for the rest or use a welding script. Sources: [Emancyphur 2021-04 7:34](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=454), [Emancyphur 2021-04 8:15](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=495). Newest source 2021-04-23.
- **Convert legacy SpecialMesh accessories to MeshPart** (single-mention): Before the 2021 accessory/head change, recreate SpecialMesh-based items as MeshPart, copying the MeshId and TextureId, to stay compatible. This is legacy advice; new accessories should be built as MeshPart. Sources: [Emancyphur 2021-04 2:24](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=144). Newest source 2021-04-23.
- **Keep UGC meshes under 4,000 triangles with a buffer** (multiple-creators): Marketplace upload cap is 4,000 triangles. Target about 3,990 as a safe buffer. Check counts with the Statistics overlay in Studio or Blender, and reduce with flat-edge dissolve and merge-by-distance before exporting. Sources: [FrenkyRBLX 2025-05 0:00](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=0), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-08 24:29](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1469), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 7:31](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=451), [Xaiyne 2026-10 2:05](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=125), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-05 0:00](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=0). Newest source 2026-10-21.
- **Reduce triangles by dissolving flat edges and merging close verts** (one-creator-repeated): Alt-click an edge on a flat straight surface, then X > Dissolve Edges to cut triangles without changing shape. Select all, press M > Merge by Distance to remove near-duplicate verts. One creator's demo went from 19k tris and removed 66 verts in a single pass. Sources: [FrenkyRBLX 2025-05 1:33](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=93), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-05 8:37](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=517), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 4:09](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=249), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 2:20](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=140), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-05 0:25](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=25). Newest source 2025-12-24.
- **Delete faces players never see** (one-creator-repeated): Remove back faces hidden under the head, and parts poking inside other geometry, using X-ray and face select. One creator cut a hair mesh from 4,500 tris to under 4K this way. Use this before the Statistics check. Sources: [FrenkyRBLX 2025-05 4:45](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=285), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 5:51](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=351), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 5:51](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=351). Newest source 2025-12-24.
- **Avoid the Decimate modifier** (single-mention): Decimate badly damages topology. Use it only as a last resort when triangles must drop fast; prefer dissolve, merge-by-distance, and hidden-face deletion first. Sources: [FrenkyRBLX 2025-05 7:17](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=437). Newest source 2025-05-05.
- **Lower curve resolution, then convert hair to mesh** (one-creator-repeated): Build hair as curves, drop Curve Properties resolution from about 12 to 6, then convert to mesh and join strands. Merge vertices by centre to close gaps. This cut one hair model from about 10k to about 5k tris. Use G-G to slide edges for natural strands. Sources: [FrenkyRBLX 2025-08 18:08](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1088), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 0:57](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=57), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 1:38](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=98), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 4:49](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=289), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-08 17:28](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1048). Newest source 2025-12-24.
- **Mirror-modifier half-build workflow** (single-mention): Disable the Mirror modifier while modelling one half, then merge and apply it. This halves the modelling work for symmetric pieces. Sources: [FrenkyRBLX 2025-08 23:38](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1418). Newest source 2025-08-20.
- **Check normals and UV orientation before publishing** (single-mention): Turn on Face Orientation to spot inverted normals (red faces), then fix them with Shift+N recalculate. If the texture appears flipped on the mesh, rotate all UVs 90 degrees in the UV editor. Sources: [FrenkyRBLX 2025-08 29:13](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1753), [FrenkyRBLX 2025-08 30:50](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1850). Newest source 2025-08-20.
- **Export AI-generated models as 2K FBX and fit as an accessory** (one-creator-repeated): Extract the zip, import the FBX via File > Import, scale with 3, ungroup with Ctrl+U, rename the part Handle, place it in an Accessory, and fit in the Avatar Editor. Place it in the Face bounds (box turns blue) before Generate Mesh Part Accessory. Sources: [Xaiyne 2026-10 3:29](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=209), [Xaiyne 2026-10 3:52](https://youtu.be/wkfCP_09CI8?t=232), [Xaiyne 2026-10 3:42](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=222), [Xaiyne 2026-10 5:14](https://youtu.be/wkfCP_09CI8?t=314), [Xaiyne 2026-10 3:29](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=209). Newest source 2026-10-21.
- **Use an exported R6 block dummy as a fitting guide** (single-mention): In Studio, use Avatar > Rig Builder and export the R6 block avatar dummy as OBJ. Import it into Blender as a silhouette to check fit before modelling the accessory. Sources: [FrenkyRBLX 2025-08 0:13](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=13). Newest source 2025-08-20.
- **Split OBJ imports by group and bind items to bones** (one-creator-repeated): Enable 'split by group' when importing OBJ into Blender so body parts and accessories can be deleted separately. To attach an accessory to a rig bone, select the item, shift-select the bone in Pose Mode, then Ctrl+P > Bone. Sources: [Rool-Studio 2026-04 4:17](https://youtu.be/WI6adMt2aUY?t=257), [Rool-Studio 2026-04 4:36](https://youtu.be/WI6adMt2aUY?t=276). Newest source 2026-04-11.
- **Photobash clothing with soft clone stamping, then add noise** (one-creator-repeated): Clone stamp at roughly 30-39% brush opacity with 100 hardness so strokes blend instead of hard-copying. Clone stamping degrades quality, so add noise afterward. Start from high-resolution references and pull textured pieces, not flat shading. Sources: [#7Phantom 2025-09 4:17](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=257), [#7Phantom 2025-09 9:19](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=559), [#7Phantom 2025-09 3:00](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=180), [#7Phantom 2025-09 0:55](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=55), [#7Phantom 2025-09 4:17](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=257). Newest source 2025-09-07.
- **Match colour and lighting across photobashed pieces** (one-creator-repeated): Match colour on a flat-tone layer first (a little magenta via colour balance, then saturation) so pieces share one brightness. Shade across pieces so brightness doesn't jump. Overlay texture packs on overlay blend mode, testing first. Sources: [#7Phantom 2025-09 3:37](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=217), [#7Phantom 2025-09 9:19](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=559), [#7Phantom 2025-09 8:38](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=518). Newest source 2025-09-07.
- **Name moving parts uniquely and group animated parts** (single-mention): Name all parts uniquely, put animated parts in one folder, and set CanCollide to false on them so they don't interfere with other physics. Sources: [Roox4 2024-06 0:15](https://youtu.be/DPpAsjUr278?t=15). Newest source 2024-06-19.
- **Build a script-free conveyor with AssemblyLinearVelocity** (single-mention): Set the belt part's AssemblyLinearVelocity in Properties to the axis speed. The creator's example is X = -50 (50 studs/s, sign reversed for direction). Sources: [BubuTheDev 2024-09 0:41](https://youtu.be/xVUqKk3OnnU?t=41), [BubuTheDev 2024-09 0:41](https://youtu.be/xVUqKk3OnnU?t=41). Newest source 2024-09-07.
- **Replace starter lighting and skybox before publishing** (single-mention): Replace default Roblox lighting and skybox with custom atmosphere, such as a moonlight setup, to stop the game looking like a starter place. Sources: [mats-dept2 2026-10 1:11](https://youtu.be/evyIBvylLdc?t=71). Newest source 2026-10-10.
- **Add a Skybox before Atmosphere** (single-mention): Atmosphere won't work unless a Skybox is in Lighting first. Add the Skybox, then the Atmosphere. Sources: [Emancyphur 2022-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=0). Newest source 2022-07-25.
- **Use SpotLights when shadows matter** (single-mention): Under the Future lighting technology, SurfaceLight shadows were not supported at the time of the 2020 advice. Use SpotLights if shadows matter. Verify current support before relying on this. Sources: [Emancyphur 2020-08 3:06](https://youtu.be/ScdTM3ju-yk?t=186). Newest source 2020-08-02.
- **Re-tune lighting for every time of day** (single-mention): Future lighting changes how areas look. Re-check each time of day, especially interiors that relied on few light sources. Sources: [Emancyphur 2020-08 4:59](https://youtu.be/ScdTM3ju-yk?t=299). Newest source 2020-08-02.
- **Prioritise a core loop over high-poly art** (multiple-creators): Stylised, simple visuals with a strong gameplay loop outperform graphically heavy games with weak loops. Asset packs and free models with cartoon lighting give a cohesive look quickly. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-08 3:26](https://youtu.be/6CefFrCFqcI?t=206), [RoyalDev 2026-08 3:26](https://youtu.be/6CefFrCFqcI?t=206), [LovJak 2026-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/0RZeCn3-XKg?t=0). Newest source 2026-08-25.
- **Build UI declaratively from the client** (one-creator-repeated): Mount declarative UI to Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui from a client script so the root ScreenGui is created on the client. Use the Codify plugin to export a Studio-built frame to Vide code in one click, then iterate in code. Sources: [Chase Higgins 2026-07 5:39](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=339), [Chase Higgins 2026-07 6:23](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=383). Newest source 2026-07-08.
- **Keep simple parts for simulation and let upres add visual detail** (single-mention): On primitive-based builds, keep the underlying geometry simple so physics and performance stay cheap. Use 3D upsampling to add visual detail on top, and let the developer choose the final style. Keep the simulation geometry separate from what players see. Sources: [Roblox 2026-02 20:07](https://youtu.be/8Djgx0jEyfs?t=1207). Newest source 2026-02-25.
- **Build repeatable objects as parametric procedural models** (one-creator-repeated): For trees, fences, buildings and Ferris wheels, make a procedural model driven by attributes (e.g. fence post spacing, window count, fence length) instead of baking thousands of unique meshes and textures. Resize and iterate by changing attributes, and keep instances editable after generation. Sources: [Roblox 2026-04 22:25](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=1345), [Roblox 2026-10 4:25](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=265), [Roblox 2026-10 18:43](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1123). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Add custom parameters after generation without breaking placement** (single-mention): You can add custom parameters such as door colour to a procedural model after it is generated. Existing instances keep their placement when the code is updated, so you can extend a model's controls without re-placing it in the level. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 23:31](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1411). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Restyle mixed assets at once with retexture and one reference image** (single-mention): Use Roblox's retexture tool with a single reference image to restyle several mixed assets together. The whole scene then shares one visual style. Use this to unify assets from different sources before final art pass. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 26:51](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1611). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Design interactive props as geometry plus behaviour** (single-mention): Roblox frames these as 4D assets: geometry and behaviour should be generated together, so a door opens and a helicopter flies. Plan interactive props with their behaviour defined alongside the shape, rather than treating them as static meshes to add scripts to later. Sources: [Roblox 2026-02 4:11](https://youtu.be/8Djgx0jEyfs?t=251). Newest source 2026-02-25.
- **Expect auto-generated collision with precision controls** (single-mention): Roblox's improved collision geometry (announced for late 2026) auto-generates better collision with a real-time debug overlay and performance-precision controls. Until it ships, keep collision simple by hand. When it arrives, check the debug overlay and tune the precision-versus-performance setting. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880). Newest source 2026-09-11.
- **Keep accessory Handle size identical across items** (single-mention): Use the same Handle scale (about X≈1.45) for every accessory so they scale the same way when fitted to characters. Mismatched Handle sizes make fitted items look inconsistent. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 5:23](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=323). Newest source 2026-08-01.
- **Fix camera zoom jolts by tuning the loop's start values** (one-creator-repeated): If a camera zoom ends with a jolt, print the start distance and height where the loop ends, then add a small offset to those start values. If the jolt cannot be removed, flip the camera angle at the end of the move so the change reads as intentional rather than as a bug. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 11:48](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=708), [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 11:48](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=708). Newest source 2026-09-19.
- **Tint one mesh for colour variants instead of cloning meshes** (single-mention): Do not build a separate mesh per variant. Tint one mesh by lerping its colour between gradient colours, or cycle hue over time for a rainbow variant. This keeps memory and asset count down. Sources: [Duckable 2026-10 16:33](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=993). Newest source 2026-10-18.
- **Use a plain white-background reference for AI 3D generation** (single-mention): When generating a 3D model with AI, feed in a reference image with a plain white background. The creator reports cleaner results, especially for hats. Sources: [Xaiyne 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=40). Newest source 2026-10-30.
- **Test accessory fit with the Accessory tool before publishing** (single-mention): Use Avatar > Accessory to test fit. Scale the item until the fit box turns blue, then raise it so it does not clip the head. Sources: [Xaiyne 2026-10 4:09](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=249). Newest source 2026-10-30.
- **Filter Toolbox by the Roblox creator account for vetted models** (single-mention): Filtering Toolbox to models uploaded by the Roblox account gives more legitimate starting assets. In 2021 Emancyphur sourced 3 of 4 team tools this way. Treat this as a vetting step, not a licence to ship unvetted free models (see the caution rule). Sources: [Emancyphur 2021-01 3:19](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=199), [Emancyphur 2021-01 3:19](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=199). Newest source 2021-01-28.
- **Keep unvetted free Toolbox models out of shipped games** (multiple-creators): Newer advice (2024-11) says some players react badly to free Toolbox assets, so use them for learning only. Possible Panda's caution is more recent than the 2021 filter tip. Where free models are used, vet them for scripts and size first. Sources: [Possible Panda 2024-11 0:41](https://youtu.be/OxGa3kVcfMY?t=41), [Emancyphur 2021-01 3:19](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=199). Newest source 2024-11-27.
- **Use free-model packs for background scenery only** (single-mention): BloxxedDev suggests free-model asset packs (for example trees) save build time when art skill is limited. Restrict them to background scenery and replace them with owned art as the game matures. Sources: [BloxxedDev 2025-03 1:31](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=91). Newest source 2025-03-09.
- **Show an orientation indicator to place oval and template parts correctly** (multiple-creators): Use a plugin or Show Orientation Indicator to see a part's front face. Set a template part's pivot at its back so rotations and branching happen around the joint. Sources: [BloxianCode 2022-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/iegUPV-wMng?t=0), [B Ricey 2023-10 8:16](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=496). Newest source 2023-10-20.
- **Recompute PivotOffset whenever code resizes a part** (single-mention): Changing Size does not move PivotOffset. After scaling a part in code, set the new pivot from half the scaled length so rotations stay on the joint. Verify in current Studio, since this advice dates from 2023-10. Sources: [B Ricey 2023-10 11:12](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=672). Newest source 2023-10-20.
- **Preview placement with a translucent cloned block** (one-creator-repeated): Clone the block for placement previews and set Transparency to 0.5, with CanCollide and CanQuery false. This avoids duplicating assets manually and keeps the preview from blocking raycasts or physics. Sources: [B Ricey 2024-01 2:47](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=167), [B Ricey 2024-01 7:00](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=420). Newest source 2024-01-06.
- **Offset placed parts along the raycast Normal by half the block size** (one-creator-repeated): Place a part at the raycast hit position plus the surface Normal times half the block size. With 3x3-stud blocks that is 1.5 studs, which stops parts sinking into ground or walls. Sources: [B Ricey 2024-01 8:21](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=501), [B Ricey 2024-01 0:43](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=43). Newest source 2024-01-06.
- **Randomise repeated tween durations for cheap variety** (single-mention): For rotating obstacles, run TweenService with RepeatCount -1 and a random duration per tween (2 to 5 seconds in ByteBlox's demo). Place obstacles on every 2nd or 3rd platform. This adds visual variation with no extra assets. Sources: [ByteBlox 2025-01 21:16](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=1276), [ByteBlox 2025-01 21:16](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=1276), [ByteBlox 2025-01 16:58](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=1018). Newest source 2025-01-24.
- **Keep interactive highlights from hiding objects and avoid dark rooms** (single-mention): Highlights should not obscure the object they mark. Dark rooms can make players lose their way, so keep lighting readable where players must navigate or find interactables. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-02 8:20](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=500). Newest source 2025-02-22.
- **Invest in a strong lobby as a statement of quality** (one-creator-repeated): Players judge a game's quality from its lobby, so GnomeCode treats it as a statement of intent. Budget real time for it: about 1 hour to model a lobby in GnomeCode's Blender workflow. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-02 12:50](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=770), [GnomeCode 2025-02 12:50](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=770), [Roox4 2023-11 3:14](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=194). Newest source 2025-02-22.
- **Deliver story and guidance through in-world means, not static cards** (multiple-creators): Use in-game documents and notes for story and guidance, and show scenario events with in-game characters. Static text and image cards feel like a missing system and are less immersive than in-world delivery. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-02 12:50](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=770), [Cumin 2026-06 6:40](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=400). Newest source 2026-06-24.
- **Name the pickup child and weld multi-part items into one model** (one-creator-repeated): A pickable model must contain a child named 'pickup' or it will not be detected. Place new item models in an Objects folder in ReplicatedStorage by duplicating an existing item. Weld all parts to the model directly and keep them unanchored, or the item falls apart. Sources: [PineappleDev 2025-03 1:03](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=63), [PineappleDev 2025-03 1:03](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=63), [PineappleDev 2025-03 1:03](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=63). Newest source 2025-03-21.
- **Drive particle and smoke effects from vehicle state** (single-mention): Enable smoke or particle effects only while the vehicle is moving, so visual feedback matches the actual state rather than playing constantly. Sources: [JustDucksey 2025-06 1:24](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=84). Newest source 2025-06-14.
- **Add particles and camera shake to explosions for impact** (single-mention): A few minutes of VFX polish, adding particles and camera shake to explosions, turned a dull prototype into a satisfying one. Roox4 spent about 15 minutes getting explosions working. Sources: [Roox4 2023-11 1:11](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=71), [Roox4 2023-11 1:11](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=71). Newest source 2023-11-26.
- **Bind custom camera code above Roblox's default camera** (single-mention): Use RunService:BindToRenderStep with priority Camera.Value + 1 so your camera code overrides the default. This is the newest guidance (2026-03), so confirm it still behaves as stated in current Studio. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2026-03 8:29](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=509). Newest source 2026-03-29.
- **Animate text and UI strokes with Easy Visuals classes** (single-mention): Use Easy Visuals gradient and stroke classes rather than hand-scripting tweens. Set offset speed, rotation and transparency sequence explicitly. Confirm the plugin's current availability and licence before relying on it. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2026-04 0:15](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=15). Newest source 2026-04-12.
- **Low budget is acceptable only with clear identity and feedback** (one-creator-repeated): A low-budget aesthetic needs strong visual identity, animation, hit sounds and camera angles, and needs to be compensated by gameplay. Basic visual design alone will not carry a low-budget game. Sources: [Cumin 2026-06 5:20](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=320), [Cumin 2026-06 5:20](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=320). Newest source 2026-06-24.
- **Upgrade visuals cheaply with lighting, blur and grass before adding content** (single-mention): BloxxedDev improves perceived quality with lighting, blur and a more realistic grass material before adding more content. This is a low-cost upgrade path that comes before new assets. Sources: [BloxxedDev 2025-03 6:53](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=413). Newest source 2025-03-09.
- **Apply consistent materials with a texture plugin and match path colours to terrain** (single-mention): Add Easy Texture applies one material to many parts quickly. Match path colours to the surrounding terrain so paths read as part of the environment rather than as separate pieces. Sources: [BloxxedDev 2025-04 2:45](https://youtu.be/RVZBjAUzf7o?t=165). Newest source 2025-04-10.
- **Grant reward tools by cloning from ReplicatedStorage** (single-mention): Keep the reward tool master in ReplicatedStorage and clone it into the player's Backpack or Character when granting it. This keeps one source copy and avoids duplicate tool instances. Sources: [TheDevKing 2019-12 12:59](https://youtu.be/QC2bDZsFiz4?t=779). Newest source 2019-12-01.
- **Pose renders with the move and rotate tools only** (single-mention): When posing the model and camera for thumbnails or promo renders, use only the move and rotate tools. This keeps the composition simple and avoids accidental scale or stretch changes. Sources: [BloxianCode 2020-11 2:34](https://youtu.be/vP_d8TWny3g?t=154). Newest source 2020-11-17.
- **Build ad creatives from modular pre-rendered blocks** (single-mention): Use a standard intro and outro plus interchangeable content blocks. Duration can then be changed quickly by reordering or removing blocks instead of re-rendering the whole ad. Low-advice source; treat as a workflow idea. Sources: [GameMakers 2021-11 3:47](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=227). Newest source 2021-11-10.
- **Test transparency and VFX on one object before applying them broadly** (one-creator-repeated): Apply transparency or a new effect to a single object first and check how it reads in-game. Drop it if it looks wrong, as one creator did with a slime ground effect. For jelly-themed pets, use slight transparency plus face textures added in Studio so they read as jelly. Low-advice source. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-01 11:36](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=696), [janisjanis01 2025-01 12:00](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=720). Newest source 2025-01-05.
- **Run responsive chopping and interaction animations on the client** (single-mention): Handle chopping and similar interaction animations on the client so the player sees immediate feedback and the interaction does not lag behind server latency. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-11 6:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=376). Newest source 2025-11-23.
- **Colour simple props in Studio and model in Blender only when needed** (single-mention): For simple props, apply colour directly in Studio because it is faster than round-tripping through Blender. Reserve Blender for shapes that Studio cannot produce well. UGC accessory modelling and export still follows the Blender rules above. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-12 4:00](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=240). Newest source 2025-12-13.
- **Hand-build basic maps from studded Toolbox parts** (single-mention): Build a basic map by recolouring, scaling and cloning studded parts from the Toolbox, placed by hand. Vet any Toolbox source before shipping (see existing Toolbox rules). Self-reported as a fast option for simple maps. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-03 10:54](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=654). Newest source 2026-03-18.
- **Set PrimaryPart on pet and vehicle models** (one-creator-repeated): Set each multi-part model's PrimaryPart correctly before positioning or scripting it. One creator spent about 10 minutes positioning a pet without it. After configuring, verify eggs open and pets are assigned correctly. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-06 8:18](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=498), [janisjanis01 2024-11 10:29](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=629). Newest source 2024-11-17.
- **Check imported model facing direction** (single-mention): Verify model orientation on import. A fire dragon faced the wrong way and was fixed by rotating 90 degrees. Correct the facing before wiring behaviour. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-04 5:10](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=310). Newest source 2024-04-14.
- **Save terrain Brush settings** (single-mention): Record or save Brush tool settings used for terrain. Losing them made terrain look worse and forced a rebuild from old videos. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-06 3:31](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=211). Newest source 2024-06-30.
- **Scatter filler with the Brush tool and folder new assets** (single-mention): Group new map assets into folders, then scatter trees and rocks with the Brush tool to fill large areas quickly rather than placing each one by hand. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-04 0:50](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=50). Newest source 2024-04-14.
- **Save and commit progress often** (single-mention): A power loss forced one creator to retexture finished pets. Save frequently and keep versioned copies of finished art so losses are recoverable. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-12 11:36](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=696). Newest source 2024-12-15.
- **Plan the asset list and build in themed batches** (single-mention): List all pets or props before starting, then build in batches grouped by area theme (candy cane, town, train station). This keeps style and palette consistent within each area. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-12 15:16](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=916). Newest source 2024-12-15.
- **Texture each model individually** (single-mention): For obby or themed assets, model and texture each piece in Blender before import. Baking textures together was ineffective, so the creator retextured each model. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-11 5:33](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=333). Newest source 2024-11-17.
- **Simulate glow with Neon material and colour variants** (one-creator-repeated): Roblox has no emissive textures. Apply the Neon material with colour variants to fake glowing neon. Keep window or glass parts white in Blender and add colour and glow in Studio. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-03 3:46](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=226), [janisjanis01 2024-12 7:00](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=420). Newest source 2025-03-23.
- **Model interactive consumables in 3D** (single-mention): Use 3D-modelled items instead of flat icons for click-to-use interactions. The creator found plain icons boring and modelled snacks in Blender. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-03 0:33](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=33). Newest source 2025-03-23.
- **Use Substance Painter for detailed decals** (single-mention): Texture stylised props in Blender. Use Substance Painter for logo and text decals on detailed items. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-03 1:14](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=74). Newest source 2025-03-23.
- **Use a limited palette for liquids** (single-mention): Liquid models read poorly when bland. Use a limited colour palette to make them stand out. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-03 5:00](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=300). Newest source 2025-03-23.
- **Budget extra time for complex meshes** (single-mention): Mesh problems can make models hard to build. Plan extra modelling time for complex shapes rather than assuming a quick pass. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-03 5:43](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=343). Newest source 2025-03-23.
- **Use Canva to recreate logos for an icon portfolio** (single-mention): Recreating existing game logos in Canva is a low-cost portfolio piece for icon design work. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-07 4:02](https://youtu.be/yYp23GK9RI4?t=242). Newest source 2024-07-11.
- **Use Reflectance for a quick chrome look on simple parts** (single-mention): Setting a part's Reflectance to 1 gives a mirror-like chrome finish without a custom material. Anchor the part and give it a unique name so it is easy to find in the Explorer. Prefer PBR metalness for new work (see existing PBR rule). Sources: [GnomeCode 2020-04 0:22](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=22). Newest source 2020-04-02.
- **Have scripts set up the scene automatically** (single-mention): Manually moving models into game folders before each run added setup time. Let a server script build or place the scene at startup, so the project needs no manual pre-run steps and stays consistent across test sessions. Sources: [GnomeCode 2020-06 33:28](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2008). Newest source 2020-06-26.

## Checklist
- [ ] Graybox and playtest the layout before final art
- [ ] Do a week of research and a mood board
- [ ] Write an art bible and UI style sheet (palette, fonts, padding about 15px, corner radius 6px)
- [ ] Pick palette, lighting and texture style early
- [ ] Check silhouettes by filling models flat black
- [ ] Build UI mobile-first with Scale, aspect ratio and size constraints
- [ ] Test every UI in the device emulator
- [ ] Keep touch targets large and actions in thumb zones, clear of Roblox restricted areas
- [ ] Set TextScaled with min/max size limits
- [ ] Set Selectable on buttons and test controller navigation
- [ ] Scale interactive UI about 1.5x for TV
- [ ] Use string tables and localization format strings
- [ ] Check contrast with a WCAG calculator and avoid colour-only signals
- [ ] Offer UI scaling and a contrast slider
- [ ] Use Atmosphere and post-processing lighting
- [ ] Use simple collision fidelity and avoid partial transparency
- [ ] Keep textures at 1024 or below and watch texture memory
- [ ] Disable custom LODs when enabling mesh streaming
- [ ] Keep UGC items under 4,000 triangles and unwrap UVs early
- [ ] Name layered clothing cages exactly and export selected objects only
- [ ] Reuse and reskin existing assets
- [ ] Organise asset packs with a ReadMe and a preview experience
- [ ] Verify AI-generated UI and builds visually
- [ ] View UI in grayscale before shipping; fix any element that disappears.
- [ ] Add an icon, symbol or outline wherever colour carries meaning.
- [ ] Caption or visually signal every gameplay-relevant sound cue.
- [ ] Add separate sliders for music, effects and speech using sound groups.
- [ ] Read GuiService.PreferredTextSize, PreferredTransparency and ReducedMotionEnabled.
- [ ] Replace TextScaled with AutomaticSize and TextWrapped where text should grow.
- [ ] Check text contrast; small text needs higher contrast than large or bold text.
- [ ] Test font sizes on real mobile devices at the smallest expected screen size.
- [ ] Build TV UI at lower resolution first, then verify it inside TV-safe areas.
- [ ] Use ScrollingFrame on large displays to reduce clutter.
- [ ] Add console shortcuts for essential actions.
- [ ] Show button icons matching the player's device (InputActionLabel or GetImageForKeyCode).
- [ ] Play selection and navigation sounds for controller menus.
- [ ] Limit haptics to explosions, collisions, attention events and notifications.
- [ ] Apply UIAspectRatioConstraint to framed images and shapes.
- [ ] Collapse secondary buttons into a menu on small screens.
- [ ] Compact-format numbers from roughly 1,000 upward (e.g. 5,000 to 5k).
- [ ] Keep item and pet text in data tables with generic fallbacks.
- [ ] Check animated gradients loop: transparency sequence set, colour sequence ends on its start colour.
- [ ] For Quest, target 72 FPS, watch draw calls and keep SurfaceGui count low.
- [ ] Keep 3D accessories simple, non-animated, single-attach-point.
- [ ] Prototype menu flow and input controls on paper before Studio.
- [ ] Keep gameplay trailers real: every shown part must exist in the game.
- [ ] Keep audio uploads under 10 seconds if your account is verified.
- [ ] Before export, check triangle count and bounds: 12 to 2,000 triangles for ordinary props; under 10,000 per MeshPart and under 2,000 units per axis.
- [ ] Upload each mesh once and duplicate it in Studio; don't reimport identical meshes as new IDs.
- [ ] Size textures to the stud area (256 for 5x5, 512 for 10x10, 1024 for 20x20) and compress images before upload.
- [ ] Put Textures and decals only on faces players see; prefer SurfaceAppearance or built-in materials.
- [ ] In Blender: Unit System None, apply transforms and modifiers, delete lights, cameras and mannequins, and embed textures in the FBX.
- [ ] Verify root orientation (green up) before rigging; check welds after import.
- [ ] Anchor every placed or spawned model; re-anchor after any physics action.
- [ ] Disable CastShadow on small or rarely seen parts; set CollisionFidelity to Box on non-interactable parts.
- [ ] Convert unions copied several times to MeshParts via Export Selection.
- [ ] Enable Expanded Terrain before custom terrain materials; replace a terrain material before deleting it.
- [ ] Generate the base terrain, then refine with Sculpt at Strength 0.1 to 1.
- [ ] Choose the light type by source (PointLight, SpotLight, SurfaceLight) and tune Range rather than Brightness for coverage.
- [ ] Set LightingStyle on purpose and set only one of ClockTime or TimeOfDay.
- [ ] Tint Ambient, OutdoorAmbient and ColorShift_Top together to hold one palette.
- [ ] Start PBR materials from the baselines and keep Metalness at 0 except for true metals.
- [ ] Save the animation after every edit and confirm in a playtest.
- [ ] Make first and last poses identical for loops; keep clips at 10 seconds or less and body motion within 1.5 studs per frame.
- [ ] Start clothing and caging from Roblox templates; never edit cage UVs or add cage vertices.
- [ ] Test accessories on Classic, Rthro Normal and Rthro Slender bodies, with layered combinations and animations.
- [ ] Parent background music to SoundService and check audio against 20 MB, 7 minutes and 48 kHz.
- [ ] Export Figma icons as white PNG and copy gradients and corners into UIGradient and UICorner.
- [ ] For thumbnails, place the model on a green background in Studio to cut it out later, and remove the name tag (DisplayName none).
- [ ] Build variants by reskinning one base model (for example three pet variants or many crate skins).
- [ ] Check any contracted or rushed model before shipping; one crop model shipped with exclusive seeds not loading.
- [ ] Polish the lobby and first-seen areas first, and give each stage or zone a distinct colour.
- [ ] Pick one grid increment per task (0.2/0.25 fine, 0.5-1 detail, 5-10 big walls) and keep it until the piece is finished.
- [ ] Offset added detail by 0.04 studs on a 0.2 grid or 0.05 on a 0.25 grid; never leave coplanar overlaps.
- [ ] Place the Build Rig dummy and confirm doorways and windows are human-scale before building further.
- [ ] Make Ctrl+D (duplicate in place) and Ctrl+G (group) default habits; use single-axis moves with Collisions on.
- [ ] Before a new area, write the palette as 2-3 base colours plus dark and light variants.
- [ ] For item previews, put the unit model in a WorldModel inside the ViewportFrame template; clone a basic character with no accessories.
- [ ] Export each UI element as a separate PNG, colour symbols white for tinting, and clean dark edges with Pixelfix.
- [ ] Set ImageLabel ResampleMode to Pixelated for pixel art shown at low canvas resolution.
- [ ] Use Image asset IDs for icons and mission or reward icons, not Decal IDs.
- [ ] Put particle emitters, beams and lights inside an Attachment per part; set EmitCount, EmitDelay and EmitDuration attributes to script all effects uniformly.
- [ ] Test every auto-skinned accessory on several body types and animations; trim outer cages to the needed region (e.g. head only for beards).
- [ ] Try Edit-Time Procedural Models (File > Beta Features): write only into targetContainer, then clear Generator to bake before hand edits.
- [ ] Back up assets before generating textures, do not edit an asset while generation runs, and use Quality mode (4K PBR) when detail matters.
- [ ] Split large rotations into steps of about 72 degrees and add buffer keyframes past start and end markers.
- [ ] Pose from the torso outward: upper arms and thighs first, then hands and feet; set a mid-animation pose at 0.5.
- [ ] Author clips in Blender, Maya or Moon Animator, but upload and assemble bundles in Studio; all 7 clips in a pack must be original.
- [ ] Tune viewmodel offset as a CFrame attribute live in playtest; set LocalTransparencyModifier to 1 on the real tool's parts.
- [ ] Weld the assembled tool and animate the whole assembly rather than tweening individually welded pieces.
- [ ] Resize a Handle by editing its Size property rather than with the resize tool.
- [ ] Save a configured instance (e.g. anchored MeshPart) via Edit > Save as Default [Instance] so new inserts match.
- [ ] Position parts relative to a rotated part with part.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(...)) instead of adding to Position.
- [ ] Store large texture data as raw RGBA byte buffers in a ModuleScript; do not open raw bitmap modules directly in Studio, which can crash it.
- [ ] For growing crops, pivot at the base (Model > Edit Pivot), name stages STAGE_1, STAGE_2, and drive colour from one growth parameter.
- [ ] Stay inside Marketplace limits: body parts under 10,000 tris, rigid accessory a single mesh at most 4k tris, textures at most 2048x2048.
- [ ] Set ExposureCompensation to about -3 and Brightness to about 65 for daytime; then playtest sun rays and reduce their intensity.
- [ ] Name the accessory's central part exactly 'Handle' and its positioning attachment 'HatAttachment'.
- [ ] Weld all accessory parts together before publishing; unwelded parts are dropped on equip.
- [ ] Check triangle count with the Statistics overlay; keep UGC meshes at or under about 3,990 tris.
- [ ] Dissolve flat-surface edges and run Merge by Distance (vertex select, A, M) to trim triangles.
- [ ] Delete hidden back faces and parts buried inside other geometry before exporting.
- [ ] Lower hair curve resolution (about 12 to 6) before converting to mesh; merge by centre to close gaps.
- [ ] Turn on Face Orientation to find red inverted faces; fix with Shift+N.
- [ ] Rotate UVs 90 degrees if a texture appears flipped on the mesh.
- [ ] Export textures at 2K in FBX format.
- [ ] Verify the fit in the Avatar Editor: the Face-bounds box should turn blue before Generate Mesh Part Accessory.
- [ ] Make the marketplace thumbnail with the ThumbMaker plugin from the Handle, then right-click Handle > Save and Export to Roblox.
- [ ] Export the R6 block dummy as OBJ for a fitting guide.
- [ ] Import OBJ with 'split by group' in Blender to delete body parts individually.
- [ ] Bind accessories to bones: select item, shift-select bone in Pose Mode, Ctrl+P > Bone.
- [ ] Loop emotes: keyframe start pose and mid pose, copy the first keyframe to the end, use curved interpolation; a typical emote is about 6 s.
- [ ] Set a conveyor's AssemblyLinearVelocity on its axis instead of scripting it.
- [ ] Replace default Lighting and skybox first; add Skybox before Atmosphere.
- [ ] Re-check lighting at every time of day after any lighting-tech change.
- [ ] Use SpotLights, not SurfaceLights, where shadows matter (verify current support).
- [ ] Use a floor texture strip for a checkered finish line instead of a flag.
- [ ] Vet free Toolbox models by reviews and scripts before inserting them.
- [ ] Use high-quality references for photobashing; low-resolution sources give poor results.
- [ ] Add noise after clone stamping to restore texture.
- [ ] Match colour on a flat-tone layer before blending photobashed pieces.
- [ ] Model low-poly and push detail into baked normals and textures to keep draw calls low.
- [ ] For primitive-based builds, keep simulation parts simple and use upres only for visual detail.
- [ ] For repeated trees, fences, or buildings, build one procedural model with attributes and vary parameters.
- [ ] After generating a procedural model, add custom parameters (e.g. colour) and confirm existing placed instances keep their position after a code update.
- [ ] When mixed assets look inconsistent, run retexture with one reference image across all of them.
- [ ] Design interactive props with their behaviour defined, not just their shape.
- [ ] Keep hand-made collision simple until the improved collision geometry feature ships; then check the debug overlay.
- [ ] Before the 4K PBR High quality texture mode is enabled, budget memory per surface area as usual and plan for 4K maps only on hero surfaces.
- [ ] Verify availability of procedural, retexture, upres, and collision features in Studio before advising a team to depend on them.
- [ ] Set the accessory Handle to the same size (about X≈1.45) on every item in a set.
- [ ] When a camera zoom ends in a jolt, print start distance and height at the loop end and apply a small offset.
- [ ] If a camera jolt persists, flip the camera angle at the end of the move so it reads as intentional.
- [ ] Create colour variants by tinting one mesh (colour lerp or hue cycle), not by cloning meshes.
- [ ] Use a plain white-background reference image when generating a 3D model with AI.
- [ ] Test accessory fit in Avatar > Accessory: scale until the box turns blue, then raise to clear the head.
- [ ] Confirm AI-generated textures are 2K or smaller and the mesh is under 4,000 triangles before upload.
- [ ] Enable an orientation indicator before building oval or template parts, and set template pivots at the joint.
- [ ] When code resizes a part, recompute PivotOffset from half the new scaled length.
- [ ] Build placement previews as cloned parts at Transparency 0.5 with CanCollide and CanQuery false.
- [ ] Offset placed parts along the raycast Normal by half the block size.
- [ ] Give repeated obstacle tweens random durations and place them on a set platform interval.
- [ ] Put ScreenGui objects in StarterGui; they are cloned into PlayerGui for each player on join.
- [ ] Check that interactive highlights do not hide the object and that rooms are not too dark to navigate.
- [ ] Build the lobby early and budget roughly 1 hour for it.
- [ ] Deliver story through in-game documents and in-world characters instead of static cards.
- [ ] Name the pickup child 'pickup'; put item models in ReplicatedStorage Objects and weld them unanchored.
- [ ] Tie smoke and particle effects to vehicle state.
- [ ] Add particles and camera shake to explosions; budget about 15 minutes.
- [ ] Bind custom camera code at Camera.Value + 1 via RenderStep.
- [ ] Filter Toolbox by the Roblox account when sourcing starter models, then vet them.
- [ ] Confirm the game still has gameplay feedback before accepting a low-budget art direction.
- [ ] Keep the reward tool master in ReplicatedStorage and clone it into Backpack or Character on grant (T18410).
- [ ] Use only move and rotate tools when posing renders and cameras for thumbnails or promos (T18425).
- [ ] Split ad creatives into an intro, an outro and swappable content blocks so length can be changed fast (T18429, low-advice).
- [ ] Apply new transparency or VFX to one object first and check it in-game before using it broadly (T18561, low-advice).
- [ ] For jelly-themed pets, use slight transparency and add face textures in Studio (T18562, low-advice).
- [ ] Run chopping and interaction animations on the client for responsive feedback (T18591).
- [ ] Colour simple props directly in Studio; open Blender only for shapes Studio cannot handle (T18615).
- [ ] For a basic map, recolour, scale and clone vetted studded Toolbox parts by hand (T18643).
- [ ] Set PrimaryPart on each pet and vehicle model, then verify eggs/pets or vehicles configure and move correctly in Studio (T18772, T18832).
- [ ] Check each imported model's facing direction and rotate it (e.g. 90 degrees) if it is wrong (T18759).
- [ ] Save Brush tool settings when building terrain so you can reproduce the look without rebuilding (T18768).
- [ ] Group new map assets into folders, then scatter trees and rocks with the Brush tool to fill areas quickly (T18755).
- [ ] Check mesh triangle counts before import and reduce polygons first if a mesh is very heavy (T18841, N8089).
- [ ] Save and commit progress frequently; a power loss forced retexturing of finished pets (T18853).
- [ ] Plan the full asset list ahead and build in themed batches (e.g. candy cane, town, train station) (T18857).
- [ ] Texture each model separately rather than baking textures together across assets (T18830).
- [ ] Keep window and glass parts white in Blender and apply colour and glow in Studio (T18861).
- [ ] Simulate glowing neon with the Neon material and colour variants, since Roblox lacks emissive textures (T18863).
- [ ] Model click-to-use consumables and key interactables in 3D instead of using plain icons (T18862).
- [ ] Use Substance Painter for logo and text decals on detailed items, and texture stylised props in Blender (T18864).
- [ ] Use a limited colour palette for liquid models so they read clearly (T18865).
- [ ] Allow extra modelling time for complex mesh shapes that are harder to build (T18866).
- [ ] Group new assets into folders before placing them in the map (T18755).
- [ ] Use Canva to recreate existing game logos as an icon-design portfolio piece (T18780).
