Not an official limit. | [janisjanis01 2024-12 6:28](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=388), [janisjanis01 2024-12 4:26](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=266) |

## Where creators agree
- RobloxOfficial and RobloxCreators agree: graybox first, design mobile-first, keep style consistent.
- RobloxOfficial, RobloxCreators and Roblox agree: use constraints and Scale for UI, not fixed pixels.
- RobloxCreators and TheDankDeveloper agree: reuse and free assets speed up work.
- RobloxCreators and Daiorg agree: simple art is clearer and runs on more devices.
- RobloxOfficial and Roblox agree: legible cohesive art beats photorealism.
- RobloxOfficial and Creators on the Blox agree: clean topology matters.
- Colour must not carry meaning alone; add symbols, icons or outlines (T3479, T3481, T4818).
- Pair sound cues with visual indicators for hearing-impaired players (T3482, T4819).
- Offer accessibility settings and respect player preferences (T3497, T3498, T4820, T4821, T4822).
- Use relative sizes and constraints so UI scales across devices (T3647, T4485, T4695).
- Reuse one mesh or asset across instances; separate uploads of identical meshes cost memory and batching (T6254, T8273, T6912, T6977, T9945).
- Put textures only on visible faces, and prefer SurfaceAppearance or materials over Textures (T5048, T6079, T5488).
- Glass, Neon and transparency cost more than opaque plastic (T5049, T5830, N3341).
- Disable collision and shadows on decorative or unreachable parts (T5828, T5829, T6076, T8268).
- Anchor placed and spawned models (T5930, T6356, T5831).
- Use WeldConstraint for fixed joins and Motor6D only for animation joints (T5106, T8131, T5135).
- Check imports for scale, orientation and welds before relying on them (T5100, T6573, T8464, T8495).
- Clone pre-built templates instead of creating each object from scratch (T5839, T5321, T8195).
- Build on a fixed grid and avoid coplanar overlapping surfaces; detail should be offset, not stacked (T10440, T11045, T11046, T14334).
- Settle on one style and one detail tier early and apply it to every asset; mismatched models break immersion (T13878, T11643, T12687, T10628).
- Keep part counts and draw calls low and merge static models late (T10831, T13372, T15639).
- Prefer built-in Materials and SmoothPlastic for performance (T13370, T15255, T10833).
- Use a limited palette with a dominant hue and rare vivid accents (T10625, T10770, T11667).
- Simple games can use bought or borrowed models and default animations; modelling gaps need budgeted time (T13637, T13638, T13644).
- Lighting, sky and fog set the whole mood; choose them to match genre and time of day (T10625, T10842, T12529, T13953).
- UGC accessories and hair must stay under the 4,000-triangle upload cap, with a buffer (about 3,990).
- Stylised, simple visuals and strong loops beat heavy high-poly art; asset packs and cartoon lighting are a fast route to a cohesive look.
- Use Studio's Statistics overlay to check triangle counts before publishing.
- Visual polish gives a large return for little time: Roox4 (explosions) and BloxxedDev (lighting, blur, grass) both say cheap feedback or visual upgrades matter before adding content.
- Story and guidance should be delivered in-world rather than through plain prompts or static cards: GnomeCode and Cumin.
- A strong, readable first impression (lobby or basic visual identity) signals quality: GnomeCode and Cumin.
- Use placeholders and cheap assets early, then replace them: BloxxedDev (placeholder primitives and free packs) agrees with the existing graybox rule.

## Where creators disagree
- **How much to rely on AI for building and UI**
  - TheDankDeveloper (T3258, T3259, T3262) uses AI for UI layouts and Blender MCP.
  - TheDankDeveloper (T3261, Q652) also says AI is weak at builds and UI, so always give a reference.
  - RobloxOfficial (T2217) suggests AI for simple repeated props, with artists on hero assets.
- **Free assets versus custom art**
  - CallMeDogey (T1657) says free assets can carry a map (self-reported).
  - RobloxOfficial (T1582, T1583) and RobloxCreators (T411) push concept art and a distinct style.
- **Colour accessibility approach**
  - RobloxCreators (T1028, Q182) prefers contrast sliders over colourblind filters.
  - RobloxCreators (T997, T999) also suggests outlines and brightness differences.
- **Font choice for accessibility**
  - RobloxCreators (T994, low-advice) says sans-serif is easier and Comic Sans is dyslexia-friendly; this is weakly supported.
- **Using Toolbox and marketplace assets**
  - Roblox Creator Docs (T4261, T4412, 2026-10): use Marketplace music, models and plugins, and Toolbox plus solid modeling, to prototype and source quickly.
  - DitchyDevelopments (T3774, 2026-03, self-reported): build a personal asset pack instead of relying on the Toolbox so maps stay original and consistent.
- **Dyslexia-friendly fonts**
  - Roblox Creators (N1543, 2023-05): Comic Sans and OpenDyslexic cited as dyslexia-friendly; serifs harder for some (panelist opinion, not formal research).
  - Roblox Creator Docs (T4699, 2026-10, more recent): avoid decorative or thin fonts and test at the smallest mobile size. Comic Sans is arguably decorative, so the newer advice governs.
- **Texture size for general objects**
  - Crusherfire (T6249) and the DevForum 2025 post (T6078) recommend 512x512 as the default.
  - Official docs (T9204, N3697) size textures to surface area, so a 20x20-stud object gets 1024. Official advice is newer and should win for sizing; 512 remains a mobile-friendly default for small objects.
- **Decal size**
  - DevForum 2025 (T6078) says 512 and keep under 500 for mobile.
  - DudeGuy (T6427) says custom decals must be 800x800 with a transparent background for the My Movie upload workflow.
  - Both can be right in different pipelines; check which pipeline the asset is for.
- **Unions versus MeshParts**
  - Author in 2019 (T5051) says Unions cost medium, to use when short on time but not habitually.
  - Author in 2025 (T6075) says convert Unions to MeshParts via Export Selection since Roblox optimises MeshParts better.
  - RoBuilder (T6226) uses Unions for flat floor pieces to hide Z-fighting. Use MeshParts for repeated or complex geometry; Unions for quick flat floors.
- **Using AI for creative and 3D work**
  - janisjanis01 (T6157, Q977) says AI-generated design ideas were low quality, so hand-designing unique cosmetics worked better.
  - janisjanis01 (T6493) uses Meshy.ai plus Blender for scenery and RoDev (T6186) uses an image-to-3D then remesh pipeline.
  - Reconciled: AI is weak for cosmetic concept ideation but workable for generic 3D scenery, with hand design for unique items.
- **Animation tooling**
  - Moon Animator is recommended for keyframing and camera tracks in 2019 and 2021 (T5033, T5458).
  - Official docs now point to the built-in Animation Editor and Animation Graph (T6983, T7614, T9487).
  - Prefer the built-in editor; Moon Animator is optional for camera tracks.
- **Terrain generation tool**
  - Gaea's free 1k export is recommended in 2019 (T5202).
  - Official docs describe a built-in Generate tool with biome selection (T7253, T7418).
  - Try the built-in tool first; Gaea or heightmaps remain options for large or specialised terrain.
- **When to finalise lighting**
  - MonarchDev (T13951): work in tiers, with overall map design first, then models, then lighting and polish last.
  - Roblox Creator Docs (T14198): set final scene lighting early to see which surface details are actually visible and skip the rest. Newer and official; prefer for visibility decisions, and do rough lighting early and polish late.
- **Using Toolbox models for speed**
  - VuukStudios (T13637): grab models, UI templates and tools from Toolbox or template sites for a few dollars.
  - Roblox Creator Docs (T14675): build with parts and terrain rather than the Toolbox, since Toolbox assets may introduce unexpected issues; T14690 suggests sampling trusted Marketplace objects instead. Official guidance wins on safety; vet any borrowed model.
- **Transparency for water and glass**
  - BloxxedDev (T12528): set water transparency high and tint it to match the sky.
  - DevForum indie (T13369): keep transparency at only 0 or 1, since partial transparency causes GPU overdraw.
  - Roblox Creator Docs (T14597): use at least 0.02 for semi-transparent detail so alpha adds visible detail; use 0 for hard cutouts (T14596). Official nuance: reserve partial transparency for places where it reads, keep it rare.
- **Icon asset type**
  - ByteBlox (T12229, 2024): upload a transparent PNG as a Decal on create.roblox.com and use its asset ID.
  - Roblox Creator Docs (T14586, 2026-10): reward icons must be Roblox image asset IDs, not decal IDs. Official and newer; prefer image IDs.
- **Animation priority (context, not a hard conflict)**
  - Suphi Kaner (T11154): Idle at Core priority, Run at Movement priority.
  - ArTheDev (T12854): set animation priority to Action before animating.
  - Use the priority that matches the clip's role: Core idle, Movement locomotion, Action one-shot actions.
- **Trust in Toolbox free models**
  - Emancyphur (2014): high-quality badge models are hand-picked by Roblox and reliable (T15650).
  - Xaiyne (2026): use a free dummy with no scripts and check reviews first to avoid malicious code (T16240).
- **Whether free Toolbox or free-model assets belong in a shipped game**
  - Possible Panda (2024-11): avoid free Toolbox assets in a real game; some players react badly; fine for learning only (T18144).
  - BloxxedDev (2025-03): free-model asset packs are fine for background scenery to save build time (T18215).
  - Emancyphur (2021-01): filter Toolbox by the Roblox account for legitimate models; 3 of 4 team tools came from there (T17997, N7737).
  - Existing knowledge rule 'Free and bought assets are fine' conflicts with Possible Panda's newer caution; prefer the 2024-11 advice for shipped games and keep free models to scenery and vetted use.

## Date context (what may be outdated)
- 2018 performance advice (textures about 5 MB, wedges not instanced, texture transcoding upcoming) is dated; 2024 gives 2.8 MB per set. Prefer newer figures.
- 2018 advice to use Roblox materials over SurfaceAppearance conflicts with later PBR and SurfaceAppearance emphasis (2023-2025). Prefer the newer advice, balanced by memory cost.
- 2025-08 says use the Accessory Fitting Tool instead of the old plugin Create Accessory flow.
- 2023 says UGC creation was limited to selected creators; 2026 mentions 1.7M in-experience UGC items, so access has widened.
- 2026 updates: draw distance min 500 studs, mesh and texture streaming (opt-in, skinned meshes unsupported), adaptive animations, animation graphs, native branch and merge planned early next year.
- Makeup UGC was planned for early next year (stated 2025).
- Indie revenue claims such as CallMeDogey's are unverified.
- 2026-10 Roblox Creator Docs supersede 2023-05 Roblox Creators accessibility guidance where they overlap; no outright contradictions were found.
- TextScaled: older tutorials use it; 2026-10 guidance says avoid it where text should grow.
- Controller prompts: with the Input Action System, use InputActionLabel; older projects use GetImageForKeyCode/GetStringForKeyCode.
- AI texture generator and retexturing advice dates from 2024-09; tool features may have changed by June 2026 and should be re-checked.
- Quest 72 FPS and Auto Quality Mode guidance is 2026-10; VR tooling may update.
- Stewiepfing items (2024-2025) are self-reported indie practice; verify against current engine APIs before relying on them.
- Ditchy and Stewiepfing claims are self-reported; official Roblox items (T-prefix 2023-2026) are platform ground truth.
- Official Roblox Creator Docs items are all stamped 2026-10 and are the most current ground truth. Where DevForum or indie advice from 2015 to 2022 conflicts with them, prefer the official guidance.
- Retired or superseded workflows in older items: Constraint Editor plugin (T5135), Tool Grip Editor (T5646), Moon Animator (T5033), Rojo workflow (T5224), Gaea free tier (T5202), Shadowmap Lighting Technology (T5151, T5152).
- Lighting moved to LightingStyle (Realistic or Soft) (T7682). 2019 Shadowmap and ColorShift tips still work for colour and time-of-day but the style setting now controls shadows.
- Blender 5.2 and later has an auto-save setting for modified images (T7005, N3002). Blender 3.0 is the baseline for official avatar tutorials (N3337, T9877). Avoid Scene Unit Scale 0.01 (T8493).
- Beta features: Face Capture and Live Animation Creator must be enabled under File > Beta Features and may change (T7575, T7578).
- Expanded Terrain is early access (T8603); custom physical properties on terrain are not yet respected (T8608).
- Audio import quota moved from a daily to a 30-day limit (N3633, N3634).
- Generative tools (/generate_mesh, /generate_procedural_model, Avatar Auto Setup, SLIM) are recent features that may change (T8030, T8001, T9789).
- Roblox Creator Docs entries are stamped 2026-10 and are platform ground truth; where they conflict with older indie tips (icon Decal IDs T12229 vs T14586, Toolbox use T13637 vs T14675), prefer the official guidance.
- 2016-2020 DevForum tips (Build v4, F3X, Photoshop 1920x1080 HUD design, older terrain tools) describe older tools; verify plugins and menu paths still exist before following them.
- 2021 lighting values (Exposure -3, Brightness 65, FogEnd 1000-2000) are starting points from older Lighting behaviour; re-tune in current Studio.
- 2023 Toolbox audio filter reports said the settings filters were broken; use the web audio dashboard or Creator Store and check current behaviour.
- 2024 skybox guidance uses Blender 4.1 and Standard View Transform; newer Blender versions may change menu locations.
- As of 2026-10: Edit-Time Procedural Models is a Studio beta (April 2026), and glTF export is beta (T15526); both may change.
- Terrain Early Access and an upcoming terrain scattering system were announced around 2026-09 (T13800, T13801); the claim that scattering uses far fewer resources than instanced meshes is unverified and the release timing is unknown.
- Asset moderation is now a gating step (T14111); older guidance that assets were usable immediately no longer holds.
- 2014 Toolbox high-quality badge advice (T15650) predates current vetting practice; prefer the 2026 advice to check reviews and scripts.
- 2020 Future-lighting notes (T15790, T15791) say SurfaceLight shadows are unsupported; verify current support before relying on this.
- 2021 accessory/head change (T15872, T15874): pre-2021 SpecialMesh accessories need conversion to MeshPart; HatAttachment and Handle naming still apply.
- 2022 Skybox-before-Atmosphere rule (T15951) is still a valid setup order.
- Latest UGC guidance (2025-05 to 2026-10): 4,000 triangle cap, 3,990 buffer, 2K FBX textures; newest pipeline is AI-generated models (Tripo, 2026-10).
- Codify/Vide declarative UI workflow (2026-07) is new; check plugin and library versions before using.
- Items added are dated 2026-02 to 2026-10. Procedural models and retexture (2026-10) are recent; verify they are generally available in Studio as of 2026-11-01.
- Improved collision geometry (T17671) is described for late 2026, so treat it as upcoming and confirm release.
- High quality texture mode (N7577) was announced for end of October 2026; confirm whether it shipped before advising on 4K textures.
- Diffusion step counts and latent compression values (N7465, N7466) are from 2026-02 and describe model internals that may change with new releases.
- Q1777 (4D assets) is a forward-looking statement about generated behaviour; treat it as direction, not a shipped workflow.
- New tips are dated Aug to Oct 2026; the current date is 2026-11-01. Xaiyne's 2K texture and under-4,000-triangle limits are indie-reported and should be checked against current Roblox upload rules before relying on them.
- Camera jolt fixes (SimTek, Sep 2026) are newer than the existing knowledge file, which had no camera guidance.
- Toolbox guidance is mixed by date: Emancyphur's 2021 filter tip is older; Possible Panda's 2024-11 caution is newer. Prefer the newer caution for shipped games.
- PivotOffset advice (B Ricey, 2023-10) and orientation-indicator advice (2023) predate current Studio; verify in the present Studio version.
- Camera BindToRenderStep priority advice (Stewiepfing, 2026-03) and Easy Visuals advice (2026-04) are the newest items; confirm plugin availability and behaviour now (today is 2026-11).
- Meshy and AI-generation advice (BloxxedDev, 2025-03) is tied to a tool version and pricing that may have changed.
- Self-reported timings (Roox4, 2023-11) are for one creator's workflow and skill level; treat them as rough benchmarks.
- New items span 2019-12 to 2026-03. The 2019-2021 items (T18410, T18425, T18429) are old and may reflect older Studio and ad workflows; check current tooling before relying on them.
- The janisjanis01 items (2025-01 to 2026-08) are the most recent. Where they overlap with older Blender or UGC guidance, the older UGC accessory pipeline still applies; the newer advice on colouring simple props in Studio applies to decor, not UGC accessories (T18615).
- Items T18707 (2026-08) was skipped as a repeat of the existing rule to prioritise a core loop over high-poly art.
- Sources date from 2024-04 to 2025-03 (indie, self-reported). Roblox may have changed since, so re-verify before relying on them.
- The Neon-as-glow workaround (T18863, 2025-03) and the lack of emissive textures should be re-checked against current Roblox material support.
- Triangle thresholds (N8089) are indie observations, not official limits; check current Studio behaviour.
- 2020 items (T18978, T18984, T18985, T18998) are older advice from indie creators. Where they touch on chrome finishes, prefer the newer PBR and metalness guidance already in the file over Reflectance-only tricks.
- T18984 and T18985 (MeshPart over Part-with-mesh, Decal per face vs Texture) are consistent with existing rules and were not added as new rules; the newer guidance on converting legacy SpecialMesh and Texture vs Decal still applies.

## Instructions for an agent using this file
- If starting a new map, then graybox and playtest first, and only then add art.
- If building UI, then use Scale, UIAspectRatioConstraint, size and text constraints, layouts, and test in the device emulator.
- If text uses TextScaled, then set UITextSizeConstraint min/max and consider longer translations.
- If targeting console or TV, then scale interactive UI about 1.5x, set Selectable, and use a grid layout.
- If an asset is a hat or layered clothing, then keep it under 4,000 triangles, unwrap UVs early, name cages exactly, export selected objects only, and keep textures at 1024 or below.
- If memory is a concern, then lower texture counts and sizes, avoid atlases and heavy UI textures, and unload audio; memory beats draw calls.
- If enabling mesh streaming, then disable your own LODs.
- If an older tip conflicts with a newer one (2018 vs 2024+), then use the newer one and flag it.
- If colour carries meaning, then add symbols, outlines or brightness differences, and check WCAG contrast.
- If using AI for art or UI, then provide a visual reference and verify the result in-game; label indie income claims unverified.
- If colour distinguishes a state or team, add a symbol or outline and check the UI in grayscale.
- If a sound cue carries gameplay information, add a caption or on-screen indicator for it.
- If UI text uses TextScaled or fixed sizes, switch to AutomaticSize and TextWrapped, and honour GuiService.PreferredTextSize.
- If the project targets TV or console, build UI at lower resolution first, keep key UI in TV-safe areas, and use ScrollingFrame for dense content.
- If the project targets Quest or mobile VR, check the 72 FPS target and draw calls first, then reduce SurfaceGui count and detail elsewhere.
- If displaying large numbers, compact-format them (e.g. 1k) rather than showing raw digits.
- If supporting controllers, add shortcuts, device-matched icons and selection sounds.
- If the project uses the Input Action System, prefer InputActionLabel for prompts.
- When official 2026-10 guidance conflicts with 2023 guidance, follow the 2026-10 guidance and say so.
- Treat indie claims as self-reported; cite them as low-advice when they are the only source.
- If a user's object is decorative or unreachable, disable CanCollide, CanTouch and CanQuery, and CastShadow if it is small (T5828, T5829).
- If a mesh will be placed more than once, upload it once and clone or package it rather than re-importing (T6254, T8273, T6912).
- If a texture covers a face, use SurfaceAppearance or a material; if it must repeat, use Texture with StudsPerTileU/V (T6079, T8074, T8075).
- If sizing a texture, use the stud area: 256, 512 or 1024; cap SurfaceAppearance at 1024 (T9204, T7951).
- If the user asks for a tool or weapon, build a transparent Handle, weld everything to it and set GripPos (T9097, T9098, T5045).
- If an accessory is requested, make it a single MeshPart in one Model and run the Accessory Fitting Tool before testing on multiple bodies (T9400, T8682, T9074).
- If an asset is reimported, keep mesh names and tree structure stable, since reimport matches by path (T6992, T6993).
- If generating assets with AI, prompt with the object plus material, age and details, and omit camera and lighting terms (T8241).
- If lighting is set up, choose LightingStyle first, then tune ShadowSoftness (Realistic only), ClockTime or TimeOfDay (one only) and the ambient colours together (T7682, T7684, T9871).
- If indie-only advice conflicts with official Creator Docs, prefer the official item and say so explicitly.
- If a request involves grid-based building, keep one increment per task and offset detail by 0.04 (0.2 grid) or 0.05 (0.25 grid) rather than leaving gaps or overlaps.
- If a request involves borrowed or Toolbox models, scan for scripts and oversized geometry first, prefer Roblox-endorsed items, and cite the official caution about Toolbox assets.
- If a request involves icons or reward images, use Image asset IDs, not Decal IDs (official, 2026-10).
- If a request involves terrain materials, use MaterialColors and MaterialVariants; never delete an in-use material; blend boundaries with low-strength growth.
- If a request involves daytime lighting, offer ExposureCompensation around -3 with Brightness around 65, warn it works poorly at night, and set environment scales to 1 for PBR reflections.
- If a request involves performance, default to Automatic RenderFidelity on distant meshes, SmoothPlastic or built-in materials, and check against the triangle and 2048 texture budgets.
- If a request involves AI-generated textures or meshes, back up first, do not edit while generation runs, and treat output as a draft to check against Roblox polygon requirements.
- If a request involves animation, set Core, Movement or Action priority and the R6/R15 rig type first, then keep the first and last keyframes identical for loops.
- If a request involves audio, verify the licence, credit the creator, filter for tracks over 1 minute when looking for music, and use positional Sounds on parts or attachments.
- When indie and official sources disagree, follow the official guidance and say which source and date you used.
- If a UGC accessory or hair mesh exceeds 4,000 tris, first dissolve flat edges, merge by distance, and delete hidden faces; only then consider Decimate, as a last resort.
- If hair is curve-based, lower curve resolution before converting to mesh so the triangle count drops.
- If an accessory loses parts in game, check that all parts are welded and that the central part is named Handle.
- If a texture looks flipped or shading looks inverted, check Face Orientation and rotate UVs before re-exporting.
- If a scene looks like a starter place, replace Lighting and skybox first; add Skybox before Atmosphere.
- If shadows are needed for a light, use SpotLights and verify current SurfaceLight support.
- If a free model is requested, check reviews and scripts before inserting it.
- If an AI-generated model is requested, export as 2K FBX, check the tri count, then fit via Avatar Editor Face bounds.
- If a user needs many similar objects (trees, fences, rows of buildings), propose a parametric procedural model with attributes rather than unique meshes.
- If a user asks to restyle or unify mixed assets, suggest retexture with one reference image.
- When planning with an AI assistant or pulling Creator Store assets, first ask the user to choose blocky or photoreal style, since the assistant asks for it during planning (see existing style-early rule).
- For primitive-based builds that need simulation, keep parts simple and suggest upres for visual detail only.
- For interactive props, ask about behaviour as well as shape before recommending geometry.
- Before recommending the late-2026 collision or 4K texture features, confirm they are available; otherwise give the current workflow.
- Treat OFFICIAL items as platform ground truth and indie figures as self-reported; say which applies when quoting numbers.
- If a camera zoom or move ends in a jolt, first print the start distance and height at the loop end and add a small offset. If the jolt remains, flip the camera angle at the end of the move so it looks intentional.
- If you need colour variants of an item, tint a single mesh with colour lerp or hue cycling instead of creating separate meshes.
- When generating an accessory with AI, use a reference image on a plain white background.
- Before publishing an accessory, verify fit in Avatar > Accessory: scale until the box turns blue, then raise it to clear the head.
- Keep the Handle size consistent across accessories (about X≈1.45) so fitted items scale identically.
- If a user asks how to place or preview a building part, use a translucent cloned preview with CanCollide and CanQuery false, and offset by the raycast Normal times half the block size.
- If code resizes a part and rotation looks wrong, recompute PivotOffset from half the new scaled length.
- If a user asks about free models, separate learning use from shipped use: recommend vetting or replacing free Toolbox models before release, and allow free packs only for background scenery.
- If a user asks about story or tutorials, recommend in-world documents or characters before static text or image cards.
- If a user asks about explosions or impact feedback, add particles and camera shake, and keep the effects tied to the actual game state.
- If a user asks about camera overrides, bind at Camera.Value + 1 with RunService:BindToRenderStep and verify against current Studio.
- If a user asks about low-budget art, state that it is only acceptable with a clear visual identity and strong gameplay feedback.
- Mark indie figures and timings as self-reported; treat [low-advice] items as anecdotes rather than standards.
- If a task involves a simple prop or decor piece, colour it in Studio first and only switch to Blender when the shape requires it (T18615). UGC accessories still follow the Blender export rules.
- If a user wants to test a new transparency or VFX effect, apply it to one object first and verify the look before applying it broadly (T18561, T18562).
- If a user asks for a quick map or prototype, suggest hand-building from vetted studded Toolbox parts and note the 1-2 hour estimate is self-reported (T18643).
- If a user is granting a tool as a reward, clone it from ReplicatedStorage rather than moving the master copy (T18410).
- Treat all items here as indie, self-reported sources. Mark the low-advice items when giving advice based on them.
- If a pet, vehicle or other multi-part model will be configured or moved by script, set its PrimaryPart first, then verify it in Studio before positioning (T18772, T18832).
- If an imported model faces the wrong direction, rotate it (the example was 90 degrees) before wiring up behaviour (T18759).
- If a mesh is very heavy (the indie example was over 100,000 triangles), reduce its polygons before import; do not expect Studio to handle it (N8089, T18841).
- If you need a glowing look, use the Neon material with colour variants instead of looking for an emissive texture, since Roblox has no emissive textures as of the 2025-03 source (T18863).
- If a model's texture bake across several assets looks poor, texture each model individually rather than baking shared sheets (T18830).
- If a task is complex (curved or intricate shapes), add modelling time to the estimate rather than assuming it will be quick (T18866).
- If production is tight and a common asset is needed, buying a cheap pack is a valid time-saver (T18774, N8061).
- If an item is interacted with directly (click to use, consume), prefer a modelled 3D object over a flat icon (T18862).
- If a theme or area has many assets, plan the full list first and build in themed batches (T18857).
- If you have finished work that would be expensive to redo (textures, pets), save and commit progress often to avoid losses (T18853).
- If a user asks for a chrome or mirror finish, first check whether PBR metalness and roughness can do it; use Reflectance = 1 only as a quick fallback on simple parts.
- If a project's setup requires manual model placement before each run, consider a server script that builds the scene at startup.
- Always anchor static decor and give parts identifiable names when adding them to the Explorer.

## Not covered here (research elsewhere)
- No coverage of the Creator Store or UGC moderation and approval rules.
- No detailed audio pipeline (licensing, compression, loudness) beyond sound groups.
- No rigging and animation authoring workflow detail (Moon Animator, keyframes, IK).
- No current numeric performance budgets for modern devices (triangle or part counts).
- No coverage of Roblox AI generation tools (Cube 3D, etc.) beyond brief mentions.
- No coverage of copyright and licensing for external assets beyond crediting.
- The 2018 part and terrain guidance has not been revalidated for current engine versions.
- No numeric WCAG contrast ratio thresholds; sources give only qualitative guidance.
- No Quest or mobile VR draw call, triangle or SurfaceGui budgets; 'few' and 'conservative' are not quantified.
- No TV-safe area dimensions or overscan percentages.
- No colourblind simulator or testing tool details beyond grayscale checks.
- No font list beyond the Comic Sans and OpenDyslexic mentions, and no font-size numbers.
- No haptic API details, intensity guidance or per-platform support.

- No quality, licensing or ownership caveats for generative texture tools.
- No localization guidance on number or date formatting beyond existing rules.
- No verification of indie self-reported figures such as orb offsets.
- No Studio performance numbers beyond the cited figures: no frame budgets for mid or low-end phones, PC or console, and no MicroProfiler or Developer Console procedure.
- No benchmarks for TV or console UI asset sizing, or art direction for gamepad-driven UI beyond the existing rules.
- No audio mixing or loudness guidance (LUFS, ducking, dynamic range) and no detail on sound effect design.
- No localisation art guidance (text expansion in images, right-to-left layouts, regional art).
- No post-processing guidance (Bloom, ColorCorrection, DepthOfField) beyond atmosphere and exposure.
- Animation Graph state machine details are covered only by a few items; no guidance on blend trees or layered upper-body masks.
- No guidance on asset licensing, creator royalties, or the legal side of selling UGC beyond the IP caution in T9933.
- No quantified guidance on thumbnails, icon sizes or click-through for item and pack assets.
- Face Capture and body video capture are beta; no verified guidance on accuracy beyond the listed tips.
- No per-device performance budgets (FPS, memory, draw calls) for mobile or console beyond the existing Quest 72 FPS note.
- No cost, limits or credit model for Texture Generator and Material Generator, nor data on how AI-made assets fare in moderation.
- Procedural Models beta: API surface, stability and production readiness are not documented beyond the sources above.
- Terrain scattering system: release date, API and resource numbers are not confirmed.
- Package versioning and AutoUpdate behaviour at scale, including cost of many linked instances.
- No loudness, LUFS or sound-group numeric guidance; the audio coverage here is sourcing and footsteps only.
- No localisation guidance for art containing text or culturally specific imagery beyond the colour-meaning research note.
- No guidance on skybox or lighting budgets specific to mobile devices.
- No details on Creator Store pricing, bundling or revenue for asset packs.
- Animation Editor UI differs across Studio versions; keyboard and menu steps from 2021-2022 posts may be outdated.
- Genre-specific lighting presets (horror, cartoon, realistic) are given only as one-line advice, without tested values.
- No [OFFICIAL] items in this batch; verify all tri-limit, Handle/HatAttachment, and lighting claims against current Roblox docs.
- Current status of SurfaceLight shadows under Future lighting (2020 advice) is not verified.
- Status of the Toolbox high-quality badge in 2026 is not verified.
- Details of the 2021 accessory/head system change are not covered beyond the MeshPart conversion note.
- Tripo and other AI-generated model licensing and commercial rights are not covered.
- Avatar Editor UI changes after 2026-10 are not covered.
- No mobile, accessibility, or performance budget numbers for non-UGC meshes in this batch.
- Photobashing copyright and licensing for reference images are not covered.
- Audio visualiser usage (record height, bars) is only partly covered and needs a dedicated source.
- No step-by-step workflow for creating procedural model attributes or custom parameters in Studio.
- No runtime performance comparison between procedural models and baked unique meshes.
- No cost or triangle-count impact figures for 3D upsampling.
- No limits, pricing, or availability details for the retexture tool or High quality texture mode.
- No precision-control values or guidance for the improved collision geometry feature.
- No shipped workflow or examples for 4D (behaviour-generated) assets.
- No mobile or Quest performance notes for 4K PBR textures or procedural models.
- No official Roblox source in these notes for the camera jolt fix; the camera advice is indie-only (SimTek).
- No detail on the Accessory tool's exact fit-box colour logic beyond the single Xaiyne tip.
- Which AI 3D generation tools were used and how they compare is not covered.
- No guidance on camera scripting structure in general (e.g., CurrentCamera control, tween timing); only the jolt-fix tips are covered.
- Toolbox simulator packs for UI icons (MonarchDev) were not added because they largely repeat the existing 'buy, commission or model missing art' rule; licensing checks for purchased icon packs are not covered here.
- No official Roblox documentation was cited for PivotOffset behaviour, CanQuery on previews, or BindToRenderStep priority; verify these against the official docs.
- No information on current Toolbox moderation status or whether the Roblox-account filter still works as described.
- No performance numbers for particles, camera shake, or tween counts; budget tests are needed for mobile and Quest.
- No licence or pricing information for Easy Visuals, Add Easy Texture, Meshy, or the orientation plugin.
- No source details for the PineappleDev pickup system (its scripts or framework), so the 'pickup' child requirement may not apply to other item systems.
- No accessibility guidance for highlights, particles, or camera shake (for example reduced motion or photosensitivity); cross-check against the existing accessibility rules.
- No mobile, console, or TV checks for the new effects, camera, and lobby items.
- No official Roblox docs cover these new items; the claims are indie, self-reported and mostly low-advice.
- No measured performance data for client-side animation, transparency cost or Toolbox map build time beyond the single 1-2 hour estimate.
- No guidance on ad creative length targets, platform ad specs or how much re-rendering the modular block approach saves.
- No licensing or vetting detail for studded Toolbox parts used in hand-built maps.
- No N or Q evidence IDs were provided for these items beyond T18643's time estimate.
- No official Roblox documentation was cited for PrimaryPart setup, Neon material, or import triangle limits; verify on creator docs.
- No specific Studio crash threshold beyond the one indie report (>100,000 triangles).
- Brush tool scatter and terrain settings save/restore workflows are not explained in detail.
- Substance Painter and Canva workflows are mentioned but not walked through.
- Version control or backup tooling for Studio projects is not covered beyond 'save often'.
- No official Roblox docs were cited for Reflectance behaviour under current rendering; verify against current engine docs.
- No detail on how scene-automation scripts affect load time or streaming in large experiences.
- Items T18984 and T18985 were not added; their MeshID-paste and per-face Decal details are not verified against current Studio UI.

## Official 2026 engine and API changes

_Chunk 12 (2026-11-01). Labels: OFFICIAL = Roblox Creator Docs release notes or staff DevForum posts. Status is as stated by the source (Pending = listed in release notes but not yet live). Items marked DEPRECATED name a deprecated, removed or sunset API/behaviour. Dates are post or release dates; 'Release N ~date' = Studio/engine release N, date approximate from the DevForum release-notes post._

- **Terrain Custom Materials exit Early Access; performance gains Studio-only** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-10-27): Custom Terrain Materials and first terrain rendering, storage and delivery improvements exit Early Access. Publish with up to 62 custom terrain materials; enable Expanded Terrain in Workspace. Terrain performance improvements are Studio-only for now due to a client crash bug. Build impact: Enable Expanded Terrain to use custom materials for all players; don't expect client-side terrain performance gains until re-enabled. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4950620).
- **Texture generation Quality and Fast modes** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-10-16): Texture generation Quality mode creates 4K PBR textures with color, roughness and metalness maps in about 2 minutes. Fast mode creates 1K color-only textures in about 30 seconds. Build impact: Use Fast for quick iteration and Quality when PBR maps are needed. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4938282).
- **Package Overrides in Studio** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-10-15): Edits to a package are tracked as overrides and reapplied on updates. Revert via the Properties revert icon or Package > Revert Overrides. Editing and reverting no longer show warning dialogs. Build impact: Local package edits survive package updates; revert overrides to return to the source package. **DEPRECATED:** Modified Packages dialog and package edit/revert warning dialogs Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4936628).
- **Collision fidelity Tunable and visualizer improvements** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-09-17): MeshPart CollisionFidelity = Tunable adds a CollisionPrecision slider to tune performance vs precision per MeshPart. Default precision scales with mesh size. The collision visualizer renders visual and collision geometry together, with filters. Build impact: Tune CollisionPrecision per MeshPart; existing meshes are unchanged. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4878198).
- **Collision groups move to WorldRoot** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-09-03): Collision group management moves from PhysicsService to WorldRoot, giving each WorldModel its own 32-group budget. New WorldModel.UseWorkspaceCollisionGroups property. Raycast, GetPartBoundsInBox and CanCollideWith respect groups in WorldModels. Build impact: Migrate collision group calls to WorldRoot equivalents. WorldModel collision group editing is temporarily disabled in the Editor. **DEPRECATED:** PhysicsService collision group methods (CollisionGroupsAreCollidable, CollisionGroupSetCollidable, GetMaxCollisionGroups, GetRegisteredCollisionGroups, IsCollisionGroupRegistered, RegisterCollisionGroup, RenameCollisionGroup, UnregisterCollisionGroup) Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4848541).
- **Terrain Scattering, Path Splines, Decals, Virtual Texturing and SDF** (OFFICIAL, Pending, 2026-09-11): Planned terrain tools: rule-based asset scattering, 3D spline Path tool, PBR Projected Decals, Virtual Texturing, and Signed Distance Fields replacing single-percentage voxel occupancy for smoother boundaries. Build impact: Plan terrain workflows around these tools once released; not yet available. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4865617).
- **Asset PromptCreatePlatformContentAsync with EditableImage** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-10-19): AssetService:PromptCreatePlatformContentAsync(Object) takes an Object, including EditableImage, and publishes to the player's inventory. Requires the Allow Mesh / Image APIs setting and a published place to test. Build impact: Replace PromptCreateAssetAsync calls; test in a published place. **DEPRECATED:** AssetService:PromptCreateAssetAsync(Instance) Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4944421).
- **BasePart:BindToCollisionSummaries** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-10-06): New BasePart:BindToCollisionSummaries(callback) fires once per frame after physics with contact Point, Normal, RelativeVelocity, Area and Part pair data. Summaries are generated on the network owner and not replicated. Build impact: Use for per-frame contact data; don't expect it on non-owners. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4918568).
- **Revamped Asset Manager** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-09-01): Revamped Asset Manager adds folder hierarchy, search across user, group and game inventories, sorting, filtering, bulk selection, drag-and-drop, sharing and the Universal Importer. Open via Home > Assets or Window > Asset Manager. Build impact: Legacy Asset Manager is replaced; new aliases can no longer be created, existing ones keep working. **DEPRECATED:** Legacy Asset Manager; creating new aliases Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4844672).
- **Auto-Reimport** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-09-08): Auto-Reimport re-imports Model, SurfaceAppearance or Decal instances with saved Reimport settings whenever the source file changes on disk. Enable via File > Beta Features, then right-click > Reimport > Auto-Reimport. Build impact: Setting is per-session and each reimport creates new asset IDs. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4859863).
- **Texture generation, mesh segmentation and image input in Assistant** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-09-23): Studio texture generation is revamped via ribbon, Right-click > Generate > Texture, or /generate_texture. /segment_mesh splits imported meshes into parts (up to five names per run). Image inputs supported for /generate_procedural_model and /generate_mesh. Build impact: Use recommended part names for better segmentation results. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4890084).
- **EditableMesh batching, parallel queries and quads** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-08-06): EditableMesh adds nine bulk methods (BatchAdd, BatchRemove, BatchSetValues, BatchGetValues, Clear). Query methods are safe in parallel Luau after task.desynchronize(). Beta EditableMesh:AddFace supports quads. Build impact: Batch edits instead of per-element calls; run mutating methods serially after task.synchronize(). Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4779401).
- **Animation Graphs full release, State Machines in beta** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-10-08): Animation Graphs are fully released and run in live games. Parameters are set via AnimationTrack:SetParameter(name, value) and replicate automatically. State Machine nodes and Luau expressions are in beta. Build impact: Drive graph blending from scripts with SetParameter; enable State Machines via Beta Features. **DEPRECATED:** Beta limitation restricting animation graph playback to local testing in Studio Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4921939).
- **Adaptive Animation GA and R15 schema renames** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-04-29): Adaptive Animation reuses animations across R15, R6 and custom rigs via HumanoidRigDescription. DigitsRigDescription maps up to 15 joints per hand. R15 renames: Pelvis replaced by Spine; LeftToes/RightToes become LeftToeBase/RightToeBase. Build impact: Update joint mappings to the new R15 names; the Custom Humanoid rig type is gone from the importer. **DEPRECATED:** Custom Humanoid rig type in importer; Pelvis joint mapping; LeftToes; RightToes Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4605672).
- **Mesh Streaming and improved LoDs** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-04-27): Mesh Streaming and improved cloud LoD generation are opt-in via Workspace.MeshStreamingAndImprovedLoDs = Enabled. Meshes stream LoDs by scene importance within a memory budget. Default expected around July 2026. Build impact: Opt in and test; don't rely on MeshPart.RenderFidelity, which is to be removed in Phase 3. **DEPRECATED:** Workspace.MeshStreamingAndImprovedLoDs and MeshPart.RenderFidelity (to be removed in Phase 3) Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4601232).
- **ProceduralModel full release** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-05-18): ProceduralModel is fully released and on by default. It generates parametric models via code, Assistant (/generate_procedural_model) or Studio MCP, and works in Team Create and live games. It cannot yet become Packages. Build impact: Use for parametric assets; avoid relying on Package workflows for them. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4642542).
- **Scene Analysis fully released** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-05-05): Scene Analysis offers real-time treemap views for script memory, unparented instances, audio and animation memory, instance composition and triangles, plus programmatic access via SceneAnalysisService. Build impact: Use it to find memory hogs; script it through SceneAnalysisService. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4620678).
- **Universal Importer and importer scale changes** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-04-02): Universal Importer is fully released with image, audio and video support, planned to replace the legacy Bulk Importer. Importer uses a 25:7 Stud/meter ratio; scale factor 5.6 preserves old behavior. Build impact: Set scale factor 5.6 to keep old import sizes; use the custom scale factor field under File Geometry. **DEPRECATED:** Legacy Bulk Importer; 20:1 Stud/meter conversion ratio for non-Stud units Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4554615).
- **Reimport fully released** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-05-06): Right-click a Model, SurfaceAppearance or Decal (or press Alt+Shift+R) to update from external files, preserving properties and hierarchy. Reimport settings are stored. Build impact: Iterate on external art without rebuilding hierarchy or properties. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4622567).
- **Asset Privacy for Image, Mesh and Decal** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-05-05): Asset Privacy is fully released for Image, Mesh and Decal assets, with bulk sharing and bulk setting to Open Use. Enabled by default for new accounts; existing assets unaffected. Private assets now work for Decal Normal, Metalness and Roughness maps. Build impact: Share assets explicitly with the experience owner or group; sharing directly to an experience ID doesn't yet work. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4620416).
- **EditableMesh/EditableImage ownership and baking** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-04-15): CreateEditableMeshAsync and CreateEditableImageAsync work with assets owned by or shared with the creator, Studio user or player. AssetService:CreateDataModelContentAsync bakes editable data into static Content. Build impact: Use CreateDataModelContentAsync output with MeshContent and TextureContent. **DEPRECATED:** Restriction of these APIs to assets owned by the experience creator or logged-in user Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4578818).
- **Store assets sandboxed in Studio** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-05-13): Store assets inserted into Studio are automatically sandboxed. LoadUnownedAsset, LoadAsset, LoadString and CapabilityControl are blocked at runtime, and getfenv/setfenv are unavailable to them. Build impact: Inserted Store assets relying on those APIs will break at runtime. **DEPRECATED:** getfenv/setfenv for sandboxed assets; LoadUnownedAsset, LoadAsset, LoadString, CapabilityControl for Store assets Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4634642).
- **Decal and SurfaceAppearance material properties** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 742 ~2026-10-08): Planned: Decal.EmissiveMaskContent, EmissiveStrength and EmissiveTint; SurfaceAppearance.SurfaceSlot with MeshPart:GetSurfaceSlotsAsync(); SurfaceAppearance.ResampleMode. Decal.Rotation (live) rotates UV maps. Build impact: Per-sub-mesh surface targeting will be possible once released. Source: Creator Docs release notes 742.
- **Next-Gen Draggers beta** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-06-25): Next-Gen Draggers refresh Move, Rotate and Scale tools with planar move handles, view-space move and rotate, trackball rotation and front-facing rotation rings. Enable via File > Beta Features. Build impact: Try for faster building; enable in Beta Features. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4703155).

## Appendix: every extracted tip on this topic

- 2014-12-27 Emancyphur [2:21](https://youtu.be/AFY6Yfm-oaw?t=141): Select a model part in Explorer, then use the Rotate tool (blue, yellow, red rings) to pose it; use Move to reposition.
- 2014-12-27 Emancyphur [5:07](https://youtu.be/AFY6Yfm-oaw?t=307): In Toolbox > Models, look for the high-quality badge (circle, triangle, square icon); Roblox hand-picks these as reliable assets.
- 2014-12-27 Emancyphur [5:47](https://youtu.be/AFY6Yfm-oaw?t=347): Test high-quality models in play mode before saving; anchor parts first or they may fall over.
- 2015-09-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-slicecenter-robloxs-9-slice-gui-property/18313): Design the template so corners stay fixed, side edges stretch in one direction, and the center stretches; example: 48x48 image with 6px borders gives a 36x36 center.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Learn building plugins and their keyboard shortcuts (Build v4, F3X, qCmd Ult); the author says most builders only got good after using plugins.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Study other showcases closely for detail rather than skimming for two minutes; use Google images for inspiration.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Build on a fixed grid (e.g. 0.2 or 0.25 studs) and never switch increments mid-build to avoid Z-fighting and messy flow.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): When adding detail on a 0.2 grid, offset parts by 0.04 studs (0.05 on a 0.25 grid) instead of leaving gaps.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Combine wedges, corner wedges, cylinders, spheres and blocks instead of only blocks and triangles to make curves, bevels and ramps without overlap.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Use a triangle terrain tool that does not leave gaps between triangles; Build v4 has one built in.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Use Resize Align and Gapfill (by stravant) to fix alignment and gaps between parts.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Add transparency to scorched or ground decals so they blend into the background instead of looking pasted on.
- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Revisit old builds that look weak and upgrade them, while keeping the map's building style consistent.
- 2016-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017): Fix ridges left by the Smooth tool by running Grow or Erode at minimum strength over the affected area; this adds or removes a thin layer and looks cleaner.
- 2016-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017): Blend two terrain materials by growing the opposite material at low strength on each side of the boundary to create a gradual transition.
- 2016-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017): Use the Paint tool's plane lock to paint only along one plane, useful for layered material bands such as canyon strata.
- 2016-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017): Use the Add tool's Snap to Grid and Ignore Water options to align chunks to the voxel grid and avoid overwriting water.
- 2016-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-method-hooking-and-keeping-your-game-protected/31442): Store models and maps in ServerStorage rather than ReplicatedStorage to reduce asset leaks; clone client-needed assets, delete originals, and hold references to clones.
- 2017-03-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729): Humanoid clothing is not auto-imported; export an OBJ from Studio and link meshes to the generated bones manually if needed.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [6:18](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=378): Design mobile-first: start from the smallest screen (phone portrait), prioritise essential content, then scale up to tablet and desktop. Cutting down is easier than fitting a desktop layout onto a phone.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [7:42](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=462): Keep main actions in the thumb-zone green area; avoid red corners for critical buttons. Non-interactive stats or info can sit in the red zone.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [7:11](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=431): Avoid Roblox mobile UI restriction areas (top menu bar, bottom-left move, bottom-right jump) so your UI does not overlap them.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [12:05](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=725): Make touch buttons big enough with comfortable spacing; a cursor can hit small targets but a thumb cannot, so mis-taps are common on phones.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [12:39](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=759): For gamepad navigation, consider where focus starts and how far players must move; place the most-used buttons close to the starting point.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [14:12](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=852): Keep font sizes, spacing and colours consistent within a hierarchy. If text doesn't fit, use two lines rather than shrinking it, and account for localisation length.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [14:53](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=893): Keep text readable on small screens: don't shrink fonts past legibility; add a scroll bar if content doesn't fit. Check colour contrast with an online contrast checker.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [19:59](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=1199): Keep UI consistent across phone and tablet, and similar for mobile and desktop; console can differ more because its interaction model is different.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [4:40](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=280): Use string tables and Roblox localization infrastructure from the start; missing them caps your addressable market (e.g. Spanish-speaking audience) for good.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [17:55](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=1075): Use UI layouts and dynamic constraints instead of hard-coded pixel sizes so UI scales across devices.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [20:41](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=1241): Abstract how players interact with game systems so your game keeps working on future platforms without rework.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [22:56](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1376): Make hit feedback unambiguous: unique animation per hit direction plus sparkle effects for blocks, so players know why an attack failed.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [24:17](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1457): Invest in parry animations and particle effects that are slowed down and obvious; they make players feel cool without health bars or status icons.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [26:44](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1604): Push characters back on stagger based on hit direction so attacks feel impactful and fights have momentum.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [4:19](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=259): Export the localization table, run a rough first-pass translation (Google Sheets Translate macro), fix broken parameter tokens, re-import CSV, and test with the Spanish button.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [9:56](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=596): Use icons instead of words for weapons and currency; icons avoid gender, plural, and conjugation problems in translation.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [12:02](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=722): Use the localization system's format strings for numbers instead of hand-formatting, since digit grouping and decimal rules vary by locale.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [18:03](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=1083): Consider tailoring art and cultural preferences per region, as League of Legends did with Karma art for China versus North America.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [21:28](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=1288): Write a thin localization helper module and a small plugin (e.g. one-button dialogue table update) that knows where your text lives.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [24:29](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1469): Terrain is efficient per unit of detail with stable performance; don't export it to mesh parts or hollow it into thin shells, which runs slower.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [28:26](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1706): On low-end mobile, budget tens of thousands of parts per area; hundreds of thousands are fine only with streaming and sparse layouts.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [31:22](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1882): Use Roblox materials rather than SurfaceAppearance/texture objects. Don't use unions as a performance fix; they defeat instancing. Keep unions to dozens of parts.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [32:50](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1970): Avoid many transparent objects; fully transparent (1) is cheapest, fully opaque (0) next, and partial transparency is the costliest. Don't hide parts by covering them with decals.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [17:06](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1026): Split on-screen UI into separate ScreenGuis by update frequency. Updating any element in a shared ScreenGui forces the slow render path.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [12:57](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=777): Cloning mesh parts (instancing) keeps memory roughly constant. Resizing or recolouring a copy does not block instancing for mesh parts, but wedges weren't instanced yet at the time.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [29:29](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1769): Use simple collision fidelity (Box or Hull) when possible. Disabling collision on distant anchored parts only helps when nothing moving nearby can touch them.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [16:25](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=985): A 1024x1024 texture costs about 5 MB of memory regardless of PNG file size; texture transcoding will reduce mobile memory.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [13:53](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=833): Keep mesh detail and volume consistent across characters and environment; a chunky character in a thin, detailed world feels like it doesn't belong.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [13:16](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=796): Check silhouettes by making models flat black; if each character or class is still recognisable at a glance, the shape language works.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [15:48](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=948): Use a similar hue and saturation across the whole game, and reserve high contrast or complementary colours for what the player must focus on.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [21:36](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1296): Pick the palette, texture style (photoreal vs cartoon) and baked vs real-time lighting early, and apply them consistently across UI, decals and assets.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [23:21](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1401): Treat lighting as a foundation, not last-minute polish; lighting and skybox matching can make mismatched assets feel like one world.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [24:58](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1498): Adjust light direction, intensity and skybox together to change a scene's mood; matching the skybox to lighting makes the world feel unified.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [29:00](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1740): Place the main light so it doesn't flatten the key point of interest; in the demo, moving light off the focal object increased its contrast.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [38:53](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=2333): Games can break visual rules (different model styles) if they keep shared lighting and materials, which keeps the world feeling immersive.
- 2018-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434): Build the first-person view model as a clone of the character with only arms, upper torso and head kept; set head and torso transparency to 1 and anchor the head so its CFrame can be set directly.
- 2018-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434): Add invisible reference parts (Handle, Right, Left, Aim) with consistent front/up faces on weapon models so offsets and hand placement stay predictable across weapons.
- 2018-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434): Expect to tune Motor6D C0 per weapon (for example C0 = CFrame.new(1,-1.5,-2)) since weapon size and camera fit vary.
- 2019-01-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752): Start with Roblox's default or old animator, then try Moon Animator or Blender once basic animations like a wave are comfortable.
- 2019-01-26 AlvinBlox [0:58](https://youtu.be/0UhMpxxRFyc?t=58): Set baseplate to a green grass texture and remove studs; add a sky box for a nicer look. Optional polish.
- 2019-01-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-a-toolobject-with-a-dummy-in-the-animation-editor/232317): Create a Handle part in the Tool, set Transparency to 1 and CanCollide to false, and place it where the tool should be held from.
- 2019-01-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-a-toolobject-with-a-dummy-in-the-animation-editor/232317): Make the Handle the primary part; weld all other tool parts to it (not the Handle itself) so the tool doesn't fall apart while animating.
- 2019-02-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059): Don't put textures/decals on every side of a part; only on faces players see. A brick with textures on all 6 sides is roughly 7x more expensive to draw than a plain material brick.
- 2019-02-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059): Use materials instead of textures where possible, but use Neon and Glass sparingly; they are the most costly materials.
- 2019-02-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059): Compare Parts, Unions and MeshParts with the Performance Analyser; Meshes are typically most efficient per unit of detail, Unions medium, welded/squished Parts worst. Union when short on time, but not habitually.
- 2019-02-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059): Terrain has little memory impact, so use a variety of terrain materials freely; water has a slight cost but is fine to use.
- 2019-02-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059): Avoid many parts with varying transparency. Use transparency 0 or 1 and merge several glass parts into one part to reduce render work.
- 2019-03-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/2-joint-2-limb-inverse-kinematics/252399): Apply solved angles either as Motor6D/Weld C0 offsets or by positioning limb parts directly from the base plane CFrame.
- 2019-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785): Export each UI element as a separate image and colour the symbol assets white so they can be tinted in Studio.
- 2019-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785): Run exported images through Pixelfix to remove dark edges caused by transparent pixels when images are scaled down.
- 2019-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785): Upload reusable UI assets via the Roblox website for use across games, and avoid duplicate uploads to keep the inventory uncluttered.
- 2019-06-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-game/287493): Give builds a written story or lore first; storytelling-driven builds (e.g. After The Flash) were cited as captivating players.
- 2019-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253): Verify root orientation before rigging: green (top) arrow must point up; red (right) and blue (front) define axes. Use Handles in the command bar to check.
- 2019-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253): Weld parts in Studio rather than live servers to avoid slightly displaced parts in running servers.
- 2019-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253): Prefer WeldConstraint over Weld: it needs no C0/C1 offsets and welds relative position automatically. Use Motor6D only for animation joints.
- 2019-09-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484): Group all tool parts under a model with a BodyAttach root part near the trigger/grip; place the model under the animating rig dummy.
- 2019-09-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484): Use Constraint Editor plugin: select BodyAttach, Ctrl-click parts; New Motor6D for moving parts (magazine, bolt), New Weld for static parts. Every part needs a weld or Motor6D.
- 2019-09-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484): Play tool animations on Equipped and stop on Unequipped using a LocalScript under the tool, with Humanoid:LoadAnimation.
- 2019-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258): Pick three colours (base, dark, light) first and reuse them across the whole HUD for visual consistency.
- 2019-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258): Design HUD at 1920x1080 in Photoshop, and convert shapes to smart objects so they stay sharp when scaled down then up.
- 2019-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258): Use clipping masks (Ctrl+Shift+N, 'Use Previous Layer to Create Clipping Mask') to paint textures confined inside a shape.
- 2019-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258): Use reference images (e.g. Google Images or Pinterest searches) when painting textures like wood grain.
- 2019-10-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805): Set Lighting Technology to Shadowmap to get dynamic shadows without baking or scripting.
- 2019-10-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805): Set ShadowSoftness to 0 for crisper shadows.
- 2019-10-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805): Use a morning or late-afternoon time of day (e.g. 09:00:00) for warmer, more atmospheric light.
- 2019-10-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805): Raise Lighting Brightness from the default 2 to 3-5 for most builds; use 6-7 for nature builds.
- 2019-10-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805): Set ColorShift_Top to a warm yellow-orange (e.g. 255,138,35) for a morning glow.
- 2019-10-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805): Use a cool blue ColorShift_Top (e.g. 44,128,255) for a moon-glow night look; adjust brightness to taste.
- 2019-10-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805): Pair lighting with a skybox that matches the time of day; the author says it noticeably improves realism.
- 2019-11-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801): Minimize clothing memory: Roblox builds one clothing texture per humanoid, so keep texture file sizes small, limit item count, and reuse items.
- 2019-12-01 TheDevKing [12:59](https://youtu.be/QC2bDZsFiz4?t=779): Store the reward tool in ReplicatedStorage and clone it into the player's Backpack or Character when granting.
- 2019-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672): Use Gaea's free version for terrain generation; its 1k resolution export is enough for Roblox terrain without a watermark.
- 2019-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672): Import OBJ meshes as Part triangle type, not Terrain; Terrain import produces an awful result.
- 2019-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672): Use FillWedge in the conversion script for better results than the older method, and back up the Studio file first since crashing is possible.
- 2019-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672): Heightmap import into Roblox is faster and supports higher resolution than mesh conversion; use it unless you need overhangs or caves.
- 2019-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672): Keep Blender decimation optional: reducing to about 200,000 faces can create holes after conversion, so skip it if your PC can handle the full mesh.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584): Set PrimaryPart on creature and character models and clone them with Archivable enabled, so they can be positioned and copied per battle.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Define scale, proportion and contrast before building; use contrast to direct the viewer's eye to the element you want to emphasise.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Build in passes: basic shapes first, then minimal details for semi-realistic perspective, then nuance, then fine details last.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Pick a colour scheme (monochromatic, analogous, complementary or triadic) and use palette tools like coolors.co, colorhunt.co or colormind.io.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Set OutdoorAmbient and lighting to establish atmosphere; showcase places are usually dominated by one colour or a small set of colours.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Research the cultural meaning of colours in the region you're emulating instead of copying online colour-meaning charts.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Gather references from ArtStation, DeviantArt, Dribbble and Behance, then derive something original from them rather than copying.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Choose a style (e.g. Gothic, Brutalist, Steampunk) and research it deeply before building; style determines the whole look.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Block building and low-poly are both valid; choose based on whether you're willing to learn a new 3D tool like Blender.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Use Light Editor plugin to view and tweak all lights in a map; use Atmos to browse and insert skyboxes quickly.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Use Stravant ResizeAlign and GapFill plugins to align faces and fill gaps between parts faster than manual resizing.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-theory-101/457156): Lighting strongly changes a scene's feel; choose natural or artificial light sources deliberately to match the environment.
- 2020-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318): Keep the viewmodel separate from the gun model; merge them only when equipped. Don't put the viewmodel in workspace.
- 2020-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318): Rig animation parts to weaponRootPart rather than the receiver; Blender did not recognise parts rigged to the receiver, so reload mag movement failed.
- 2020-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318): Use a Rojo + VS Code workflow instead of the default Studio script editor; the author found it much more productive.
- 2020-04-02 GnomeCode [0:22](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=22): Set a part's Reflectance to 1 for a chrome look; anchor it and rename it so it's identifiable in the explorer.
- 2020-04-05 AlvinBlox [1:53](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=113): Use MeshParts rather than Parts with a mesh inside; paste the MeshID into a MeshPart so the egg is a single native part.
- 2020-04-05 AlvinBlox [3:57](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=237): Decals must be applied to each face of a part; textures wrap the whole surface. Copy the asset ID to set a texture.
- 2020-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-part-2-2020/581791): Scale weapon models to real player height; oversized guns broke the author's animations and needed rework.
- 2020-06-26 GnomeCode [33:28](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2008): Moving models into game folders before running took setup time; have scripts set up the scene automatically to avoid manual steps.
- 2020-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943): Disable default footstep sounds and add custom footsteps synced to the bobbing, since default footsteps won't match the camera motion.
- 2020-07-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collectionservice-in-a-nutshell/668984): Install the Tag Editor Plugin (by Sweetheartichoke) to create tags and apply them to selected parts in Studio without scripting; use World View to verify tagged parts.
- 2020-08-02 Emancyphur [3:06](https://youtu.be/ScdTM3ju-yk?t=186): Don't rely on SurfaceLight shadows under Future; they are not supported yet, so use SpotLights if shadows matter.
- 2020-08-02 Emancyphur [4:59](https://youtu.be/ScdTM3ju-yk?t=299): Re-tune lighting for every time of day, especially interiors that relied on few light sources, since Future lighting changes how areas look.
- 2020-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866): Pick a stylization first: low poly uses bright flat colours for cheerful mood; high detail uses muted, darker tones for realism or danger.
- 2020-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866): Keep vibrant colours rare in realistic builds so that intentional accents like flowers or mushrooms stand out.
- 2020-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-raycasting-gun/723716): Pre-build the bullet beam as an anchored, non-collidable neon part in ServerStorage and clone and resize it per shot.
- 2020-08-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207): Before converting a model to an accessory: weld all parts together, unanchor them, turn off collisions, name one part Handle, and add an Attachment inside the Handle.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Minimise part count: combine parts where possible and use greedy meshing for block-based games.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Apply textures only to faces that players can see; skip hidden faces.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Anchor parts, use SmoothPlastic, CollisionFidelity Box, and disable CastShadow where detail is not needed; set Transparency to 0 or 1.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Use the lowest triangle count that achieves the look; a cube needs 12 triangles.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Set Model LevelOfDetail to Automatic or Disabled with StreamingEnabled, and RenderFidelity to Automatic, to help lower-end devices.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Copy and transform identical models rather than individually editing them, to share memory.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013): Match lighting to game style: cartoony sky for cartoony games, dark fog for horror games.
