- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013): For mild background fog, set FogEnd to 1000-2000; default is 100000, and lower values increase fog.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013): Use a BlurEffect Size of 1 or 2 for a subtle showcase-style blur.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013): Use ClockTime (24-hour clock) to set sun position; 12 is noon, 24 or 0 is midnight, PM hours are 12 + hour.
- 2020-11-17 BloxianCode [2:34](https://youtu.be/vP_d8TWny3g?t=154): Only use the move and rotate tools when posing the model and camera to keep the composition simple.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [16:58](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1018): Avoid models for cosmetic bullets in FastCast; they are too laggy. Use simple in-code parts such as a small metal ball.
- 2021-01-28 Emancyphur [3:19](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=199): Filter Toolbox by the Roblox creator account to get vetted tools; the creator said Roblox-uploaded models are legitimate.
- 2021-02-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/justice-the-awesome-npc-system/1026927): Use discriminators (e.g. gender) to restrict which assets apply to which NPCs, and the 'all' discriminator to share assets across every NPC type without duplication.
- 2021-02-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/justice-the-awesome-npc-system/1026927): Set an accessory or hair slot to -1 to skip it, giving NPCs variety without forcing an item.
- 2021-03-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419): Keep ordinary meshes like rocks, crates, tables, chairs, barrels, and bricks around 12 to 2,000 triangles instead of maxing out at 10k.
- 2021-03-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419): Name parts, models, and folders with short descriptive names from the start, and group related items into folders to reduce Explorer clutter.
- 2021-03-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-a-mesh-deformation-ocean/1122587): Balance ocean mesh resolution against performance; the author's ~8k tris worked but was at the edge of performant.
- 2021-03-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-a-mesh-deformation-ocean/1122587): Scale up the plane's UVs before export so textures repeat more and look smaller/clearer.
- 2021-04-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/camera-animations-for-cutscenes-and-gameplay/1181691): Use Moon Animator to keyframe a camera's CFrame track; add a character with animation to sync camera shots to character animation.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [0:20](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=20): Name the central part of an accessory exactly 'Handle' (capital H); other names won't equip properly on characters.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [2:24](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=144): Recreate meshes as MeshPart with copied MeshId and TextureId from SpecialMesh before the 2021 accessory/head change to stay compatible.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [3:46](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=226): Pick the most central part of a multi-part model as the Handle so the accessory sits centred on the character's head.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [6:52](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=412): Name positioning attachments 'HatAttachment' so the accessory positions identically on both R6 and R15 characters.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [7:34](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=454): Weld all accessory parts together; unwelded parts are dropped when the accessory is added to a character, only the Handle transfers.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [8:15](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=495): Weld-button auto-welding only joins intersecting parts; manually add WeldConstraints for the rest or use a welding script.
- 2021-05-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208): Keep the gun to as few parts as possible; name the functional parts (barrel, handle, sight) in a GunComponents folder for scripts to reference.
- 2021-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842): Prefer built-in materials and MaterialService over textures, since textures use more memory (GraphicsTexture in Developer Console).
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Export icons and images from Figma as PNG (or SVG for quality, but Studio needs PNG); make icons white so Studio ImageColor3 can recolor them.
- 2021-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916): Copy Figma gradients into UIGradient and rounded corners into UICorner; match sizes from Figma W/H values.
- 2021-05-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701): Set the ModularScript's p0.Material from 'Neon' to 'Glass' to get the glass-style blur part.
- 2021-06-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/meshpart-usage-performance-optimizations/1319217): Only import MeshParts when an object can't be built efficiently from primitives; fewer triangles is the goal.
- 2021-06-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/meshpart-usage-performance-optimizations/1319217): If a UnionOperation will be copied multiple times, export it via Export Selection and reimport as an .obj MeshPart instead.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [1:06](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=66): Learn hotkeys: Ctrl+D duplicates in place (new copy inside the original spot), Ctrl+G groups parts, Ctrl+1-5 switch tools, F zooms to selection.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [2:29](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=149): Use Ctrl+D instead of Ctrl+C/Ctrl+V when you want the copy placed beside the original rather than stacked on top.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [3:50](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=230): Hold Shift while moving the camera to slow it down for precise detail work.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [5:32](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=332): Use the move tool locked to a single axis for accurate placement; it stops parts slipping and getting caught on neighbours.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [6:12](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=372): Turn on collisions when placing parts so they stop against existing geometry instead of passing through it.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [9:41](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=581): Use the transform tool's snap-to-grid option aligned to a part so rotations and resizes are relative to that part's orientation.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [11:46](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=706): Use the Build Rig dummy (Plugins > Build Rig) to check doorways and windows are human-scale before building further.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [16:26](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=986): Keep grid snapping/increments enabled and build at consistent increments (e.g. 0.5 studs, 1 or 5 studs for big walls) to avoid gaps, overlaps and skewed parts.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [17:49](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1069): Overlapping coplanar faces cause z-fighting (flickering textures); avoid placing two surfaces at exactly the same position.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [19:52](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1192): Size parts to exact dimensions with snapping on so later edits are easy; unsnapped builds end up with decimals like 52.082 that are hard to modify.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [23:17](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1397): Choose increment size per task: 10 studs for big walls, 1 or 0.5 for detail, 0.1 for fine parts; avoid going below ~0.1.
- 2021-07-06 GnomeCode [28:13](https://youtu.be/7vXk_YqxW-c?t=1693): Prefer Roblox-endorsed (orange badge) free models when worried about safety; they are vetted and usually better quality.
- 2021-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/comprehensive-beginners-guide-to-cframes-and-how-to-use-them-cframe-guide/1334085): Use part.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0,5,0)) to place a part relative to a rotated part, rather than adding vectors to Position.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Tweak every lighting setting until it fits your own game; the provided values are starting points, not universal settings.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Add a thin foil-material block under the water surface to create extra sun reflections on water.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Set ExposureCompensation to about -3 and raise Lighting Brightness to about 65 for crisper daytime lighting; lower exposure needs higher brightness.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Exposure compensation trick requires brighter spot/point lights and does not work well for night scenes.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Exposure compensation also removes the glare from neon parts.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Bloom has little visible effect once the exposure compensation trick is applied.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Use SunRays for any time of day, but keep them subtle for realism since real sun rays are not very intense.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): If sun rays make the sun look too big, change SunAngularSize in the Skybox instead of only changing the ray spread.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Atmosphere requires a skybox; fog is a simpler alternative found in Lighting.
- 2021-08-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033): Use ColorCorrection to set a cinematic look or a black-and-white effect across the whole screen.
- 2021-08-26 Roblox Creators [30:55](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=1855): Block out levels in grey parts in Studio and playtest the layout before adding art, so layout changes stay quick.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [10:00](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=600): Keep cadence releases primarily art-based (reskins, recolors, re-themed existing assets); art is the easiest part to outsource.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [21:03](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1263): Reuse assets: recolor models, reuse rigs and animations for similarly shaped models, or tweak geometry for a new silhouette.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [17:48](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=1068): Create a style sheet (colours, fonts, basic elements, padding) before designing UI to keep frames consistent and reduce rework.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [1:01:36](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=3696): Use a clear division of labour: one person does layouts and scripting, the other does icons and mockups, then iterate together heavily.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [1:09:02](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=4142): Check accessibility in colour choices; the Outlaster team's early colours ignored colourblind players and a colour-matching competition had to be replaced with symbols.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [1:10:01](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=4201): Start UI on mobile, the most limiting platform, then scale up to PC; mobile-to-PC is easier than PC-to-mobile.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [14:21](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=861): Write an art bible covering shapes, colours, UI and character design so every area of the game feels consistent.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [16:52](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=1012): Keep environments low-detail around the avatar so clutter doesn't distract from the roleplay; use chunky, oversized shapes.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [12:16](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=736): Define world building as providing a unique environment with tools so players can tell their own stories; the world comes alive through player choices.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [1:18:09](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=4689): Avoid generic settings; world uniqueness matters because virtual worlds are not limited to real-world constraints.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [9:56](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=596): Make cadence releases mostly art: reskins, recolors and geometry tweaks of assets already in the game.
- 2021-11-10 GameMakers [3:47](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=227): Build creatives from modular pre-rendered blocks (standard intro/outro plus interchangeable content blocks) so duration can be varied quickly by reordering or removing blocks.
- 2021-11-10 Suphi Kaner [21:12](https://youtu.be/JyMxrcqEzu8?t=1272): Copy Idle and Run animations from an existing character and load them via Animator; set Idle to Core-priority and Run to Movement priority.
- 2021-11-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dynamic-weather-library-beta-10/1547614): Ensure an Atmosphere object named "Atmosphere" in Lighting and a Clouds terrain instance named "Clouds" exist; the module needs both to run.
- 2021-11-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dynamic-weather-library-beta-10/1547614): Tune weather behaviour by editing DynamicWeatherDatabase min/max ranges for temperature, humidity, wind shear and pressure rather than code.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [9:13](https://youtu.be/TLRakuu4vd0?t=553): Useful asset sources: Unity Asset Store (needs conversion), RoDev Market (Roblox-made), game-icons.net for icons, Zapsplat for sound effects. Credit creators.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [10:34](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=634): Add consistent padding (about 15px) around all element edges as a baseline polish step.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [11:09](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=669): Pick one limited color palette with gradients and apply it across all UI to boost readability and consistency.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [11:48](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=708): Standardise corner radius across all UI frames (example used 6px) for a unified look.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [12:09](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=729): Simplify overwhelming UI by enforcing consistency first, then remove or repurpose elements that add clutter.
- 2021-12-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301): Use Brick Counter plugin to measure total parts in your place and per model before optimizing.
- 2021-12-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301): Use Texture Library Tool plugin and low-poly texture packs to add color and realism to low-poly builds cheaply.
- 2021-12-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301): Use Stravant GapFill and Model Reflect plugins for precise connecting geometry and symmetrical builds.
- 2022-01-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginners-guide-on-scripting-animefighting-vfxvisual-effects-part-1/1610853): Set hitboxes to Neon, Transparency 0.5, CanCollide false, CanTouch true, CastShadow false for visible, non-blocking debug hitboxes.
- 2022-02-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tools-the-easiest-and-best-way-for-melees/1654589): Group all weapon parts and meshes, add an invisible part named Handle directly under the Tool (not the model), and weld everything to it.
- 2022-02-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tools-the-easiest-and-best-way-for-melees/1654589): Use the Tool Grip Editor plugin to align how the character holds the weapon before animating.
- 2022-02-17 Roblox Creators [46:39](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=2799): Create bottlenecks and compact hangout spaces (e.g. dome-shaped town hub) so players congregate instead of spreading thin.
- 2022-02-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basics-of-cframe-multiplication-and-weldsmotor6dsjoint-instances/1682079): To rotate a CFrame around world axes in one line: CFrame.Angles(Rx,Ry,Rz) * (cf - cf.Position) + cf.Position.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [23:39](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1419): Spend about a week researching real locations and building a mood board before building; take the best parts of several reference photos.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [30:51](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1851): Gray boxing: build layouts from simple gray block shapes so you can walk the space and change it cheaply before adding art.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [38:11](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=2291): Use modular building pieces to mix facades; reduces asset count and helps performance when working with many imported assets.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [1:23:17](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=4997): Hide map edges with distant buildings, mountains or floating islands; use paper-thin tall terrain for distant mountains.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487): Store effect models in ReplicatedStorage and clone them on the client so every player sees the same prebuilt effect.
- 2022-06-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/yuruzuus-open-source-vfx/1840021): Open-source VFX assets may be used free in your game; credit is appreciated but not required. Never claim you made the asset.
- 2022-07-25 Emancyphur [0:00](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=0): Add a Skybox to Lighting before adding Atmosphere, otherwise Atmosphere won't work.
- 2022-08-15 BloxianCode [0:00](https://youtu.be/iegUPV-wMng?t=0): Use a plugin that shows which side of an oval (rounded) part is its front face, to orient parts correctly when building scenes.
- 2022-11-17 XTB7 [0:18](https://youtu.be/k2p9KMrlSAA?t=18): Add an Animator object under the Humanoid before loading animations; animations only play through an Animator.
- 2022-12-08 GnomeCode [3:36](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=216): Add animation events named exactly 'footstep' with parameter 'left' or 'right' on the frames where each foot contacts the ground, so footsteps sync to the feet.
- 2022-12-08 GnomeCode [3:36](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=216): Copy the animation ID before opening the animation editor; it can wipe the clipboard, so re-copy the ID before pasting into import.
- 2022-12-08 GnomeCode [7:28](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=448): Replace the default run animation ID in the load-animations function with your own published R15 run animation ID; animations can't be shared directly between accounts.
- 2022-12-08 GnomeCode [9:03](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=543): Group similar materials (slate, brick, cobblestone) under one generic sound via the material map; fewer sound files means less data players must download.
- 2022-12-08 GnomeCode [9:03](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=543): Tune per-material volume in the footstep module (e.g. 0.3 instead of default 0.5) and footprint decay time to control how long prints persist.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Search the Toolbox audio by mood words rather than exact track names; exact names return few results or the same track everyone uses.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Filter Toolbox audio to tracks over 1 minute long to exclude sound effects, since audio search cannot exclude SFX by name.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Filter Toolbox results by creator to avoid unlabeled AI-generated DistroKid tracks; Monstercat and APM were considered safer at the time.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Use the web audio dashboard (create.roblox.com/store/audio) instead of the Toolbox, since the Toolbox settings filters were reported as broken.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Give proper credit to creators even when the license (such as CC0) does not require attribution, to encourage future permission to reuse their work.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Use TLDRLegal to get a brief summary of license conditions before using a track.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): For off-platform music, check OpenGameArt, itch.io (music tag), Incompetech (with attribution), and Freesound (filter by license) for usable tracks and SFX.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): On SoundCloud, search, select tracks, then choose 'to use commercially' under the listen-to options to limit results to commercially usable tracks; still verify the license.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): When you like a track, look up its album on an external site and browse the album, since the Toolbox album feature returned random results.
- 2023-02-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490): Use RbxMusic or AudioScape plugins to search the Roblox audio library by genre, album, artist, and length, and to see sound licenses.
- 2023-02-13 Suphi Kaner [13:52](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=832): Align HingeConstraint attachments by copying world CFrame position and rotating orientation to match the handle, otherwise the part snaps to the wrong angle.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [11:08](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=668): Use Scale for larger screens and Offset (raw pixels) for smaller screens so UI stays legible on phones.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [12:34](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=754): Add UI aspect ratio constraints so UI keeps its shape on any screen instead of stretching and distorting.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [14:12](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=852): Use Studio's device emulator to check phone and tablet layouts; verify text is legible and buttons are big enough for finger taps.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [19:43](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1183): Place main actions in bottom-corner thumb zones (right side); keep few controls on screen and show context-sensitive buttons only when needed.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [0:30](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=30): Learn UI constraints (UIAspectRatioConstraint, UISizeConstraint, etc.) instead of fixed offsets; they make UI scale across the hundreds of mobile aspect ratios.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [6:40](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=400): Prioritize readable, simple UI over ornate styling; top Roblox games use simple, functional UIs.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [7:59](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=479): Lay out buttons in a grid so controller cursor navigation is easy, and enable the Selectable property on UI buttons for console/controller support.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [8:54](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=534): Pair UIGridLayout with ScrollFrame and script the canvas size to fit contents; this makes dynamically sized grids easy.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [14:13](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=853): Keep core UI within a safe central layout that fits all screens; move overflow content like leaderboards into the 3D world on a panel the camera can reach.
- 2023-03-02 Roblox Creators [14:54](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=894): Test every UI in the device emulator to confirm it fits on different screen sizes.
- 2023-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571): Keep model quality consistent: a low-detail dagger next to a high-detail sword breaks immersion.
- 2023-03-11 GnomeCode [11:01](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=661): Avoid long flat wall surfaces; break them up with blocks, trim, columns, and small offset parts, which can triple visual detail with very few parts.
- 2023-03-11 GnomeCode [11:44](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=704): Do not rely on a single texture like brick; add subtle real-world details such as slab supports, contrasting courses and skirting boards.
- 2023-03-11 GnomeCode [13:50](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=830): Build colour palettes from two or three colours; take a base colour and slightly darken or lighten it for contrast rather than using clashing brights.
- 2023-03-11 GnomeCode [15:59](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=959): For a cartoony look, lower saturation one step from pure neon to avoid a garish, chaotic result; desaturate further for realistic or post-apocalyptic themes.
- 2023-03-11 GnomeCode [17:22](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=1042): Vary palette and lighting per map area to give each zone a distinct mood, as the Wild West map does with warm and swampy zones.
- 2023-03-16 Roblox Creators [1:27](https://youtu.be/CmXyiJw5N_Y?t=87): Use lighting and post-processing (dynamic clouds, atmospheres, post-processing effects) to lift the look without physically detailing every object; it is underused and high impact.
- 2023-04-06 Roblox Creators [0:30](https://youtu.be/uumqDp020jk?t=30): Start accessibility with visuals: ensure colours contrast with each other and avoid bad combinations like white text on yellow.
- 2023-04-06 Roblox Creators [1:10](https://youtu.be/uumqDp020jk?t=70): Make UI text large enough and placed sensibly so it is readable; tiny or oddly positioned text hurts players.
- 2023-04-06 Roblox Creators [1:35](https://youtu.be/uumqDp020jk?t=95): Choose font carefully; some fonts make text unreadable. Speaker says sans-serif is generally easier than serif and that Comic Sans is dyslexia-friendly.
- 2023-04-13 Roblox Creators [0:07](https://youtu.be/syQcaeRqqc4?t=7): Follow WCAG contrast guidelines for UI text: check foreground/background contrast with a calculator; larger text can use lower contrast.
- 2023-04-13 Roblox Creators [1:31](https://youtu.be/syQcaeRqqc4?t=91): Don't abandon your palette for contrast; tweak shades (e.g., darker blue behind white text) so contrast fits the game's look.
- 2023-04-13 Roblox Creators [2:06](https://youtu.be/syQcaeRqqc4?t=126): Add outlines or secondary colors (e.g., white outline around a circle) so key elements stand out for colorblind players without hurting aesthetics.
- 2023-04-13 Roblox Creators [4:16](https://youtu.be/syQcaeRqqc4?t=256): Red-green is not the only deficiency; use brightness differences, since greens/browns and blues/purples blend for different deficiency types.
- 2023-04-14 TheDankDeveloper [2:45](https://youtu.be/HZLsTYgFo4c?t=165): Separate mesh faces into distinct parts (select, P > Selection) so each part can be colored independently.
- 2023-04-14 TheDankDeveloper [3:27](https://youtu.be/HZLsTYgFo4c?t=207): Use UV project from view and a shared color palette image as base color texture to create gradient colors on low-poly parts.
- 2023-04-15 TheDankDeveloper [0:01](https://youtu.be/WD6xum-dptI?t=1): Export models as FBX from Blender (File > Export) to bring them into Roblox Studio.
- 2023-04-15 TheDankDeveloper [1:25](https://youtu.be/WD6xum-dptI?t=85): In Asset Manager bulk import, leave Import as One Mesh unchecked if you need the parts separately; it cannot be customized afterward.
- 2023-05-07 1Coal [2:41](https://youtu.be/JzB8fpyofnw?t=161): Give each stage a distinct color instead of uniform gray/sand to reduce boredom.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [28:53](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1733): Keep your palette but add outlines or tweak brightness to make key elements stand out for colorblind players rather than abandoning the look.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [29:34](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1774): Test colorblind legibility by viewing your UI in monochrome; if elements blend in grayscale, they will likely blend for colorblind players.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [32:59](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1979): Don't convey meaning by color alone; add symbols or icons, e.g. team markers, as Super Doomspire does.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [34:36](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2076): Provide visual alternatives for sound cues, such as captions or on-screen indicators for a ringing phone.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [35:56](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2156): Give players separate volume controls for music, effects and speech; use Roblox sound groups to make this easy to implement.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [38:00](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=2280): Include in-game settings for volume, camera shake and controls; the Split game by EgoMoose and Minecraft accessibility settings are examples.
- 2023-05-18 Roblox Creators [21:52](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1312): Make visual and audio options customizable across the board, as Minecraft does, to inspire your own accessibility settings.
- 2023-05-18 GnomeCode [10:16](https://youtu.be/TJnV2ycnXZc?t=616): Use proper humanoid rigs instead of placeholder parts (like a ball on a character) when you can; it reads as unfinished and hurts immersion.
- 2023-05-18 GnomeCode [8:18](https://youtu.be/TJnV2ycnXZc?t=498): Spamming many free models into one scene can cause heavy lag on low-end machines; keep model counts modest for performance.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [0:21](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=21): Highlight important objects with proximity prompts, billboard GUIs, or non-neon materials, varying color, pattern, or subtle animation, instead of intense red neon that can hurt light-sensitive players.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [7:30](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=450): Use UI scaling so players can enlarge all game UI without changing the world view; handles text size and readability together.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [8:12](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=492): Use bold text for readability, especially with thin fonts. Set minimum text size when using TextScaled, since longer translations like German or Spanish shrink text.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [11:52](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=712): Use clear, recognizable icons (e.g. a house icon) so players don't have to press buttons to guess what they do.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [14:48](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=888): Offer a contrast slider rather than colorblind filters, which make games look muddy; or let players swap one specific color for another.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [17:30](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=1050): Break long text into short paragraphs with blank lines between them, and keep copy succinct to reduce overwhelm for dyslexic and ADHD players.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [18:10](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=1090): Use rich text to bold or color key terms and item names so players can spot important information quickly.
- 2023-05-25 Roblox Creators [21:36](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=1296): Use images or icons when referencing UI elements so players know what button to find; describing it in text adds reading load.
- 2023-06-01 TheDankDeveloper [0:22](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=22): Add Atmosphere, Blur, ColorCorrection and SunRays under Lighting to get a cheap cartoon look without custom art.
- 2023-06-01 TheDankDeveloper [0:30](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=30): Set Blur size to 4 for the cartoon look shown in the video.
- 2023-06-01 TheDankDeveloper [0:42](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=42): Set Atmosphere density to 0.269 and pick a Skybox from the Toolbox for the sky.
- 2023-06-01 TheDankDeveloper [0:48](https://youtu.be/_bLWOWL0fCw?t=48): Set ColorCorrection brightness 0.1, contrast 0.1, saturation 0.8, and tint a nice blue for the final grade.
- 2023-06-19 SmartyRBX [3:45](https://youtu.be/74VNexodi10?t=225): Sell asset packs (models, textures, audio, animations) on stores; each sale pays repeatedly, unlike one-off commissions.
- 2023-06-19 SmartyRBX [7:07](https://youtu.be/74VNexodi10?t=427): For UGC accessories, learn Blender and apply via the DevForum; Roblox is only accepting selected creators.
- 2023-06-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-experiences-for-people-17-and-older-on-roblox/2432184): 17+ assets are not supported yet; depict mature content by combining under-17 assets, e.g. weapons with animations and audio for violence, multiple realistic blood assets for gore, red cups in animations for alcohol.
- 2023-07-10 TheDankDeveloper [0:10](https://youtu.be/fkT8nMnomb8?t=10): Keep imported assets organized in folders from the start; the creator says this keeps the project manageable.
- 2023-07-10 TheDankDeveloper [0:41](https://youtu.be/fkT8nMnomb8?t=41): Make enemies match the map's theme; a mismatched enemy style made the creator remake them.
- 2023-08-29 Suphi Kaner [11:58](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=718): Use a Trail between two attachments on a part to draw path visuals with Face Camera, light emission and width/transparency sequences.
- 2023-08-29 janisjanis01 [0:25](https://youtu.be/fRBNHaMtvFs?t=25): Polish the lobby and buildings first; new players judge quality immediately, so basic visuals make them leave within minutes.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Disable CanCollide, CanTouch and CanQuery on small, unreachable, or decorative parts (plants, windows, tools) to reduce physics cost.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Disable CastShadow on small or hard-to-see parts such as flowers and windows.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Use fewer semi-transparent, Glass, and Neon parts since they need more rendering calculations than opaque plastic.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Keep parts anchored unless physics is needed; unanchor only for the action, then re-anchor afterward.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Avoid unnecessary gradients, many open frames and glitter effects, which can reduce performance on low-end devices.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Clone pre-built template parts instead of calling Instance.new for each object when possible.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [5:16](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=316): Use clean topology and optimise each model (triangle counts) so PBR reflections look right and performance holds.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [8:45](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=525): Realistic characters and animation, not just graphics, drew older audiences; avoid overly linear or robotic animation.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [10:30](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=630): Fine-tune lighting per map, add props and wear-and-tear textures, and use PBR so surfaces catch light realistically.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [11:11](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=671): Use sound groups for automated mixing, e.g. dampen gameplay audio when health is low; dampen enemy audio behind walls.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [11:54](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=714): Polish UI and VFX: tune hit-marker jiggle, reactive sparks on metal, and feedback animations like ammo counter turning red.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [11:11](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=671): Record real references where possible; community members supplied real gun recordings used to build weapon sound variants.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [28:51](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1731): Make sure image ads are actually viewable: Roblox uses IAB viewability standards, so ads placed off-camera or far away earn zero impressions pay.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [31:16](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1876): Don't bury ads behind walls or at bad angles; a viewable ad needs most of its area visible for about a second.
- 2023-10-14 TheDankDeveloper [2:15](https://youtu.be/mwu3k4nXYZQ?t=135): Fix lighting early; the creator said a lighting problem looked much better after changing the skybox.
- 2023-10-20 B Ricey [11:12](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=672): When resizing a part in code, also reposition its pivot; Size changes do not move the PivotOffset, so compute the new pivot from half the scaled length.
- 2023-10-20 B Ricey [8:16](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=496): Orient a template part with Show Orientation Indicator and set its pivot at the back so rotations and branching happen around the joint.
- 2023-10-25 TheDankDeveloper [2:12](https://youtu.be/OR7xEm4iaO8?t=132): Add strokes or outlines to characters and decorations to make a low-effort art style look more cartoonish.
- 2023-10-29 TheDankDeveloper [0:20](https://youtu.be/K9cVA4EbbB0?t=20): Add a BillboardGui to a part, put a TextLabel inside, and set the part's transparency to 1 so the part doesn't obscure the label.
- 2023-10-29 TheDankDeveloper [0:41](https://youtu.be/K9cVA4EbbB0?t=41): Enable TextScaled on the TextLabel so text fits its frame instead of staying tiny.
- 2023-10-29 TheDankDeveloper [1:22](https://youtu.be/K9cVA4EbbB0?t=82): Use Scale, not Offset, for TextLabel and BillboardGui size (set offsets to 0) so floating text doesn't grow larger as the player moves away.
- 2023-10-29 TheDankDeveloper [2:04](https://youtu.be/K9cVA4EbbB0?t=124): Set the BillboardGui size to a fixed scale-based value (e.g. Y 3, 15.5 in the example) so it stays readable at distance.
- 2023-10-29 TheDankDeveloper [2:45](https://youtu.be/K9cVA4EbbB0?t=165): Add a UIStroke with a bigger thickness and a UIGradient (e.g. red to blue) to make floating text look nicer.
- 2023-11-26 Roox4 [1:11](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=71): Add particles and camera shake to explosions; a few minutes of VFX polish turned a dull prototype into something satisfying.
- 2023-12-30 ItsZyonic [3:48](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=228): Part size has a hard cap; build large maps by duplicating multiple parts rather than scaling one base plate.
- 2023-12-30 ItsZyonic [13:05](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=785): Use Toolbox models (e.g. trees) to dress a map quickly; scale them to fit the scene.
- 2024-01-06 B Ricey [2:47](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=167): Make a preview by cloning the block, setting Transparency 0.5, CanCollide and CanQuery false, instead of duplicating assets manually.
- 2024-01-06 B Ricey [8:21](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=501): Offset placed parts along the raycast surface Normal by half the block size to avoid sinking into ground or walls.
- 2024-01-09 TheDankDeveloper [4:27](https://youtu.be/DzFVKqdX0VY?t=267): Start from free assets and templates to build faster; the speaker says using free assets in UEFN is expected and easy.
- 2024-02-18 SmartyRBX [18:56](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=1136): Use atmosphere and lighting effects to make the game look polished, as in Anime Last Stand's distant-fog scene.
- 2024-03-02 B Ricey [2:44](https://youtu.be/SVBmZ3yIMRo?t=164): Anchor all parts in placeable models; the placement system relies on anchored objects.
- 2024-04-14 janisjanis01 [0:50](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=50): Group new map assets into folders and scatter trees and rocks with the Brush tool to fill areas quickly.
- 2024-04-14 janisjanis01 [5:10](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=310): Check pet model orientation on import; the fire dragon faced the wrong way and was fixed by rotating 90 degrees.
- 2024-04-23 Stewiepfing [2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159): Use your own custom currency icon rather than default toolbox icons; the creator says custom is better.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/viewportframe-masking/2964839): Erase vertex alpha in Blender Vertex Paint using the Erase Alpha blend mode, or run the provided script to set all vertex alpha to 0.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648): Use the Part to Terrain plugin to convert placed parts into terrain, then sculpt the landscape.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648): Gather a mood board of many reference images (e.g. in PureRef) before building to define look and feel.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648): Sketch unusual fantastical objects by hand when reference images fail to provide inspiration.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648): Speed up environment dressing with plugins: Brushtool for bulk prop placement, TerrainPainter for color and material variety, MeshVox for instant hills and mountains (sculpt down after).
- 2024-06-19 Roox4 [0:15](https://youtu.be/DPpAsjUr278?t=15): Name all parts uniquely, put animated parts in one folder, and set CanCollide to false on them so they don't interfere with other physics.
- 2024-06-23 janisjanis01 [5:48](https://youtu.be/e725ByFD8EI?t=348): Model each pet in three variants (normal, golden, darkened) to multiply content from one design.
- 2024-06-23 janisjanis01 [4:18](https://youtu.be/e725ByFD8EI?t=258): Build areas quickly by reusing an asset pack of trees, flowers and bushes, then add themed assets per area.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [2:16](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=136): Use a UIGridLayout with Scale cell size and vertical fill direction to lay out purchasable item rows; add UIPadding to stop items touching the edges.
- 2024-06-30 janisjanis01 [3:31](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=211): Save brush tool settings when building terrain; losing them made terrain look bad and forced a rebuild from old videos.
- 2024-06-30 janisjanis01 [8:18](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=498): Set each pet model's PrimaryPart correctly; the creator spent 10 minutes positioning it. Verify eggs open and pets are assigned correctly after configuring.
- 2024-06-30 janisjanis01 [9:36](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=576): Buy cheap asset packs (e.g., a $4 flower pack) to save time when production is tight.
- 2024-07-06 ByteBlox [8:07](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=487): For transparent PNGs without Canva Pro, remove the background with remove.bg, then upload as a Decal on create.roblox.com and use its asset ID.
- 2024-07-11 PolarBear [4:02](https://youtu.be/yYp23GK9RI4?t=242): Use Canva to recreate existing game logos as a portfolio piece for icon design work.
- 2024-07-19 Mulch [2:02](https://youtu.be/y9Ii0VEFaEU?t=122): Theme each map distinctly (e.g. stud/Lego look) to make maps feel fresh; he found this the most fun to build.
- 2024-07-19 Mulch [3:25](https://youtu.be/y9Ii0VEFaEU?t=205): Use a floor texture strip instead of a flag for a checkered finish line; simpler and reads clearly.
- 2024-08-17 Daiorg [2:34](https://youtu.be/Yku_51UE_GM?t=154): Use layered ambient sound (growls, whispers, water bubbling) as the main scare driver; the speaker says ambience scares more than the monsters.
- 2024-08-24 bubDEV [1:22](https://youtu.be/th2DJ7-5MnU?t=82): Remove the Noob's name tag (or set Humanoid DisplayName to none) to clean up the character before capturing it.
- 2024-08-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324): Render skybox images square at 1024 or 2048px; non-square images will not work.
- 2024-08-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324): For a simple gradient skybox, render only Top, Bottom and Front views; Front is reused for Back, Left and Right.
- 2024-08-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324): Set Blender View Transform to Standard under Color Management, otherwise skybox colours render darker or greyish.
- 2024-08-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324): Name rendered skybox images by direction (e.g. Skybox_Name_Front) to simplify assigning them to Sky properties.
- 2024-09-07 BubuTheDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/xVUqKk3OnnU?t=41): Make a script-free conveyor by setting the belt part's AssemblyLinearVelocity in Properties to the axis speed (e.g. X = -50).
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [15:04](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=904): Scale UI about 1.5x for anything players interact with (fonts, icons, buttons) since players sit 10+ feet from the TV; simplify the UI.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [16:27](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=987): Support d-pad up/down/left/right, select and back navigation; use the simplified UI so controller navigation is easy.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [29:14](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=1754): Use context-sensitive ('live') controls HUD that updates to the current action instead of static on-screen control lists, as Ultimate Football does.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [30:36](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=1836): Map buttons consistently with player expectations (A jump, B secondary, X main action) and make controls customizable.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [4:19](https://youtu.be/NEK7jq1LFu8?t=259): Use Blender live link for live-updating avatar content in Studio, glTF import, and the Avatar Preview tool to check bodies and animations before publishing.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [15:11](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=911): Insert local flavor (art, music, regional references, offline events) so the experience feels native to each market, not just translated.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [22:33](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1353): Translating text is the baseline; cultural details (landscapes, references, festivals) are what make players feel the game was made for them.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [35:49](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=2149): Keep global appeal by staying authentic to the IP rather than pandering; humor tends to translate across cultures.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [11:49](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=709): Use the texture generator to make variants of one asset (e.g. lightning and fire pillars), then have Assistant attach an attribute and script to change gameplay per variant.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [23:10](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1390): Retexturing many marketplace assets for branded updates is a major artist time sink; generative retexturing can let a small team ship more games per update cycle.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [30:08](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1808): Watch texture memory: one fully resident 1,024 SurfaceAppearance set is ~2.8 MB; 100 of them is ~300 MB of client memory.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [31:30](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1890): Don't keep audio resident on the client until it is needed; loaded sounds sit in memory.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [3:53](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=233): Start with concept art and sketches before building; sketches let you lock in details and get approvals faster than redoing built geometry.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [8:00](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=480): Draw your own reference images instead of relying on online references; it reduces revision cycles caused by inaccurate or off-style references.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [10:08](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=608): Follow four stages: concept art, graybox, art build, finished map. Graybox early to test playability before art work.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [14:14](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=854): Add your own style (personal interests, e.g. industrial architecture, concrete, organic shapes) to make the work stand out.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [18:43](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1123): Use atmosphere settings, sun rays and depth of field to separate foreground from background for a cinematic look.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [19:23](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1163): Analyze a movie still you like, then match its light direction, shadows and tone in Studio to learn cinematic lighting.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [23:35](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1415): Counterbalance a strong skybox tint (it colors everything) when colors must stay accurate, such as brand colors.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [26:19](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1579): Limit the palette to two or three colors for a softer, calmer mood; a single color reads as more dramatic.
- 2024-09-28 BloxxedDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/5cLUGex3nUY?t=0): Use a low-vertex cylinder (8 vertices) as the base for low-poly tree trunks and canopies.
- 2024-09-28 BloxxedDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/5cLUGex3nUY?t=41): Color low-poly models by UV-projecting from view and assigning a palette image as an Image Texture base color, rather than per-face colors.
- 2024-09-28 BloxxedDev [1:21](https://youtu.be/5cLUGex3nUY?t=81): If palette colors don't show in Blender, switch viewport shading from Material Preview to Solid/Viewport shading.
- 2024-09-28 BloxxedDev [2:01](https://youtu.be/5cLUGex3nUY?t=121): Shape organic silhouettes by moving individual vertices and extruding/scaling faces instead of building new primitives.
- 2024-09-28 BloxxedDev [2:44](https://youtu.be/5cLUGex3nUY?t=164): Use Ctrl+R loop cuts and scale to add a mid-section bulge so the tree looks rounder and more natural.
- 2024-09-28 BloxxedDev [3:25](https://youtu.be/5cLUGex3nUY?t=205): Export as FBX and import into Studio via View > Asset Manager; keep parts separate so you can edit them later.
- 2024-09-28 BloxxedDev [3:25](https://youtu.be/5cLUGex3nUY?t=205): Import errors reported on FBX import can usually be ignored; the mesh still imports correctly.
- 2024-10-13 Suphi Kaner [4:12](https://youtu.be/AkF-4x7qfzM?t=252): Split large rotations into several small keyframes (e.g. 72° steps applied twice) because Studio's rotation input misreads large angles.
- 2024-10-13 Suphi Kaner [35:18](https://youtu.be/AkF-4x7qfzM?t=2118): Add buffer keyframes past the start and end markers; without them the track reaches its end and stops, snapping objects back.
- 2024-10-13 Suphi Kaner [44:15](https://youtu.be/AkF-4x7qfzM?t=2655): Animation keyframes only play forward; if reversing from a point, add keyframes covering that path rather than relying on events.
- 2024-10-24 ByteBlox [1:01](https://youtu.be/dd9QvPp6HQI?t=61): ViewportFrames render any model inside them as UI, so they draw on top of the world and ignore walls; useful for unusual visual effects.
- 2024-10-24 ByteBlox [12:42](https://youtu.be/dd9QvPp6HQI?t=762): Use a basic character with no accessories for ViewportFrame cloning; accessories break the clone setup.
- 2024-11-03 janisjanis01 [17:11](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=1031): Reuse a consistent colour palette for assets; the speaker says texturing became much easier after switching to one.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [5:33](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=333): Build obby assets in Blender, texture them, then import; baking textures together was ineffective, so the speaker retextured each model.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [7:18](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=438): Keep texture palettes consistent across assets; recolouring candy assets to match the theme improved the look.
- 2024-11-17 janisjanis01 [10:29](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=629): Set PrimaryPart on pet and vehicle models so they configure and move correctly in Studio.
- 2024-11-27 Possible Panda [0:41](https://youtu.be/OxGa3kVcfMY?t=41): Avoid using free Toolbox assets in a real game; some players react badly to them. Fine for learning only.
- 2024-11-27 Possible Panda [5:00](https://youtu.be/OxGa3kVcfMY?t=300): Unanchored parts fall under physics; anchor static parts in Properties, and turn off CanCollide to make them walk-through.
- 2024-12-01 janisjanis01 [4:26](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=266): Check mesh triangle counts before importing; a 100,000+ triangle wreath would crash Studio, so reduce polygons first.
- 2024-12-15 janisjanis01 [11:36](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=696): Save your work often: a power loss forced the speaker to retexture finished pets, so commit progress frequently.
- 2024-12-15 janisjanis01 [15:16](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=916): Plan a pet list ahead and build pets in batches, grouped by area theme (candy cane, town, train station).
- 2024-12-15 janisjanis01 [7:00](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=420): Keep window parts white in Blender and colour them in Studio, then add a glow effect there.
- 2025-01-05 janisjanis01 [11:36](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=696): Test visual effects like transparency on one object before applying broadly; creator dropped the slime effect on the ground as it looked awful.
- 2025-01-05 janisjanis01 [12:00](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=720): Use slight transparency on jelly-themed pets and add face textures in Studio to make them read as jelly.
- 2025-01-05 ByteBlox [8:21](https://youtu.be/K-KvVwt_Y4I?t=501): Store default and alternate Lighting/Atmosphere presets in ReplicatedStorage so you can clone them in and restore them at runtime.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Convert Unions to MeshParts via Export Selection, since Roblox optimizes MeshParts better; resize and reposition after.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Set CollisionFidelity to Box on non-collidable MeshParts and Unions, but not for parts used with CanTouch.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Avoid DoubleSided and PreciseConvexDecomposition on MeshParts; they are not optimized.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Keep textures and decals around 512x512; keep decals under 500x500 for mobile players.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Prefer SurfaceAppearance or custom materials over Textures, since Textures are checked on all six faces and hurt mobile.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Disable shadows on very small parts and on parts players rarely see.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Reuse meshes and be careful changing transparency or color on shared meshes.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Compress images before upload (e.g. imagecompressor.com) to cut texture size.
- 2025-01-10 PineappleDev [1:19](https://youtu.be/hHKQEohHqwU?t=79): Set Camera.CameraType to Scriptable before driving the camera in RenderStepped, and hide your own body parts and decals (Transparency = 1) for first person.
- 2025-01-13 BloxxedDev [0:10](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=10): Save the place first and build terrain with the Terrain Editor draw tool; shape a messy oval, add an offset second layer, then smooth it for a natural look.
- 2025-01-13 BloxxedDev [2:13](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=133): Add water to terrain for realistic lighting and reflections; fill it about halfway up the terrain and scale it to fit so it doesn't stick out.
- 2025-01-13 BloxxedDev [3:03](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=183): Set water transparency high and tint it yellow or orange to match the sky; a solid default colour looks cartoonish.
- 2025-01-13 BloxxedDev [3:34](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=214): Pick orange or yellow toolbox skies, which look more like sunrise or sunset and read as more realistic.
- 2025-01-13 BloxxedDev [4:15](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=255): Use the Celestial Dragger plugin to position the sun lower so it lights the scene with shadows without blowing out the view.
- 2025-01-13 BloxxedDev [4:56](https://youtu.be/xGXCApkkkCE?t=296): Reduce the sun ray intensity after playtesting; the first setting was too bright and blinded the player.
- 2025-01-15 CallMeDogey [2:34](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=154): Free Toolbox assets can carry a map; he built a 99%-free-asset map and still earned money without scripting or building skill.
- 2025-01-24 PineappleDev [8:40](https://youtu.be/2HRX5AqBfNA?t=520): Group viewmodel meshes under a Model named exactly like the Tool, with a part called Handle; anchor only the Handle and weld everything else to it.
- 2025-01-24 PineappleDev [9:21](https://youtu.be/2HRX5AqBfNA?t=561): Set the viewmodel's PrimaryPart to the Handle, and store a CFrame 'offset' attribute on the model so you can tune position live in-game.
- 2025-01-24 PineappleDev [10:02](https://youtu.be/2HRX5AqBfNA?t=602): Tune viewmodel offset by changing the attribute while playtesting; fix backwards orientation by adjusting the offset's rotation.
- 2025-01-24 PineappleDev [6:33](https://youtu.be/2HRX5AqBfNA?t=393): Set LocalTransparencyModifier to 1 on the real tool's BaseParts so the world tool is hidden for the holder while the viewmodel shows.
- 2025-01-24 ByteBlox [21:16](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=1276): Use TweenService with repeat count -1 and random durations for rotating obstacles to add visual variation cheaply.
- 2025-01-27 ByteBlox [1:47](https://youtu.be/RkgxOf3iZfA?t=107): Fix accessories/avatar scaling by using a StarterCharacter in StarterPlayer, then set body scale values there (e.g. 0.5) to shrink players.
- 2025-01-29 JustDucksey [2:51](https://youtu.be/jKyzG9HPrRM?t=171): Welding parts to tween models is awkward on Roblox; use welds/constraints and animate the assembled tool rather than tweening individual models.
- 2025-02-06 ArTheDev [2:20](https://youtu.be/JtZ8RzQaQco?t=140): Put the ScreenGui in StarterGui; it gets cloned into PlayerGui for each player on join, so access it from PlayerGui.
- 2025-02-09 Quez [0:42](https://youtu.be/hCW2OhUBPf4?t=42): Scale up a small model and stamp it out to create hills and terrain variation instead of flat ground.
- 2025-02-09 Quez [2:05](https://youtu.be/hCW2OhUBPf4?t=125): Use the Archimedes plugin to generate paths; the speaker says it is far faster than building paths manually.
- 2025-02-09 Quez [2:45](https://youtu.be/hCW2OhUBPf4?t=165): Use the Brush tool plugin: select parts, activate brush, click across the map to scatter natural-looking duplicates with variation.
- 2025-02-09 Quez [3:26](https://youtu.be/hCW2OhUBPf4?t=206): Increase the Brush radius to spawn foliage in bunches rather than one at a time.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [8:20](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=500): Keep interactive highlights from hiding the object; avoid rooms so dark players get lost.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [12:50](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=770): Invest in a strong lobby as a statement of intent; players judge game quality from it.
- 2025-02-22 GnomeCode [12:50](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=770): Use in-game documents and notes to deliver story and guidance more immersively than plain prompts.
- 2025-03-09 BloxxedDev [1:31](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=91): Use free-model asset packs for background scenery like trees to save build time when art skill is limited.
- 2025-03-09 BloxxedDev [6:53](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=413): Improve visual quality cheaply with lighting, blur and a more realistic grass material before adding content.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [2:35](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=155): Color trees and models in Studio rather than in Blender; it is easier to adjust colors there.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [8:05](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=485): Build one base crate model and reskin variants to produce many crate designs quickly.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [15:06](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=906): Use reflective materials sparingly; they look good from one angle but poor from others.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [17:25](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=1045): Use PBR textures to make exclusive or premium items stand out.
- 2025-03-21 PineappleDev [1:03](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=63): Put new item models in an Objects folder in ReplicatedStorage by duplicating an existing item and editing it in Workspace.
- 2025-03-21 PineappleDev [1:03](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=63): Each pickable model must contain a child named 'pickup' or the system won't detect it as grabbable.
- 2025-03-21 PineappleDev [1:03](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=63): Weld all parts of a multi-part item together, parent them directly to the model, and keep them unanchored, or the item falls apart and won't work.
- 2025-03-22 AlvinBlox [5:35](https://youtu.be/dtRGRx-2WNg?t=335): Sell assets that solve a pain point (environment packs, UI kits) since they can be created once and sold repeatedly.
- 2025-03-23 janisjanis01 [0:33](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=33): Use 3D-modeled consumables instead of plain icons for item interactions; the creator found click-to-use icons boring and modeled snacks in Blender.
- 2025-03-23 janisjanis01 [3:46](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=226): Roblox lacks emissive textures; simulate glowing neon by setting the part material to Neon and making color variants.
- 2025-03-23 janisjanis01 [1:14](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=74): Texture stylised props in Blender; use Substance Painter for logo and text decals on detailed items.
- 2025-03-23 janisjanis01 [5:00](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=300): Use a limited colour palette to make liquid models read well instead of leaving them bland.
- 2025-03-23 janisjanis01 [5:43](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=343): Mesh problems can make models hard to build; plan for extra modeling time on complex shapes.
- 2025-03-29 PineappleDev [0:31](https://youtu.be/ntHgQBEVERE?t=31): Use the carry/weld plugin from the description via Insert Model; move the setup folders into ReplicatedStorage, StarterPlayerScripts, Workspace and ServerScriptService as instructed.
- 2025-03-29 PineappleDev [1:27](https://youtu.be/ntHgQBEVERE?t=87): Build new items as a Model with all parts parented directly to it, set a PrimaryPart, unanchor parts and weld them to the primary part before storing in ReplicatedStorage.
- 2025-03-29 PineappleDev [2:49](https://youtu.be/ntHgQBEVERE?t=169): Keep the server-owner BuValue inside item models; do not remove it.
- 2025-03-29 BloxxedDev [2:46](https://youtu.be/y5aNv16bT3s?t=166): Generate meshes with Meshy from text or image prompts; an image reference gave a more usable jar model than text alone.
- 2025-03-29 BloxxedDev [0:43](https://youtu.be/y5aNv16bT3s?t=43): Use placeholder primitives (Parts) for NPCs and models first, then swap in finished assets later.
- 2025-03-30 janisjanis01 [4:14](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=254): Texture models in Substance Painter for higher quality; it takes longer than Blender texturing but looks noticeably better.
- 2025-03-30 janisjanis01 [6:03](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=363): Texture repeated items (e.g. multiple coins) once and reuse the material instead of texturing each instance.
- 2025-03-30 janisjanis01 [4:14](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=254): Batch gloves in sets matching a crate (12 gloves for 4 crates) to keep the content pipeline organised.
- 2025-03-30 janisjanis01 [6:38](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=398): AI-generated design ideas were low quality for this creator; hand-designing unique cosmetic concepts gave better results.
- 2025-03-30 janisjanis01 [7:18](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=438): Check for smooth-shading modifier side effects on every model; applying it to one wall model caused glossy artifacts elsewhere.
- 2025-04-10 JustDucksey [1:23](https://youtu.be/BbNlDGBROdg?t=83): Test whether models visually communicate function (e.g. wheels appear to turn); a plain static model may need a simple texture or animation to read as moving.
- 2025-04-10 JustDucksey [1:23](https://youtu.be/BbNlDGBROdg?t=83): Keep asset style consistent: choose one visual style (here bubbly/plastic) and make all parts match so the game looks cohesive.
- 2025-04-10 JustDucksey [3:29](https://youtu.be/BbNlDGBROdg?t=209): Create multiple variants of key parts (e.g. basic and gold engines) to give players progression and collection, planned across all parts.
- 2025-04-10 BloxxedDev [2:45](https://youtu.be/RVZBjAUzf7o?t=165): Use a texture plugin (Add Easy Texture) to apply a consistent material to many parts quickly, and match path colors to surrounding terrain.
- 2025-04-27 Stewiepfing [8:50](https://youtu.be/e48wgHQll2Y?t=530): Apply UIAspectRatioConstraint to frames and images so shapes keep their proportions across phone, tablet and PC resolutions.
- 2025-05-02 Duckable [4:19](https://youtu.be/Wc8wjiy1oYk?t=259): Use an animation plugin like Moon Animator 2 instead of Roblox's built-in Animator, which the speaker found much slower to work with.
- 2025-05-05 FrenkyRBLX [0:00](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=0): UGC items must stay under 4,000 tris; check the Statistics overlay in Studio/Blender to see the count.
- 2025-05-05 FrenkyRBLX [1:33](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=93): Dissolve edges lying on flat surfaces (Alt-click edge, X, Dissolve Edges) to reduce polycount without changing shape.
- 2025-05-05 FrenkyRBLX [4:45](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=285): Delete hidden faces that players never see, such as parts poking inside other geometry, using X-ray and face select.
- 2025-05-05 FrenkyRBLX [7:17](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=437): Avoid the Decimate modifier; it badly damages topology. Use only as last resort when tris must drop fast.
- 2025-05-05 FrenkyRBLX [8:37](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=517): Use Merge by Distance (vertex select, A, M) to clean up very close vertices; often removes many vertices unnoticed.
- 2025-05-21 JustDucksey [1:22](https://youtu.be/qVkYbVHWI1I?t=82): Name crop growth stages exactly as STAGE_1, STAGE_2 etc. and weld all parts to an anchored main part so the model grows correctly.
- 2025-05-21 JustDucksey [4:45](https://youtu.be/qVkYbVHWI1I?t=285): Set each crop model's pivot to its bottom using Model > Edit Pivot so it grows up from the ground.
- 2025-05-21 JustDucksey [2:43](https://youtu.be/qVkYbVHWI1I?t=163): For regrowable plants, add a SpawnPositions folder of invisible parts marking where crops can grow; the crop needs default value, growth type, and is full attributes.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [11:32](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=692): Generate reference images in an AI image tool, then image-to-3D, then remesh to a low poly count before importing to Roblox; drop the texture and retexture in Studio.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [7:02](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=422): Check map generic-ness early; Dev 4 noted the map looked generic and changed the setting to fit the theme.
- 2025-05-30 Arow [0:40](https://youtu.be/o4YPlpYdToo?t=40): Shoulder accessories are a UGC category; upload via Studio as avatar accessory, set creator to a group, then publish.
- 2025-06-07 BloxxedDev [3:25](https://youtu.be/Gk-iTN8Ru_Q?t=205): Drive visual state (color) from growth progress with a single base color plus an impact multiplier, so one parameter controls how much growth changes appearance.
- 2025-06-14 JustDucksey [1:24](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=84): Enable smoke/particle effects only while the vehicle is moving so the visual feedback matches the state.
- 2025-06-26 RoBuilder [0:53](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=53): Use reference images when building; the streamer calls them the key tip for newer builders.
- 2025-06-26 RoBuilder [43:11](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=2591): Model props in Blender and build layouts in Studio; import props into Studio from Blender rather than round-tripping back.
- 2025-06-26 RoBuilder [2:00:47](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=7247): For repeating parking-line markings, use a stretchable texture with one line on the canvas edge instead of a plugin.
- 2025-06-26 RoBuilder [2:00:47](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=7247): Use unions for flat floor pieces to hide Z-fighting; collision issues are unlikely on floor-level geometry.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [4:47](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=287): Keep flower petals as low-poly as possible; petals use most of the UGC triangle budget. Start from a basic plane shape and add geometry only as needed.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [6:08](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=368): Unwrap UVs early, before subdivision and final edits, because unwrapping later is much harder.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [10:07](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=607): Use M to move objects into collections for organisation only. Use Ctrl+J to actually join meshes, since each exported model must be one mesh.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [11:29](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=689): Keep bulb and petals as separate objects until late in the process; joining them early makes later edits messy.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [16:17](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=977): Build models centred at the scene origin (Shift+S, cursor to selected) so transforms and rotations behave predictably.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [18:20](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=1100): Set origin to Volume (Object > Set Origin) so rotation pivots at the model centre. Use Ctrl+A to apply location if you moved it in object mode.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [31:37](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=1897): Track triangles with the Statistics overlay. A 12-triangle petal times five petals is about 60 triangles; hats have a 4,000-triangle limit.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [33:12](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=1992): Check for missing faces by enabling back face culling in the viewport overlay options, and flip normals (Alt+N) if petals show only one side.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [36:32](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=2192): After auto smooth, mark hard edges as sharp (Ctrl+E > Mark Sharp) so flat petal edges stay crisp rather than shading oddly.
- 2025-07-09 Roblox Creators [49:53](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=2993): Unwrap all parts into one UV layout with room left for the ring. You can start texturing from there, before the crown band is modeled.
- 2025-07-16 ArTheDev [0:50](https://youtu.be/csxJTfPVW8U?t=50): Set animation priority to Action and rig to R6 or R15 in game settings before animating, so the rig matches the animations you publish.
- 2025-07-16 ArTheDev [3:41](https://youtu.be/csxJTfPVW8U?t=221): Publish animations via Animation Editor > publish to Roblox, then copy the asset ID to load them in code.
- 2025-07-25 Crusherfire [13:42](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=822): Downscale textures to 512x512 where possible, use texture atlases and trim sheets; 1024 uses 4x the graphics memory of 512.
- 2025-07-25 Crusherfire [21:31](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=1291): Reuse identical mesh IDs: meshes imported with separate IDs (duplicate trees, doors, tires) break batching into separate draw calls. Shipping container draw calls fell 50% after fixing.
- 2025-07-27 janisjanis01 [9:23](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=563): If a contracted modeler quits, creator modeled crops in Blender to save money; a rushed model shipped with exclusive seeds not loading.
- 2025-07-28 SimTek Game Development [1:17:39](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=4659): Keep animation priority set to Movement and tighten keyframe spacing to speed up an animation rather than changing speed in code.
- 2025-07-28 SimTek Game Development [4:09](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=249): Make the first and last keyframes identical so looping animations transition smoothly.
- 2025-07-28 SimTek Game Development [2:03](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=123): Remove default body colors from rigs and add new ones so the NPC is not moderated for having no clothing.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [4:34](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=274): Manually position array elements along a curve; tinkering is the normal workflow. Use fit-to-curve while positioning, then switch back to fixed count and lower it until gaps close.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [9:48](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=588): Apply array and curve modifiers before joining parts. Unapplied curves cause pieces to fly off into space after joining.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [10:29](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=629): Order modifiers by trial and error; the mirror modifier should almost always sit at the top of the stack.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [15:02](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=902): Position accessories on a reference avatar from Creator Hub (Woman 2.0 or Rthro-style body) rather than a single body; Woman 2.0 has a real neck, blocky has none.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [17:56](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=1076): Export a first test FBX early; scale mismatches are fixable and exporting gives real feedback on how Roblox will import the item.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [19:18](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=1158): Metalness at 100 in Blender does not export to Roblox accessories; approximate metallic looks by hand-painting texture in Photoshop.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [43:13](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=2593): Mark seams on hard edges (about 45-90 degree angles), unwrap by angle-based, and keep UV islands at least one square away from borders.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [46:51](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=2811): Give the most visible UV area to the front of the item; bigger UV islands hold more texture detail.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [49:04](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=2944): Texture in layers: flat base colors first, then multiply for shadows and overlay/soft light for highlights; use 0% hardness brushes and 20-30% smudge strength.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [1:00:22](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=3622): Push shadows and highlights more intensely on the focal piece; keep highlights smaller and sharper than shadows for gold and metal looks.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [1:03:05](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=3785): Test textures in Blender with flat shading and shadows/cavities enabled to approximate how the item will look in Roblox before uploading.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [1:05:32](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=3932): Export FBX with only selected objects, FBX unit scale, and uncheck Upload to Roblox. Apply smooth-by-angle before export if you want smooth shading.
- 2025-08-02 BloxxedDev [3:17](https://youtu.be/od95x-afmBU?t=197): Check imported map and model contents for hidden scripts and oversized geometry before placing them; large models caused lag and blocked paths.
- 2025-08-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566): Set ImageLabel ResampleMode to Pixelated to avoid blurry renders at lower canvas resolutions.
- 2025-08-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566): Store textures as raw RGBA byte buffers in a ModuleScript and convert large string data to a ModuleScript; opening raw bitmap modules directly can crash Studio.
- 2025-08-08 Xaiyne [0:51](https://youtu.be/aqv5bLmqbzc?t=51): Use a free R15 dummy from Toolbox that has no scripts to avoid malicious code; check reviews first.
- 2025-08-10 Stewiepfing [2:18](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=138): Store pet info as a data table with name, rarity, add-on and generic keys; the script checks for add-on keys and falls back to generic text.
- 2025-08-10 Stewiepfing [5:28](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=328): Format numbers in the thousands (5,000+) into compact form like 1k so they are readable; unformatted large numbers are hard for players to read.
- 2025-08-10 Stewiepfing [12:00](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=720): When building gradients, always set a transparency sequence and end the color sequence with the starting color so the animated gradient loops without clipping.
- 2025-08-10 Stewiepfing [15:02](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=902): Drive orb effects with clamped elapsed time divided by duration and bezier curves; disconnect and tween parts out when the clamp reaches 1.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [5:41](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=341): Name layered clothing meshes exactly: MeshName_InnerCage, MeshName_OuterCage, and armature as 'Armature'. Names like 'tank top.00002' break the cage lookup.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [6:22](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=382): For FBX export of layered clothing, use Selected Objects only and unit scale; Blender defaults are otherwise fine.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [8:24](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=504): Use Avatar > Accessory Fitting Tool in Studio to convert a mesh into layered clothing, instead of an old plugin's Create Accessory flow.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [11:08](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=668): If the neck looks see-through, turn on Back Face Culling in Blender's Solid mode to find flipped normals, select them, and flip (Shift+N).
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [13:48](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=828): Keep layered clothing under the triangle limit, stated as 4,000. Remove extra subdivision modifiers; the mesh dropped from 51,136 to 3,196 triangles.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [15:12](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=912): Auto smooth the mesh at 180 degrees to fix odd shading on top areas.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [17:01](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=1021): Apply the subdivision modifier, then mark edges sharp (Alt+E menu) to get crisp seams and collars.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [20:18](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=1218): Unwrap UVs before adding faces or waterproofing, or new faces will be uncolored in the texture; hidden inner faces matter little.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [26:32](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=1592): To keep text from warping, keep the UV straight and grid-aligned in the text area; Substance Painter lets you paint text directly.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [28:30](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=1710): Add detail with a normal map instead of geometry: make a high-contrast copy of the color map and run it through an online normal map generator.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [35:23](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=2123): Make a grayscale metallic map (white = shiny, black = matte) and invert it for roughness; set normal and metallic maps to Non-Color.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [36:04](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=2164): Keep texture maps at 1024 or below; Roblox auto-downscales high-res item textures.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [43:03](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=2583): Use Photoshop's Spot Healing Brush to remove unwanted text or logos from textures.
- 2025-08-12 Roblox Creators [43:18](https://youtu.be/xU3ikvnA5OM?t=2598): Upload texture maps from a file with Asset Manager access, then insert a Surface Appearance and paste the color, normal, and metallic maps.
