- 2025-08-15 Roblox Creators [7:12](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=432): Judge quality on texture and model. Add real-world imperfections like asymmetry, dents and folds, and think about physics.
- 2025-08-20 FrenkyRBLX [0:13](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=13): Export the R6 block avatar dummy from Studio via Avatar > Rig Builder and save-export selection as OBJ to use as a fitting guide in Blender.
- 2025-08-20 FrenkyRBLX [18:08](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1088): Convert curves to mesh, join parts, and merge verts at center so the hair has fewer tris and no holes before publishing.
- 2025-08-20 FrenkyRBLX [24:29](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1469): Keep UGC hair under 4,000 triangles; check count via Overlays > Statistics and dissolve edges on flat surfaces to reduce it.
- 2025-08-20 FrenkyRBLX [23:38](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1418): Disable the mirror modifier while building one half, then apply it after merging to halve the work.
- 2025-08-20 FrenkyRBLX [29:13](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1753): Enable Face Orientation overlay to find inverted normals (red faces), then fix with Shift+N and recalculate.
- 2025-08-20 FrenkyRBLX [30:50](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1850): If the hair texture appears flipped on the mesh, rotate all UVs 90 degrees in the UV editor.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [8:10](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=490): Keep UI modular: one top-level UI initializer module in ReplicatedStorage, with folders per interface type (menus, HUD) and one module per feature exposing an init function.
- 2025-09-07 #7Phantom [3:37](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=217): Match color on a flat-tone layer first: add a little magenta via color balance and adjust saturation so pieces share one brightness.
- 2025-09-07 #7Phantom [4:17](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=257): For clone stamp blending, set brush opacity around 30-39% and hardness 100 so strokes blend partially instead of hard-copying.
- 2025-09-07 #7Phantom [3:00](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=180): Pull in textured pieces from the reference to avoid flat-looking areas; the creator says flat shading reads as unrealistic in photobashing.
- 2025-09-07 #7Phantom [9:19](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=559): Clone stamping degrades image quality; add a little noise afterward to restore texture.
- 2025-09-07 #7Phantom [9:19](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=559): Shade and match lighting across pieces so brightness doesn't jump between sides; add shadows for realism.
- 2025-09-07 #7Phantom [8:38](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=518): Overlay paid or free texture asset packs (e.g. Char's void) on overlay blend mode for pants detail; test before committing, as converting can cause color/lighting problems.
- 2025-09-07 #7Phantom [0:55](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=55): Low-resolution reference images produce poor final quality; choose higher-quality references before starting.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [10:50](https://youtu.be/3h25HUtBHFk?t=650): Use the bounding-box option when generating 3D models over gray-box placeholders so output matches the intended footprint and aspect ratio.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [12:12](https://youtu.be/3h25HUtBHFk?t=732): Generate simple repeated props (crates, lily pads, flowers) with AI and reserve artist time for hero assets.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [9:45](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=585): Build each UI element responsive from the start: use scale properties (e.g. 0.8 icon scale) plus UIAspectRatioConstraint to keep square items.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [10:27](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=627): Use UIListLayout or the new flex properties so repeated items line up with consistent padding at any screen size or item count.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [13:39](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=819): Swap UI style tags instead of duplicating instances to rearrange HUD per orientation; style position and anchor point via the style editor.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [14:06](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=846): Use the viewport display size API (small/medium/large) to decide content: expand minimap over viewport on large screens, fullscreen it on small ones.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [22:18](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1338): Use TextScaled plus UITextSizeConstraint (min/max) so text stays legible on mobile without becoming oversized on monitors.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [23:01](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1381): Use UISizeConstraint to enlarge hit targets on touch screens and allow smaller ones on mouse, keeping them easy to hit on every device.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [23:43](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1423): Define themes with the style system to support light/dark mode, seasonal event theming and high-contrast accessibility packs.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [6:20](https://youtu.be/Tese1QLD3nU?t=380): Use PBR (roughness and metal properties) for avatar accessories to make them look realistic; PBR items can be published alongside non-PBR ones.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [23:55](https://youtu.be/Tese1QLD3nU?t=1435): Makeup UGC (planned early next year) will use Maya/Blender templates with paintable regions (face, eye, lip); max texture size 1024x1024.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [0:42](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=42): Use avatar settings such as uniform collision boxes and consistent scaling to keep competitive play level without stripping player identity.
- 2025-10-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940): Build structures in stages: visual references, then blockout, then shapes, then details. Keep part, vertex and instance counts low for mobile performance.
- 2025-11-02 janisjanis01 [9:33](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=573): Anchor spawned models or they keep falling over; forgetting to anchor was a bug the creator hit.
- 2025-11-10 Creators on the Blox [51:27](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=3087): Roblox's built-in default avatar and easy model import make content quick to produce, which Zeal cites as a reason to stay on the platform.
- 2025-11-23 janisjanis01 [6:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=376): Handle chopping/animations on the client to avoid lag for responsive interactions.
- 2025-12-02 TheDankDeveloper [5:03](https://youtu.be/D6Ej1CgiWOc?t=303): Use simple, low-cost assets (free lighting pack, easy texture plugin) to make a 2D-style game look much better quickly.
- 2025-12-02 TheDankDeveloper [7:14](https://youtu.be/D6Ej1CgiWOc?t=434): Add a highlight with fill transparency 1 to hazards to replicate Geometry Dash's outlined spike look cheaply.
- 2025-12-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855): Use texture atlases only when textures appear together on batchable parts; one high-res texture in an atlas streams the whole atlas at that mip level.
- 2025-12-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855): Prefer individual unique textures over atlases for non-batchable parts so texture streaming can load mips independently.
- 2025-12-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855): Keep near-uniform texel density across UV-mapped meshes so the mip selection heuristic produces the best results.
- 2025-12-13 janisjanis01 [4:00](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=240): Model assets in Blender only when needed; color simple props directly in Studio because it is faster.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [33:52](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=2032): Simple part-based art runs on all devices and reaches a wider audience; performance matters more than graphical polish for reach.
- 2025-12-24 FrenkyRBLX [0:57](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=57): Lower curve resolution on hair strands (Curve Properties, resolution ~12 down to ~6) to cut triangles roughly from 10,000 to 5,000.
- 2025-12-24 FrenkyRBLX [1:38](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=98): Convert hair curves to mesh, join strands, and merge vertices by center so no gaps remain in the hair mesh.
- 2025-12-24 FrenkyRBLX [2:20](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=140): Dissolve edges that cannot be merged (Alt-select edge, Ctrl+X) to reduce triangle count.
- 2025-12-24 FrenkyRBLX [4:09](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=249): Dissolve edges on flat, straight surfaces that add no topology value; repeat across the mesh to drop under 4,000 triangles.
- 2025-12-24 FrenkyRBLX [4:49](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=289): Use G-G to slide edges so strands look smoother and more natural after dissolving edges.
- 2025-12-24 FrenkyRBLX [5:51](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=351): Delete back faces of hair parts that are hidden under the head and never visible in game to save triangles.
- 2025-12-24 FrenkyRBLX [6:47](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=407): Select all, press M, Merge by Distance to remove hidden overlapping geometry; this removed about 80 triangles in the example.
- 2025-12-24 FrenkyRBLX [7:31](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=451): Keep the UGC accessory under 4,000 triangles, the limit to upload to the Roblox marketplace.
- 2026-02-08 janisjanis01 [4:21](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=261): Create unique art assets (balloons, blimps, bosses) in Blender and texture them to differentiate from stud-style templates.
- 2026-02-25 Roblox [20:07](https://youtu.be/8Djgx0jEyfs?t=1207): Use 3D upsampling on primitive-based builds: keep simple parts for simulation performance and let the upres step add visual detail, with the developer choosing the style.
- 2026-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-we-are-improving-home-this-year/4502571): Keep gameplay videos simple: straightforward recordings of the core loop with minimal editing; curated highlight reels are fine if every shown part exists in the game. In-game UI is acceptable.
- 2026-03-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374): Use watertight (closed, non-self-intersecting) meshes for CSG operations; non-watertight meshes will not work with solid modeling APIs.
- 2026-03-14 DitchyDevelopments [4:10](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=250): Build a personal asset pack instead of relying on the toolbox to keep maps original and consistent in style.
- 2026-03-14 Creators on the Blox [22:33](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=1353): Keep modeling topology clean; messy topology from sculpting caused poor performance and criticism from other artists.
- 2026-03-14 Creators on the Blox [24:37](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=1477): Pull reference images from real-world and game references (e.g., Resident Evil, Village); check references for AI-generated images before modeling.
- 2026-03-17 Xaiyne [1:58](https://youtu.be/O5FjkMlRPPI?t=118): Use an R15 rig in Studio's Avatar > Animation editor; pick a rig whose front/back colours are visible so limbs aren't misposed.
- 2026-03-17 Xaiyne [4:02](https://youtu.be/O5FjkMlRPPI?t=242): Make emote animations loop: keyframe a start pose, a mid-point pose, copy the first keyframe to the end so motion returns smoothly.
- 2026-03-17 Xaiyne [5:26](https://youtu.be/O5FjkMlRPPI?t=326): Switch keyframes to curved interpolation so motion eases in and out instead of looking robotic; compare with Play before saving.
- 2026-03-18 VuukStudios [10:54](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=654): Build maps by hand from Toolbox studded parts (recolour, scale, clone); the speaker estimates a basic map takes 1-2 hours.
- 2026-03-18 DudeGuy [0:29](https://youtu.be/k3id70yCMRY?t=29): Mix exponential-in and exponential-out easing: move object halfway with exponential in, then copy the frame and finish with exponential out for smoother motion.
- 2026-03-18 DudeGuy [4:53](https://youtu.be/k3id70yCMRY?t=293): Custom decals must be 800x800 px with transparent background; upload via Creations > Development Items > Decals, then paste the asset ID into My Movie's search.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [22:22](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=1342): Give each character a distinct gait and motive; reusing the same idle/walk across characters makes them feel generic. Work closely with designers.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [24:28](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=1468): Roblox rigs have no animated face, so convey emotion through body movement and posture.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [27:10](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=1630): Learn your character's vision before animating: know how it should move and what performance it needs, then move it from point A to B.
- 2026-03-29 Stewiepfing [8:29](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=509): Bind camera updates with RunService:BindToRenderStep at priority Camera.Value + 1 so your code overrides Roblox's default camera.
- 2026-03-29 Stewiepfing [11:39](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=699): Use a quadratic Bezier curve (start, control, end points) with elapsed-time alpha clamped 0-1 to move parts along smooth arcs.
- 2026-03-29 Stewiepfing [16:11](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=971): Pass alpha through an easing function (e.g. easeInCubic from easings.net) before feeding it to lerp or Bezier to change motion feel.
- 2026-04-11 Rool-Studio [4:17](https://youtu.be/WI6adMt2aUY?t=257): When importing an OBJ into Blender, enable 'split by group' so body parts and accessories can be separated and deleted individually.
- 2026-04-11 Rool-Studio [4:36](https://youtu.be/WI6adMt2aUY?t=276): Attach UGC accessories to rig bones by selecting the item, shift-selecting the bone in pose mode, then Ctrl+P and choose Bone.
- 2026-04-11 TheDankDeveloper [1:24](https://youtu.be/i5sldtdF8m0?t=84): Import generated PNGs via Studio Window > Asset Manager, copy the asset ID, and set image scaling to Crop to avoid stretching.
- 2026-04-11 TheDankDeveloper [2:46](https://youtu.be/i5sldtdF8m0?t=166): Replace a UI icon across all frames after checking it in-game; the creator found the AI often did not fully apply requested changes, so verify visually.
- 2026-04-12 Stewiepfing [0:15](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=15): Use Easy Visuals gradient and stroke classes to animate text and UI strokes instead of hand-scripting tweens; set offset speed, rotation, and transparency sequence explicitly.
- 2026-04-16 Roblox [22:25](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=1345): Build reusable parametric smart objects (e.g. trees, Ferris wheels) and vary parameters instead of baking thousands of unique meshes and textures.
- 2026-04-16 Roblox [15:10](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=910): Pull assets from Creator Store and choose a consistent art style (blocky vs photoreal) early, since the agent asks for it during planning.
- 2026-04-17 Roblox Creators [2:07](https://youtu.be/qNocmxFyfxs?t=127): When importing, set Creator to 'me' in Studio; assets listed under a community cannot be sold on the Creator Store.
- 2026-04-17 Roblox Creators [3:12](https://youtu.be/qNocmxFyfxs?t=192): Organise packs into folders Workspace, Server Storage and Server Script Storage, and include a ReadMe script with setup instructions for buyers.
- 2026-04-17 Roblox Creators [5:37](https://youtu.be/qNocmxFyfxs?t=337): Set up a Roblox preview experience so buyers can try the asset as it works in the engine before purchasing.
- 2026-04-18 ArTheDev [4:24](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=264): Put unit models in a WorldModel inside the viewport template so idle animations play in the inventory preview.
- 2026-04-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056): Enable via File → Beta Features → 'Edit-Time Procedural Models', then restart Studio. Insert Object → ProceduralModel gives a template.
- 2026-04-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056): Write OnGenerate to only write into targetContainer; never modify the DataModel directly, so undo, Team Create and Dragger integration work.
- 2026-04-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056): Generate geometry around the origin at the given Size; the system applies PivotTo/ScaleTo positioning automatically.
- 2026-04-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056): To make bespoke manual edits, finalize parameters then clear the Generator property to bake the result; otherwise edits to the Generated folder are overwritten.
- 2026-04-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056): Doubling Size regenerates with twice the extent, while doubling Scale uniformly scales the existing results; choose based on whether details should stay constant.
- 2026-05-10 janisjanis01 [3:04](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=184): Use AI 3D tools like Meshy.ai plus Blender to quickly create scenery assets such as hills and leaderboards for a better-looking map.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Pick the lowest CollisionFidelity that works (Box < Hull < Default < PreciseConvexDecomposition); disabling CanCollide is cheapest.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Set RenderFidelity to Automatic or Performance for distant MeshParts; Automatic drops detail past 250 and 500 studs.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Shadows are very expensive; disable CastShadow on small objects where shadows aren't needed.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Keep transparency at only 0 or 1; partial transparency causes GPU overdraw.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Prefer Roblox built-in materials over custom textures; they are already on the device and save memory and load time. Use SmoothPlastic for best performance.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Don't re-import existing textures or meshes; duplicates are treated as separate and waste memory and load time.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Reduce triangles to what each object needs, and merge static models into single assets near the end of development.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Use one main light for game lighting rather than many overlapping lights, which add shadow and overlap calculation cost.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Size images and audio to need: no 4K textures for small objects, no maximum-quality audio for small ambient sounds.
- 2026-05-25 TheDankDeveloper [5:32](https://youtu.be/uYuiCJrAcmI?t=332): Reuse existing UI templates (copy from public prompts or a UI kit) and restyle them instead of building UI from scratch.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [2:02](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=122): Use Blender for models and Substance Painter for textures; cheap texturing workflow keeps turret art consistent.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [9:25](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=565): Check welds after import; misWelded parts (like a hornet model) can disappear in play.
- 2026-06-10 Deconstructor of Fun [13:42](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=822): Invest in a durable style guide (logos, lockups, character art); it stays reusable for years, amortising the cost.
- 2026-06-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pilot-create-and-sell-animation-packs-and-emotes-on-marketplace/4684642): Author animation clips in external tools (Blender, Maya, Moon Animator), but final upload and bundle assembly must be done in Roblox Studio.
- 2026-06-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pilot-create-and-sell-animation-packs-and-emotes-on-marketplace/4684642): All 7 clips in an Animation Pack must be originally authored; packs reusing default Roblox animations will fail upload.
- 2026-06-19 Yann [4:31](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=271): Gather most assets before building systems; placeholders are fine if they resemble the final look.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [5:20](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=320): Low budget is acceptable only with clear visual identity and feedback. Basic visual design, animation, hit sounds and camera angles still matter.
- 2026-06-24 Cumin [6:40](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=400): Show scenario events with in-game characters rather than static text and image cards, which feel like a missing system.
- 2026-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-unlocking-massive-terrain-with-up-to-64-custom-materials/4703282): Create custom terrain materials by tinting a base material; a Material Variant is optional and only needed for custom texture maps.
- 2026-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-unlocking-massive-terrain-with-up-to-64-custom-materials/4703282): Edits to a terrain material's settings apply instantly across the whole world; deleting an in-use material turns affected terrain bright purple.
- 2026-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-unlocking-massive-terrain-with-up-to-64-custom-materials/4703282): Use Terrain:SetMaterialSlot and WriteVoxelChannels to script terrain generation; avoid writing to slots you have reset, since those voxels render invalid.
- 2026-07-04 Duckable [12:14](https://youtu.be/eRhFPgj8tc4?t=734): Fill empty maps early; a bare void map is an obvious flaw. Free nature asset packs can populate a map quickly.
- 2026-07-08 Chase Higgins [5:39](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=339): Mount declarative UI to Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui from a client script so the root ScreenGui is created on the client.
- 2026-07-08 Chase Higgins [6:23](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=383): Use the Codify plugin to export a Studio-built UI frame into Vide code in one click, then iterate in code.
- 2026-07-23 Erthlord [10:14](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=614): Commission artists for finished UGC items when your own modeling is slow; the creator paid artists per item and uploaded eight at once.
- 2026-07-27 rasdev [4:45](https://youtu.be/_Tt-l7EMEHE?t=285): In-game screenshot method: use a green background behind your model in Studio to cut it out later; requires good in-game visuals.
- 2026-07-27 rasdev [5:26](https://youtu.be/_Tt-l7EMEHE?t=326): Photoshop/Blender method gives the best quality but needs strong Photoshop skills; or pay a specialist.
- 2026-07-29 VuukStudios [9:26](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=566): Buy or grab models, UI templates and tools from Toolbox or template sites for a few dollars instead of commissioning custom art for a simple game.
- 2026-07-29 VuukStudios [10:06](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=606): For simple games, skip custom animations and keep Roblox default animations.
- 2026-07-29 Raul [7:13](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=433): If free asset packs lack needed models like plants, plan to model them yourself; budget extra time for this step.
- 2026-08-01 SimTek Game Development [5:23](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=323): Keep the Handle size consistent across accessories (e.g. X≈1.45) so they scale the same way when fitted to characters.
- 2026-08-02 janisjanis01 [4:42](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=282): Keep visual detail proportional to the game; avoid high-poly maps for simple simulators.
- 2026-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-august-3-7-2026/4781555): Enable EditableMesh Batching (File > Beta Features) for faster mesh editing; batching is stated as up to ~8x faster.
- 2026-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-august-3-7-2026/4781555): Animations attached to imported 3D models are now auto-saved under AnimSaves; Animation Clip Editor Importer exposes Rig Scale, Rest Pose Source, Rig Type and track selection.
- 2026-08-21 LovJak [0:00](https://youtu.be/0RZeCn3-XKg?t=0): Use asset packs and free models to build fast when you are not a modeller; then add grass, flowers and cartoon lighting for a cohesive look.
- 2026-08-23 MonarchDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=41): Use a toolbox simulator pack for quick UI icons rather than building all art from scratch.
- 2026-08-25 RoyalDev [3:26](https://youtu.be/6CefFrCFqcI?t=206): Prioritise a strong core loop over high-poly models and custom lighting; stylised, simple visuals outperform graphically heavy games with weak loops.
- 2026-08-27 Leif [0:41](https://youtu.be/UhpaVrb5Imo?t=41): Model a bottom-stroke with inverted normals in Blender to get a cheap black outline effect that reads well in game.
- 2026-08-27 Leif [1:24](https://youtu.be/UhpaVrb5Imo?t=84): Use a depth-fade mesh with alpha fades to make a hole or pit look endless with little geometry.
- 2026-08-27 Leif [1:38](https://youtu.be/UhpaVrb5Imo?t=98): Attach a spring to a clicked button (Ripple library) so spam clicks keep pushing it, making the press feel springy and satisfying.
- 2026-08-28 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4836039): Assemble, publish, and manage avatar looks in one place directly in Studio.
- 2026-08-30 Duckable [6:52](https://youtu.be/3XX3Y74hzq0?t=412): Put repeated character animation code into one reusable animator module rather than duplicating it into every character model.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/terrain-updates-object-scattering-path-splines-projected-terrain-decals-and-more/4865617): Apply to the Terrain Early Access Program to get early access to upcoming terrain features; you'll be emailed when new features are added.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/terrain-updates-object-scattering-path-splines-projected-terrain-decals-and-more/4865617): Use the upcoming terrain scattering system instead of placing meshes one by one; it uses far fewer resources than instanced meshes.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880): Improved collision geometry (late 2026) auto-generates better collision with a real-time debug overlay and performance-precision controls.
- 2026-09-16 Leif [10:42](https://youtu.be/xVtvDYvUTqc?t=642): Use the Photo Booth plugin (by Ego Moose) to make item icons with no background and a consistent stroke, instead of viewport frames or green screen.
- 2026-09-16 Leif [12:45](https://youtu.be/xVtvDYvUTqc?t=765): Give each item a distinct click animation (wiggle, slam, kickflip, roll) to make rare or strong items feel different.
- 2026-09-16 Leif [5:24](https://youtu.be/xVtvDYvUTqc?t=324): Learning Blender through a prior game (Slime RNG) paid off; simple primitives like a sphere plus rectangles can make cute stylized models.
- 2026-09-16 Leif [6:45](https://youtu.be/xVtvDYvUTqc?t=405): Tag buttons with CollectionService instead of building physical barriers to define zones, keeping map transitions open.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [1:10:48](https://youtu.be/3a2RbLOAl4A?t=4248): Use normal maps on lightweight meshes to keep detail while maintaining performance.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [28:38](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=1718): Branch and merge is coming natively to Studio early next year; all actions are backed by APIs for agentic workflows.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [1:05:18](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3918): Draw distance minimum rises from 200 to 500 studs, including low-end Android devices, rolling out now.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [1:06:41](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=4001): New Voronoi-based convex decomposition for collision geometry auto-picks a performance/precision balance, with granular tweaking.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [1:10:46](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=4246): Terrain scattering is non-destructive: set density, footprint, orientation and scale rules and let the engine place rocks and trees.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4880336): Set CollisionFidelity to Tunable and adjust CollisionPrecision per MeshPart to trade performance against collision precision; defaults scale with mesh size.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4880336): Use the improved collision visualizers to filter by CanCollide, CanQuery, CanTouch and collision groups before publishing to catch wrong collision geometry.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-september-14%E2%80%9320-2026/4880336): Use CollisionFidelity = Tunable with CollisionPrecision per MeshPart to trade performance against collision precision; preview collision geometry with the improved visualizers.
- 2026-09-19 SimTek Game Development [11:48](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=708): If a camera zoom ends with a jolt, tune the start distance and height by printing values where the loop ends; a small offset fixes it.
- 2026-09-19 SimTek Game Development [11:48](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=708): If a jolt can't be eliminated, flip the camera angle at the end of the move so the change looks intentional rather than like a bug.
- 2026-09-30 ArTheDev [2:25](https://youtu.be/HjDD_0cDfPg?t=145): Put each effect's particle emitters and beams inside an Attachment per part, not directly in the Part, so the effect stays locked in position.
- 2026-09-30 ArTheDev [5:11](https://youtu.be/HjDD_0cDfPg?t=311): Loop over effect descendants and set EmitCount, EmitDelay and EmitDuration attributes on every emitter, beam and light so one handler can script all effects uniformly.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Creators [38:03](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=2283): Programmer art can stay if it is clear and charming; it became part of 99 Nights' identity.
- 2026-10-03 MonarchDev [2:14](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=134): Settle on one art style early. Mismatched assets forced a full rebuild to blocky style, and a bad sky tint turned the whole map blue.
- 2026-10-06 TheDankDeveloper [2:49](https://youtu.be/eExtO_uF6Jg?t=169): Ship a sliding/launch core with an upgrade shop and multipliers quickly, then iterate on it daily based on playtesting.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [1:11](https://youtu.be/evyIBvylLdc?t=71): Replace default Roblox lighting and skybox with custom moonlight and atmosphere to stop the game looking like a starter place.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [15:03](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=903): Back state with instances already used client-side (e.g. ValueBase objects) to cut refactoring when porting an existing codebase.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [4:25](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=265): Use procedural models with user-defined attributes (e.g. fence post spacing) to resize and iterate geometry in real time instead of remodelling.
- 2026-10-12 Xaiyne [2:05](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=125): Keep UGC accessory meshes under Roblox's 4,000 triangle limit; the speaker targets 3,990 as a safe buffer.
- 2026-10-12 Xaiyne [3:29](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=209): Export textures at 2K and FBX format, the highest texture resolution Roblox supports for meshes.
- 2026-10-12 Xaiyne [3:42](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=222): In Studio, ungroup the imported mesh, rename it to Handle, place it inside an Accessory, then fit it using the Avatar accessory tool preview on a character.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [24:15](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1455): Disable your own custom mesh LOD systems when enabling Roblox mesh streaming; otherwise you get LODs of LODs.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [24:55](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1495): Increase texture variety and resolution incrementally while monitoring stability, since texture streaming now scales automatically across devices.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [24:55](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1495): Enable mesh streaming via its workspace property (opt-in phase); skinned meshes are not yet supported.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [27:38](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1658): Don't use texture atlases unless needed; if you use them, ensure parts sharing an atlas are on screen together, since streaming unloads whole maps only.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [27:38](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1658): When memory and draw calls conflict, prioritise memory: running out of memory is fatal, while slower performance is less severe.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [29:44](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1784): Use uniform UV density across texture mapping so the system doesn't wrongly request higher-resolution mips.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [16:58](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1018): Avoid heavy UI textures on desktop for now; UI textures are not streamable there yet.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [18:43](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1123): Use procedural models for buildings and fences: attributes like window count or fence length scale geometry rather than the mesh, keeping objects editable after generation.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [23:31](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1411): Add custom parameters (e.g. door color) to procedural models after generation; existing instances keep their placement when the code is updated.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [26:51](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1611): Use retexture with a reference image to restyle several mixed assets at once so the whole scene shares one visual style.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [5:05](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=305): Adaptive animations let one R15 animation clip play on R6, R15 and custom rigs via humanoid rig description, so no reauthoring is needed.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [5:51](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=351): Use animation graphs (node editor in Studio) so animators can own movement logic without writing Luau; swap animation IDs and speed parameters visually.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [12:33](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=753): Use Auto Setup with Platform or Development presets to turn a raw mesh into a rigged avatar in minutes; it only fills in missing parts of pre-rigged bodies.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [22:31](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=1351): If Auto Setup output is inconsistent, file a dev forum bug report, or pre-fix the mesh part (e.g. head seams in Blender) and reimport.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [23:11](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=1391): Roblox is looking at exposing animation data sources to the graph so values can be set from code, useful for data-driven reloads.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [24:34](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=1474): Importing high-poly separated meshes from another engine can flood inventory with parts; previews before committing would prevent this.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [24:53](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1493): Prioritise consistent, legible art over photorealism; cohesive style and sound matter more than budget.
- 2026-10-13 TheDankDeveloper [2:03](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=123): Give the AI a screenshot of a reference game's UI and ask it to replicate the layout with changes.
- 2026-10-13 TheDankDeveloper [2:44](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=164): Upload an icon pack (e.g. free vector packs) to Claude so it uses those icons in the UI.
- 2026-10-13 TheDankDeveloper [4:47](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=287): Don't rely heavily on AI for builds or UI; always supply a visual reference, since AI is weak at both.
- 2026-10-13 TheDankDeveloper [6:09](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=369): Use Blender MCP to have Claude model low-poly assets, then import into Studio and place/color them.
- 2026-10-16 MonarchDev [5:08](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=308): Decide the visual style explicitly (medieval, futuristic, realistic) before iterating; style is separate from graphics quality.
- 2026-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-12-18-2026/4938638): Update published 3D accessories and clothing in Studio; asset ID, sales history and owners carry over.
- 2026-10-18 Duckable [16:33](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=993): Avoid making separate meshes per variant; tint with color lerp between gradient colors, or cycle hue over time for a rainbow variant.
- 2026-10-21 Xaiyne [3:52](https://youtu.be/wkfCP_09CI8?t=232): Extract the Tripo zip before importing; import FBX via File > Import, then press 3 to scale and Ctrl+U to ungroup, renaming the part to Handle.
- 2026-10-21 Xaiyne [5:14](https://youtu.be/wkfCP_09CI8?t=314): Use Avatar Editor accessory fitting: place model in the Face bounds (box turns blue when within limits) before clicking Generate Mesh Part Accessory.
- 2026-10-21 Xaiyne [6:21](https://youtu.be/wkfCP_09CI8?t=381): Use the free ThumbMaker plugin to produce the marketplace thumbnail from the Handle, then right-click Handle > Save and Export to Roblox.
- 2026-10-21 studio rook. [2:28](https://youtu.be/z04_RTBiMgg?t=148): Model rough low-poly shapes and push detail into baked normals and textures; this keeps poly counts and draw calls low for Roblox.
- 2026-10-21 studio rook. [3:20](https://youtu.be/z04_RTBiMgg?t=200): Speed trick: select all sharp corners and bevel them in one pass instead of placing exact bevels everywhere.
- 2026-10-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-19%E2%80%9323-2026/4953263): Custom Terrain Materials support up to 62 materials per game with reduced file size and load time; stay within that limit.
- 2026-10-26 MonarchDev [1:16](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=76): Work on visuals in tiers: overall map design first, then models, then lighting and polish last.
- 2026-10-26 MonarchDev [3:06](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=186): Give concrete art direction: sunset lighting, outlined characters for a cartoon look, and specified base colours.
- 2026-10-26 MonarchDev [4:00](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=240): AI-generated meshes often need conversion for Roblox and may be lower quality; check them against Roblox's polygon requirements before relying on them.
- 2026-10-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-build-2d-games-with-animatedimages/4960734): Use AnimatedImageService:AddAnimatedImageClipAsync to build new or override tracks from existing frames, e.g. retimed limp or ping-pong sway animations. Custom tracks save with the place.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/optimize-your-experience): Reuse assets via packages so every instance shares one asset ID; separate imports of identical meshes count as unique assets and cost memory and performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/optimize-your-experience): After simplifying geometry in third-party tools, update the existing package with the new import rather than creating a new asset ID, to avoid extra asset calls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/blender-configurations): Before exporting, either replace the Mix node with the ColorMap node or export textures manually as separate image files, since the skin tone preview blocks automatic texture packaging into the .fbx.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/blender-configurations): Use textures with full or partial transparency so underlying Class.Part.Color shows through, enabling customizable skin tones; use full opacity for areas that should always be a fixed color.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/blender-configurations): Save custom project settings as startup defaults via File > Defaults > Save Startup File to reuse them in future Blender projects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/play-your-animation): 
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/add-new-models): Import kit assets via Toolbox > Inventory > My Models, after moving the camera to the target area; kits import at camera position.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/add-new-models): Duplicate kit parts with Ctrl+D (Cmd+D on Mac) to build variants rather than modelling from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-3): Build with parts rather than Toolbox models, since Toolbox assets may introduce unexpected issues into the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/sculpting): Use Elastic Deform at 0.5 strength to stretch vertices so the clothing mesh fully covers the mannequin; adjust brush radius with F.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/sculpting): Hide the mannequin to reach hard-to-access areas while sculpting clothing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/sculpting): Disable X-Axis symmetry before using the Cloth tool; it can produce unexpected results when symmetry is enabled.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies): Use facial bones and face pose keyframes mapped by pose name in the Head object so expressions work in chat and socializing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/performance): Reuse art assets across the game instead of creating unique meshes for every object, to reduce rendering cost.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials): Learn solid modeling in Studio before rigging; use Blender with Roblox's template models for custom avatar characters.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials): Use the built-in Animation Editor for basic animations, then swap default avatar animations with your own.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport): Use Reimport (Alt+Shift+R) to update models from Blender instead of re-importing; it keeps existing properties and saves re-entering import settings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport): Keep mesh path/names unique in your external 3D tool; reimport matches meshes by path/name, so renames create new MeshParts instead of updating.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport): Maintain the naming and tree structure of MeshParts inside the Studio model so reimport updates them correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport): Name PBR texture files with recognized suffixes (e.g. _Color, _Normal, _Rough, _Metal, _Emissive) so reimport maps them to SurfaceAppearance automatically.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport): Reimport only updates mesh content and transform; CollisionFidelity and RenderFidelity are preserved, and a mesh with no texture keeps the existing texture.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/create-the-map): Use the Terrain Editor Draw tool with Brush Mode to add terrain for hills and grasslands and subtract it for cliffs and rivers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-setup): In Blender, textures must be saved manually in the Texture Paint 2D view; saving only the .blend file does not save them. Save often to avoid losing hours of work.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-setup): On Blender 5.2+, set Edit > Preferences > Save & Load > Blend Files > Save Modified Images to Always Save to avoid losing texture work.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-setup): Final albedo texture resolution should be 1024x1024, the resolution Roblox Studio supports for albedo maps.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-setup): For fine details, temporarily resize the texture to 2048x2048 or 4096x4096 while painting, then resize back to 1024x1024 to bake the changes in.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/automatic-skinning-transfer): Test auto-skinned accessories on multiple avatar body types and animations; the transfer is based on closest distance to the body's outer cage.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/automatic-skinning-transfer): Trim irrelevant regions from the outer cage (e.g. keep only head for beards) to stop skinning from wrong body parts. Partial cages are in-game only, not Marketplace-valid.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping): Prototype UI/UX early, especially menu interaction and input controls, since paper is well suited to exploring UI layouts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): Group multi-part items into a Model and anchor all parts so the harvestable item stays in place.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Always start caging from one of Roblox's provided caging templates; they include inner and outer cages with properly set UVs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Do not edit cage UVs; editing them introduces visual artifacts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Keep the clothing mesh fully between the inner and outer cage so the layering system deforms it correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Move outer cage vertices outward along vertex normals and keep the outer cage as close to the clothing as possible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Do not stack vertices or change the outer cage's vertex, edge, and face count from the starting template.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Keep outer cage spacing even across faces; uneven spacing causes artifacts and uneven deformation when other layers are added.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Align cage break lines with joint positions, e.g. elbow with the LowerArm joint, to improve animation and posing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): Only modify cage regions related to your accessory; for a t-shirt, leave lower leg cages untouched.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): For bulky clothing, displace outer cage vertices along normals and keep neighbouring vertices moving in similar directions to avoid distortion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/caging-best-practices): For protrusions like puffy hoods or spikes, keep the outer cage below the protruding part so it stays visible when other layers are equipped.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/work-with-the-animation-editor): Open the Animation Editor via Avatar tab > Clip Editor; rig must be selected so part names display in the editor.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/work-with-the-animation-editor): Turn off Rotate and Move snapping before posing so rig parts rotate freely.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/work-with-the-animation-editor): Select the Rotate tool; if you see arrows instead of circular handles, switch to Rotate. Rotate from the torso outward: upper arms and thighs first, then hands and feet.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/work-with-the-animation-editor): Copy the first pose's keyframe at 0:00 and paste it at the end of the timeline, then enable Loop so the animation repeats seamlessly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/work-with-the-animation-editor): Pose from multiple camera angles; hold Shift to slow camera movement; press F to focus on the selected object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/work-with-the-animation-editor): To restart a pose, right-click its top white diamond and choose Reset Selected.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/classic-clothing): For shirts and pants, download and modify the official template files rather than starting from a blank canvas, since they have fixed size requirements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/classic-clothing): Keep shoe designs near the bottom of leg regions; designs extending too far up look like part of the pants.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/classic-clothing): Export classic clothing as .png or .jpg before testing and uploading.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/studio/explore-ui): Use a 2-button mouse with a scroll wheel; Roblox strongly recommends it for navigating 3D space in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/studio/explore-ui): Select a part and press F to focus the camera on it; use , and . to orbit around the focused object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/studio/explore-ui): Check Toolbox Inventory and Creations for your own assets; Creations excludes Creator Store items, Recent shows latest used.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/modeling-setup): Start clothing from Roblox's Clothing_Cage.blend template and use File > Save As so the template stays untouched.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/modeling-setup): Duplicate the InnerCage, then Parent > Clear and Keep Transformation so the copy keeps its position and scale without a parent.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/modeling-setup): Remove the helper colorSet1 vertex color attribute before importing, since it can clash with the texture in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/user-interface): Use a ProximityPrompt attached to a 3D model as a simple way to let players toggle world-embedded UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/adding-the-second-pose): Pose body parts connected to the torso first (upper arm, thigh), then work outward to hands and feet to pose faster.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/get-started): Start animation learning from a Roblox-provided template (e.g. Island of Move) that already contains working gameplay code and environment.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/solid-modeling): Solid modeling on meshes requires watertight geometry: closed, manifold, non-self-intersecting. Check with DoubleSided toggling; if the mesh looks different, it is a shell and repair tools will fail.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets): Use rbxassetid instead of rbxgameasset if an asset must work across multiple games; rbxgameasset only resolves in the game where it was uploaded.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets): Assets are private by default on import; distribute to the Creator Store to let everyone use the asset ID.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets): Use Packages for reusable asset hierarchies so a package update can be applied to all copies or set to auto-update.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets): Import assets before publishing; unapproved assets in the moderation queue are invisible to users until Roblox approves them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets): Use the Toolbox to reuse any asset you or your groups have imported, across all games, rather than keeping local copies.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/implement-designs-in-studio): Import a shared UI asset library via Toolbox > Inventory > My Packages to reuse prebuilt icons and components across projects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-eyes): Increase texture image resolution before painting eyes so fine details like pupils render sharply.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-eyes): Join both eye meshes before texture painting so both eyes receive identical texture work at the same time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-eyes): Paint in order: fully black base, then outer eye color, then inner eye, then pupils, so layers stack correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-eyes): Hide head, teeth and tongue meshes in the viewport while painting eyes, keeping only eye meshes visible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-eyes): Toggle X Symmetry on for symmetric eyes and off for asymmetrical designs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-eyes): Periodically make the head mesh visible to check the eye texture against the full model.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/texturing-eyes): After painting, return to Edit mode, then separate joined eyes with P > By Loose Parts and rename each to its original name.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/finishing-the-project): Turn the graybox into a real map with a clear setting; use premade textured packs like Synty Nature, City, or Dungeon if not building from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/landing): Finish by decorating the map with assets and extend the base game with optional challenges.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/export): If using Roblox's avatar template files, complete the cleanup steps or the assets will fail validation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/main-page): Use a 3-button mouse with a scroll wheel when building in Roblox Studio, as the page strongly recommends.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/terrain): Use the Generate tool with biome selection to rapidly create large base terrain, then fine-tune details with brush tools.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/terrain): For colormaps, use hard-edged colors matching the RobloxColorMapIndex values exactly, since anti-aliasing can produce colors that map to the wrong material.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/terrain): Enable Expanded Terrain to paint custom terrain materials, or apply custom materials as overrides.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/import): If your single-mesh model needs splitting, use the Avatar Setup tool to partition it into the 15 required avatar body parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/import): If textures fail to load, upload them manually via Asset Manager; save and publish the game first if Asset Manager is inaccessible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/import): For PBR textures, add a SurfaceAppearance to the MeshPart and map ColorMap=_ALB, MetalnessMap=_MTL, NormalMap=_NOR, RoughnessMap=_RGH.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/trimming): Start clothing from a clean mesh: duplicate the mannequin body mesh and trim it down to the garment shape rather than modelling from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/trimming): Enable Wireframe mode in Blender's viewport when trimming, so you can see and select the vertices you want to delete.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/trimming): Set the body mesh to unselectable in Blender while editing the clothing to prevent accidental edits to the mannequin.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/create-flickering-lights): Store flicker parameters as model attributes (BrightnessCurve as NumberSequence, LoopDuration as Number) so you can tune them in Studio without editing script code.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/create-flickering-lights): Keep original brightness and color values in local variables and multiply by a relative 0-1 factor, so the effect scales correctly from the authored values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating): Texture the mesh in Blender to set surface appearance and color before export.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world): Set final scene lighting early to see which surface details are actually visible and skip the rest.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world): Bake high-poly sculpt normal maps onto low-poly spheres to keep detail without performance cost.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/test-blender): Periodically test character bodies in Blender to catch issues before importing into Studio; use templates and non-destructive modeling to avoid breaking components.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/calisthenics-tool): Test accessory attachment with Normal Size and Slender Size reference accessories; if one attaches wrong, reposition its Attachment object in the rig.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/creating): For avatar items, a simple 2D t-shirt can be made in an image editor without opening Studio; use Blender plus Studio for basic 3D accessories.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/creating): 3D accessories should be simple, non-animated items that attach to a single point on the avatar's body.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor): Use Generate to create a large base terrain map quickly, then fine-tune details with Edit tab tools.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor): Use Select with Snap to Voxels enabled to keep terrain selection bounds aligned to the voxel grid.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor): Disable Live Edit in Transform and press Enter/Return or Apply to preview a wireframe before committing a large terrain transform.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor): Check the rotation snapping setting in the toolbar before rotating terrain with rotate rings; disable it for free-form rotation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor): Enable Auto Material on Draw/Sculpt to match the terrain material already near the brush when adding terrain.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor): Use Sculpt's Strength (0.1 to 1) for gentle edits instead of Draw, which has no strength control.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor): Hold Alt on mouse click with brush tools to open the material picker quickly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/terrain-editor): Use B, Ctrl+B and Shift+B with scroll or drag to adjust brush base size, height and strength respectively.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/data-model): Import meshes (MeshPart) and combine primitive parts via solid modeling for complex structures rather than relying only on simple parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/buttons): Place screen buttons under a ScreenGui in StarterGui; place part-face buttons under a SurfaceGui parented to the part.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts): Anchor most parts; unanchored parts are affected by gravity and physics as soon as the game runs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts): Use WeldConstraint or Motor6D/Bone to connect related parts into one rigid assembly; group them in a Model for easy handling.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts): Set CanCollide to false only when parts should pass through things; physics ignores non-colliding parts entirely.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts): Material affects physical traits (density, sinking in water), not just looks; pick materials with real-world weight in mind.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts): Use Visualization Options > Anchored parts to see which parts cannot move under physics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts): Use Ctrl+L (Mac Cmd+L) to toggle local vs world orientation when transforming parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts): For detailed or organic shapes, import third-party models as MeshParts rather than building from primitives.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/emotes/import): Publish the CurveAnimation to Roblox via Publish to Roblox to get a cloud asset ID, then set it as the AnimationId of an Animation object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/roblox-blender-plugin): Use the official Roblox Blender plugin to send models straight from Blender to Studio, skipping manual export/import of third-party files to reduce errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/trails): Place the two Attachments apart to set trail width; closer attachments produce a narrower trail. Enable View > Show Constraint Details to see them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/plugins): Wrap plugin edits in ChangeHistoryService TryBeginRecording/FinishRecording so users can undo and redo them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/capture): Enable File > Beta Features > Face Capture before using webcam face capture; it is off by default.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/capture): For face capture, sit in a well-lit room, close to camera, with face centred in frame to improve tracking accuracy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/capture): Face capture records up to 60 seconds; use Re-Record to discard and retry a take before saving.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/capture): Enable File > Beta Features > Live Animation Creator before importing video for body animation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/capture): Body video must be .mp4 or .mov, one person, well lit and fully visible, single continuous shot under 15 seconds, stable camera.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/capture): Body video import applies only to R15 rigs; expect roughly a minute of processing before keyframes appear.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/capture): Captured keyframes remain editable in the timeline; fine-tune, save, and export the animation to reuse across games.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/enhance-outdoor-environments): Switch Lighting.LightingStyle to Realistic so local light sources (PointLight, SpotLight, SurfaceLight) cast accurate shadows; Soft is the default.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/enhance-outdoor-environments): Set Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale and EnvironmentSpecularScale to 1 so PBR materials, MaterialVariants and SurfaceAppearance show real metal reflections.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation): Use the Animation Editor to pose rig joints; the editor interpolates smoothly between poses, so you only author keyframe poses.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/watertight): Make accessory meshes watertight so only the visible outer surface is exposed; open holes expose backfaces that affect rendering and performance when equipped.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/watertight): To seal a hole: Alt-click the last edge, press E to extrude, click once to add a small extrusion, right-click > Merge Vertices > At Center, then G to reposition the vertex.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/unwrapping): Create seams along natural separations (e.g. shirt center front/back edges) before unwrapping so Blender's UV layout splits the mesh into sensible front and back surfaces.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/unwrapping): Use Blender Texture Paint mode to apply a base colour and smaller design directly to the UV-mapped clothing; PBR textures need third-party tools like Substance Painter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/jumpstart): Pick a distinctive visual style (hyper-realistic 3D, stylized 2.5D sprites, high-fidelity avatars) so players think 'Wait, that's Roblox?'
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator): Use 'close up', 'top down' or 'texture' in prompts for close-up patterns.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator): Use 'simple', 'pattern', 'symmetrical' or 'flat' for simpler repeating patterns.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator): Add 'photorealistic', 'cartoon' or 'hand-drawn' to add stylistic control over output.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator): Include 'grayscale' in prompts so the generated material can be tinted afterwards.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator): Expect iteration: every Generate click yields different results, so refine with longer keyword lists.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator): Use the Studs Per Tile slider to preview texture scale on selected parts before saving.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator): Enable the Organic toggle to reduce visible repetition by randomizing the output.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/material-generator): Pick a Base Material before saving to inherit its default physical properties.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/light-sources): Insert PointLights into an Attachment rather than a BasePart, since Attachment is recommended for point-specific light emission.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/light-sources): Use local light sources (PointLight, SpotLight, SurfaceLight) instead of relying only on global Lighting for immersive, mood-driven environments.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/light-sources): Brightness is capped by Range: raising Brightness does not light a larger area, so adjust Range to change coverage.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/light-sources): Match light type to source: PointLight for omnidirectional sources like bulbs and torches, SpotLight for directional sources like streetlamps and flashlights, SurfaceLight for screens and signs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting): Use OutdoorAmbient for time-of-day color shifts outdoors; Ambient tints the whole game, including indoor areas, so it cannot mimic sunlight alone.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting): Set LightingStyle to Realistic for the most advanced shadows and lighting; use Soft for a flat, retro Roblox look.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting): Set ShadowSoftness only when LightingStyle is Realistic; it has no effect with Soft.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting): Adjust ClockTime or TimeOfDay via script to change time; they do not advance on their own during play.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting): Use ExposureCompensation to control perceived brightness; lower values under-expose, higher values over-expose.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting): Set EnvironmentSpecularScale near 1 so smooth and metal surfaces reflect the environment realistically.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/video-frames): Parent VideoFrame to a ScreenGui, SurfaceGui or BillboardGui, otherwise it will not play.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props): Set a part's Material to Neon to make it look glowing; Neon alone emits no light, so add a light object to it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props): Use PointLight for bulbs, torches and fireballs (spherical emission from a single point).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props): Use SpotLight for street lamps, flashlights and headlights (cone emission); adjust Face to choose the emitting axis.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props): Use SurfaceLight for TV/computer screens, lit billboards and fluorescent panels, since it emits from the whole face.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props): Keep SpotLight Angle moderate (e.g. 30-75) for focused cones; max 180 lights a full half sphere.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props): For SurfaceLight, Angle 0 emits straight outward from the surface; Angle 180 emits perpendicular-spread across the surface.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/light-with-props): Tune PointLight Range (e.g. 8 to 12 studs) to control illumination radius, alongside Brightness and Color.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/go-beyond-the-challenge): Import new models to add variety to vehicles or props, and customize colors and textures to give a vehicle a distinct style.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save): Make the first and last animation poses identical to avoid a skip or glitch when the animation loops; copy the first pose and paste it at the end.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save): Keep the rig inside the white grid in the Animation Editor so it does not pass through walls or the ground during playback.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save): Save animations in the Animation Editor after every edit; unsaved changes will not show in playtests.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/roblox-for-the-ap-csp-create-performance-task): Avoid Toolbox assets unless every bundled script is removed; prepackaged scripts can conflict with your code and waste debugging time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/artist): Start from the Mansion of Wonder sample template to learn VFX in Studio; it gives a working workspace to modify rather than building from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture): Settle on one uniform grid size (station used 16 studs) for all artists and pieces so modules snap and swap easily.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture): Treat DCC units as studs: Studio does not convert meters or feet. One stud is roughly 28 cm real-world.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture): Keep the modular kit small with a few versatile pieces; each extra piece costs creation time and memory/performance budget.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture): Use trim sheets with multiple surface treatments per sheet to reduce draw calls and avoid splitting meshes; station used them for about 90% of architecture.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture): Make a trim sheet template first so map sets (e.g. clean vs rusted metal) can be swapped without rework.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture): Convert modular pieces and materials into packages before populating the world so updates can be pushed to all instances.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/building-architecture): Create a master SurfaceAppearance material library as packages; edit the master and republish to update every object using it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Target 72 FPS on Quest; frame drops cause discomfort. If visuals look poor under Auto Quality Mode, optimize the game further rather than accepting degraded output.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Mobile VR is sensitive to draw call count: add detail where it matters, use lower detail elsewhere, and keep object count in scenes conservative.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Keep custom meshes low-geometry for runtime efficiency.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Minimize semi-transparent objects, partially transparent Decals, and Glass material; they cost performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Keep SurfaceGuis few and simple; numerous or complex ones are costly for rendering and CPU.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/next-steps): Use Materials to change a part's visual appearance and physical properties instead of relying on plain parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/next-steps): Use the Terrain Editor to generate realistic environments such as mountains, grass hills, or flat deserts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/next-steps): Use textures to scale and decals to stretch 2D images onto parts to decorate them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/next-steps): Use solid modeling operations (unions, intersections) to join parts into more complex custom shapes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/galactic-speedway-lesson): Encourage experimenting with different part sizes and rotations; unexpected part uses often produce better designs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/animation/create-an-animation): x
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-policy): Face makeup beyond shading (facial painting, multicolor eyeliner or lips) must be sold as separate items, not baked into the head.
