- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes/create-cages): 
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/modeling/create-neon-signs): Build signs from basic Block parts, then use Negate and Union to sculpt complex shapes such as a border, all within Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/modeling/create-neon-signs): Set Material to Neon on parts that should glow; this is the core technique for neon signage.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/modeling/create-neon-signs): Name parts and group them into a Model (Ctrl+G) so the asset is organized and can be saved to Roblox as one object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/modeling/create-neon-signs): Save reusable assets to your Toolbox via Save to Roblox; you can later publish them to the Creator Store.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/caging): For full-body sculpt changes across many parts, use the Blender Cage Template with its std_cage_deformable helper mesh instead of editing each _OuterCage manually.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar): Start from Roblox's downloadable Blender, Maya and .fbx project files for base rigs, attachment points and cage meshes to match standard humanoid specs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar): Use the Accessory Fitting Tool in Studio to test custom accessories and layered clothing across body types, animations and other accessories.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/resources): Clean up Roblox avatar template files (per warnings) before publishing to the Marketplace or they will fail validation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/resources): Remove clothing and accessory items from Nature Girl and Stylish Male references before uploading; they can cause validation errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/resources): Check Blender armature bone orientation: references exported from Maya may look misoriented, but this does not affect rig performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/resources): Use mannequin models (Classic, Rthro, Rthro Slender) as scale, body shape and caging reference when designing accessories.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/resources): Lola reference lacks inner and outer cage mesh data, so it cannot equip rigid or layered accessories; use a caged reference instead.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/import): Import layered accessories via Home > Import as FBX or glTF; the Importer previews the asset and checks it against general 3D requirements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/import): If textures fail to load, upload images in Asset Manager and wait for moderation to clear before assigning them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/import): For a single basic texture, set MeshPart.TextureID; for PBR, add a SurfaceAppearance and assign ColorMap (_ALB), MetalnessMap (_MTL), NormalMap (_NOR), RoughnessMap (_RGH).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/custom-characters): Model the character in a neutral pose; curled poses stretch when animated to bend the opposite way.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/custom-characters): Keep each mesh part under 10,000 triangles and the total size under 2000 units on any axis before import.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/custom-characters): Keep SurfaceAppearance texture maps at 1024x1024 or smaller and pack maps into one sheet per type.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/custom-characters): Delete the AnimSaves folder after closing the Animation Editor; its files are only needed during editing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/export): Apply or delete all modifiers and project object modifiers before exporting a rigid accessory model, so mesh and attachment data export correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/export): Enable Embed Media in Maya export only if you need textures imported as .png.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/npc-kit): Customize NPC looks by modifying BodyParts and adding Accessory objects rather than changing the base rig.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/npc-kit): Do not rename Motor6D joints on NPC rigs; Animator relies on their names matching those used when animations were created.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/create-a-timed-bridge): Make inactive bridge parts semi-transparent (Transparency 0.8) with CanCollide false so players can see them but not walk on them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets): Organize polished assets into folders (Platforms, Mountains, Coins) under World and mirror greybox level names so swaps are easy to track.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets): If meshes appear gray after importing a library, drag the referenced MaterialVariant objects into MaterialService to install their textures.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets): Keep polished coins named exactly 'Coin' inside the Coins folder so existing scripts that reference coins by name keep working.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets): Rotate and scale repeated background meshes (e.g. mountains) with varied Orientation and Scale so the repetition is not noticeable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/apply-polished-assets): Delete placeholder greybox parts only after every polished replacement is placed, so you can compare progress against the greybox layout.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim): Separate problematic PartOperation unions/negations into their own model or simplify CSG if they render missing or distorted in SLIM.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim): Move coplanar or overlapping parts apart by about 0.01 studs to eliminate Z-fighting on SLIM models.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim): Use SurfaceAppearance for important surfaces and group related parts in one model, since SLIM regenerates UVs and default material tiling may shift.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/custom-thumbnails): When resizing a Handle, change its Size property rather than using the resize tool, which can cause unintended changes to the item.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant): Use /generate_procedural_model for objects you may want to resize later; use /generate_mesh for single fixed textured models.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant): Tweak Lighting properties in Studio for skybox, atmosphere and lighting; changes apply instantly without re-prompting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/guide): Segment generated or imported meshes into parts so you can apply different materials, scripts, or replace components without regenerating the whole model.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/guide): Use recommended part names (e.g. body, left front wheel, blade) for common object types; they produce the most reliable segmentation. Max five parts per /segment_mesh run.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/textures-decals): Use a Texture instead of a Decal when the image must tile across a surface; Texture repeats, Decal stretches to fit the face.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/textures-decals): Scaling a part does not change a Texture's tile size; it only changes repeat count. Set StudsPerTileU/V to control tile size.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/creating-a-sparkler): Start from a pre-made tool set from the Toolbox Inventory (My Models) to skip building from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/texture-generator): Select a single mesh or a unified Model of MeshParts; avoid scattered meshes or more than one identical mesh in one operation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/texture-generator): Use Texture Generator for contextual custom 3D assets; use Material Generator for repeating or tiled surfaces like wood floors or cobblestone.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/texture-generator): Don't modify the asset while generation runs; editing before completion can cause the process to fail.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/texture-generator): Back up assets before texturing, since Ctrl/Cmd+Z undo gets complicated when other scene work happens concurrently.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/texture-generator): Use Quality mode (4K PBR) when fidelity matters; small generation artifacts are more visible at this sharpness.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/rig-and-cage-existing-models): Use the rig template project so the accessory parents to the default mannequin armature required for layered accessories.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/materials): Use a consistent PascalCase naming convention for MaterialVariants with the base material as first word (GrassWet, GrassDry, GrassBurned) to enable adaptive materials.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/materials): Rename a custom material before applying it to parts; renaming after application requires re-applying it to those parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/materials): Use square textures for custom material texture maps; unassigned texture maps remain blank.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/materials): Create MaterialVariant instances in MaterialService and include them when distributing models to the Creator Store, otherwise custom materials won't carry over.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/materials): Use material overrides to restyle all instances of a base material in a place at once, rather than re-applying materials to each part.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/materials): Use MaterialVariant for reusable tileable textures; use SurfaceAppearance for a specific UV-mapped mesh.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/adding-new-models): Add reusable models to your Inventory first, then insert them from Toolbox > Inventory > My Models so they are available across Studio projects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/constraints/rigid): To join two BaseParts together, use WeldConstraint instead of RigidConstraint.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/social-interactions): Install SocialInteractions from the Creator Store Packages category and move it into ReplicatedStorage; it starts running automatically.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui): Use SurfaceGui or BillboardGui for UI that must live in the 3D world, such as nameplates or signs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/in-experience-containers): Use Show Orientation Indicator (right-click part) to pick the correct SurfaceGui Face before building.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/clouds): Clouds only render if the Clouds object is parented under Terrain; it can live elsewhere for organization or replication.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/clouds): Set cloud motion through global wind (Workspace wind), since wind controls drift direction and speed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/clouds): Tune cloud Color together with Lighting and Atmosphere properties, which also affect cloud color; experiment to get the atmosphere you want.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/clouds): Use low Density (around 0.1) for light, semi-translucent clouds; higher Density (around 0.3+) for heavy, dark stormy clouds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/beams): Use FaceCamera = true on flat beams so they remain visible from any camera angle, since a beam is a 2D projection in 3D space.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/duplicate-and-design): Duplicate parts with Ctrl+D (Cmd+D on Mac) to reuse existing parts instead of rebuilding them from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/duplicate-and-design): Turn off rotation snapping in the toolbar next to Rotate for free rotation; remember to turn it back on afterward.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/educator-onboarding/4-running-classrooms): Use Live Collaboration in Studio so invited collaborators can build or edit scripts in the same project in real time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid): Rigid accessories attach to avatar attachment points; users can adjust position and rotation per body type, so design items to tolerate that offset.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools): Source royalty-free music, community-made models, and plugins from the Marketplace instead of building everything from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar-setup): Import models with the Importer tool and disable 'Upload To Roblox' in File General to avoid unwanted copies of similar assets in Toolbox.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar-setup): Enable Improve facial caging if you plan to use makeup with the character; it improves head cage alignment with facial features.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/export-character): In Blender FBX export, set Path Mode to Copy and enable Embed Textures so the .fbx carries its textures with it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/prompt-engineering): For asset generation, start with the object name and add material, age and distinctive details; omit backgrounds, camera angles, lighting and generic quality terms.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/prompt-engineering): For procedural models, explicitly name the properties to expose and define how parts scale with size or count (e.g. add chairs proportionally when table length grows).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/prompt-engineering): For procedural model reference images, use .png with good lighting, a clear subject, simple background, and isometric or 3/4 angles; avoid low-poly style images.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/prompt-engineering): For objects that cannot be captured from one angle, include multiple viewpoints in a single reference image.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio): Pull free models, meshes, images, sounds and videos from the Creator Store rather than building every asset from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio): Use the Avatar Setup tool to auto-rig and cage imported character models so they support layered accessories.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio): Use the Material and Texture generators to create custom materials and textures for 3D objects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve): Set MeshPart CollisionFidelity to Box for small or non-interactable parts, box/hull for small-medium, and build custom collision from invisible parts for large complex meshes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve): Upload each mesh once and duplicate it in Studio rather than importing whole maps, so identical meshes share asset IDs and instance into single draw calls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve): Load audio only when needed for the part of the game being played, since audio files can be a surprising source of client memory use.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/import): Import rigid accessories as .fbx or .gltf via the Home tab Import button; the Importer previews the asset and checks it against general 3D requirements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/import): If textures fail to load on import, upload them via Asset Manager, wait for moderation to clear, then add a SurfaceAppearance to the MeshPart.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/import): Assign SurfaceAppearance maps from the _ALB, _MTL, _NOR and _RGH images to ColorMap, MetalnessMap, NormalMap and RoughnessMap respectively.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/labels): Use a ScreenGui in StarterGui for on-screen labels, such as character portraits with dialog.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/labels): Use a SurfaceGui on a part face for billboards, posters and wallpaper. If the insert button is missing, change the SurfaceGui's Face property.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/labels): ImageLabel images must be imported into Studio assets. Display them at native resolution, since they look best there.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/engagement-rewards): Reward icons must be Roblox image asset IDs, not decal IDs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/specifications): Keep individual meshes under 20,000 triangles; avatar items have their own separate budgets for bodies, rigid and layered accessories.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/specifications): Make meshes watertight with no exposed holes or backfaces, and give them real volume (no zero-thickness geometry).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/specifications): Model with quads where possible and avoid N-gons.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/specifications): Name inner and outer cages after the primary mesh with _InnerCage and _OuterCage suffixes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/specifications): Keep cage faces consistently sized and symmetrical; use symmetry tools in your modeling software.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/rig-a-simple-mesh): Set Blender to Studio's relative scene units before importing a model to rig, so the exported rig scales correctly in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/animate-in-roblox-lesson): Use body movement or reference images when posing; a visual reference helps identify which body part to rotate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Insert a SurfaceAppearance under a MeshPart and enter asset IDs in its map properties; it overrides the MeshPart's original texture.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Use an albedo (color) map, not a diffuse map with baked lighting; baked shading looks wrong when lighting changes. Let normal, roughness and metalness maps handle detail.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Set Transparency alpha mode with MeshPart.Transparency at 0 for hard-edged cutouts (foliage, lace) so depth occlusion renders correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Set MeshPart.Transparency to at least 0.02 when alpha adds detail to semi-transparent objects like dirty glass or soft feathers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Author color maps in near-white grayscale so SurfaceAppearance.Color tinting gives the strongest effect; reuse one map with multiple tints to save memory.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Keep normal map seams hidden; normal maps accentuate awkward seams. Use OpenGL tangent-space normal maps only, since Roblox supports no other format.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Base material values on physical characteristics rather than tuning for one lighting setup; test across lighting environments.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): For fresnel-related unexpected sheen on rough surfaces, increase the roughness map value by about 0.1.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Use partial metalness only for moderate-reflectance materials like satin or silk; otherwise use 0% or 100%.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Use an emissive mask instead of the all-or-nothing Neon material for detailed glow like illuminated text or bioluminescence. Marketplace avatar items must keep EmissiveStrength between 0 and 40.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/create-waterfalls): Break a real-world reference (e.g. Snoqualmie Falls) into distinct components before building; each component maps to specific VFX objects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/adding-animations): Set animation priority to Action and Looped to false for one-shot celebration animations so they override idle/walk animations.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing): Keep each base body part under 10,000 triangles; body geo, cage parts and skeleton must follow the standard R15 schema.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing): Unbind the rig from the mesh before texturing; do range-of-motion tests first to catch deformation issues early.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing): Make shirt, shorts, gloves and boots opaque for skin-tone control; use partial alpha on exposed skin to add anatomy detail.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing): Always supply albedo, metalness, roughness and normal maps for SurfaceAppearance; missing metalness defaults to plastic and missing roughness to smooth.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing): Test materials in Studio's actual lighting before finalising textures; adjust metalness after seeing it in-world.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing): Use attachments for particles, beams or lights on accessories, since attachments are valid in accessories while parts are not.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing): Reuse shared trim maps across the environment and props to minimise texture count.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/layered-clothing): Use hardpoint accessories (no cage deformation) for rigid items like helmets; a single mesh can be placed as a MeshPart and given a SurfaceAppearance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/design-dark-soundscapes): Enable SoundService.VolumetricAudio, then put a Sound inside an anchored, non-collidable, transparent Part sized to the sound source (e.g. a car) so audio spreads across its surface.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/converting): Select the layered accessory MeshPart in Explorer as the AFT Part field before clicking Next; AFT needs the MeshPart, not the Model, to convert.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio): Only import audio you have permission to use, such as audio you made yourself; otherwise use Creator Store assets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio): Use the Creator Store for free-to-use sound effects and music (100,000+ professionally produced tracks) instead of sourcing audio from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/meshes): Use the Importer to preview and check texture, rigging, skinning and animation data before importing; it flags issues and rejects meshes with errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/meshes): Name objects ending in _Att, _OuterCage, _InnerCage or _OuterCage in the source file to have the Importer convert them to Attachments and wrap objects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/meshes): Use Asset Manager for bulk-importing images and audio, but not meshes with rigging, skinning, animation, accessories or facial animation; use the Importer for those.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/meshes): Setting a TextureID cannot override a mesh's existing PBR textures; remove the PBR textures first if you want a single texture.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/meshes): Add a SurfaceAppearance child to apply up to four PBR maps without changing geometry; if both SurfaceAppearance and MaterialVariant are set, only SurfaceAppearance's texture maps apply.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/blender): Set Blender Scene > Units > Unit System to None and Rotation to Degrees so measurements match Studio studs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/blender): Use UV Editing trim sheets applied to many objects to add visual detail without extra texture imports, reducing memory impact.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/blender): Avoid Scene Unit Scale 0.01 for modeling: it causes camera, modifier and other Blender issues. Prefer FBX Unit Scale export.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/blender): Set Studio import Scale Unit to Stud and enable Insert Using Scene Position so objects keep Blender scale and position.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/export-textures): Test character assets multiple times at every stage, both inside Blender and after importing into Studio, not just at final export.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/export-textures): Choose texture workflow by need: embed textures for a single self-contained .fbx that simplifies import; unpack to .png when you need to test and swap texture maps quickly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Build with parts and terrain tools rather than the Toolbox; Toolbox assets may introduce unexpected issues into the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/finish-the-challenge): Clear any search filters in the Explorer before adding models so the folder tree is fully visible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/finish-the-challenge): Sample trusted Marketplace objects as an alternative to building models from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/finish-the-challenge): Objects can be copied and pasted from other Studio files into the template, as long as they are placed in the correct scoring folder.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters): placeholder
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/in-game-sounds): Parent a Sound to a part or attachment to make it positional; volume scales with player distance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials): Enable Expanded Terrain on Workspace before using custom terrain materials; it is off by default and requires reopening the place to take effect.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials): Early Access terrain users must re-set Expanded Terrain to Enabled, or custom materials may appear as grass.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials): Create several shades of a material (e.g., grass variants) as separate custom terrain materials instead of globally overriding one base material.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials): Before deleting a terrain material, replace it; terrain still using a deleted material turns bright purple.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials): Custom physical properties on MaterialVariant are not yet respected by custom terrain material slots; don't rely on them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/custom-terrain-materials): Heightmap/colormap imports may differ from before; colormap import is sensitive to material colors, so check results after importing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/template-files): Start from a pre-built template body to skip the technical avatar components; building character bodies from scratch takes substantial time and modeling expertise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/template-files): Use the .blend project file when working in Blender or following template creation guides; each zip also includes .fbx and PBR texture PNGs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages): Put a single object inside a Model before converting it to a package so child objects can be added, removed, or scaled later without breaking the package.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages): Use packages for greybox workflows: placeholder shapes can be published first, then copies auto-update as detailed versions are published.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world): Plan a shared material art direction upfront and reuse a small set of materials across many assets to stay within texture memory budgets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world): Use clean, non-repeating-looking trim maps for assets at varied scales; heavy stains or wear that repeat look wrong, so use a unique 1:1 map for heavily worn assets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world): Package one Surface Appearance node with trim maps and apply it to many meshes by laying out UVs creatively.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world): Use an alpha mask with AlphaMode set to Overlay and BrickColor/Color3 to tint one surface appearance into many color variations.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world): Name custom Material Variants with an _MV suffix to distinguish them from default materials.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world): Choose the Base Material for a Material Variant by physical properties (friction, density, footstep sounds), since variants inherit these and only visuals change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/materialize-the-world): Terrain can only be customized by overriding the 22 built-in materials; per-part Material Variants have no such limit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories): Export all avatar components (mesh, rig, cages) in a single .fbx or .gltf file before importing into Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories): Use the Accessory Fitting Tool to auto-generate attachment points for layered accessories instead of placing them manually.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/create): Create avatar meshes in Blender, Maya, or GIMP, then import into Studio; almost every custom avatar asset needs both third-party work and Studio tooling.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/create): Use Avatar Setup to auto-generate missing essentials (geometry partitions, rig armatures, skinning weights, cages, facial animations, attachments) before Marketplace publishing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling): Build meshes in a third-party tool like Blender or Maya and import into Studio; parts can't be created directly as custom shapes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling): Every mesh must meet general mesh specs; character and accessory meshes must also meet avatar/accessory specs for rigging and skinning data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling): Use PBR textures with multiple maps (color, roughness, reflectivity) for realistic shading across lighting environments.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/animating-parts): For fully textured MeshParts, use a SpecialMesh and tween VertexColor, since the MeshPart texture overrides tinted Color.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/animating-parts): If a model has many parts, tween one part (e.g. the button) and parent the tween script to it rather than the whole model.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/lights-and-polish): Use Post Processing Effects (lighting-based) to dramatically change a game's look and feel without rebuilding assets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes): Add an icon asset ID to each mission reward in the Missions config so tier reward icons appear on screen.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/facial-animation/create-face-accessories): Model face accessories together with the character body model and match accessory edges to head topology; this improves deformation fit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/facial-animation/create-face-accessories): Export only the accessory mesh, armature and cage meshes; remove animation data, lights and cameras before exporting FBX.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications): Freeze all transforms and set pivots to 0,0,0; characters must face positive Z and stand upright in positive Y before export.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications): Make all geometry watertight with no exposed holes or backfaces; use quads over N-gons where possible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications): Estimate tris by doubling the quad count; each body asset must stay under its tri budget.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications): Keep body parts fully opaque and make each part fill at least 50% of its bounding box in front, side and back views; head at least 50%.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications): Keep total body dimensions within min/max bounds (width 1-8, length 4-10, depth 0.5-4 studs); overlapping parts don't add up.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications): For custom skin tones, set exposed skin areas transparent so no default color is baked in; keep eyes and brows at 100% opacity.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications): Keep textures at or below 2048x2048 for Marketplace assets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications): Use Roblox's cage templates for outer cages named <Part>_OuterCage; do not delete vertices or alter UVs on cages.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Use solid modeling and the Toolbox to prototype quickly for testing and iteration.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/animation/play-character-animations): Override default character animations by setting the AnimationId of animateScript children (e.g. walk.WalkAnim) from a server Script on PlayerAdded/CharacterAdded so it applies to every player.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/studio): Install Roblox Studio on Windows or Mac; it is free and contains everything needed to start creating games.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor-state-machine): Set Play Mode to OnceAndHold on fall and land clips so they hold their final pose until the transition fires.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor-state-machine): Name state machine states to exactly match the state machine input names; the state name must match the input name.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor-state-machine): Assign distinct Priority values to competing transitions; equal priorities pick randomly among true expressions, making behaviour unpredictable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor-state-machine): Use BeforeFinished on a Finished transition to start blending before a clip ends, avoiding a visible pause when returning to locomotion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor-state-machine): Drive transitions from gameplay code by updating boolean graph parameters (e.g. Grounded) as the character leaves or touches the ground.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/manually-update-catalog-heads): Delete the original cage and use a Roblox-provided cage template matching the head shape instead of editing the broken cage; this is usually faster.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/assign-textures): In Blender, the material's Surface must be Principled BSDF; add a new material first if none exists, or textures will not import correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/assign-textures): Makeup assets have their own texture reassignment process; do not apply this general guide to them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/highlighting): Keep the 255 simultaneous client-side Highlight limit in mind; extra highlights are silently ignored.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/using-textures): Textures can be any 2D image, including custom drawings, not just the provided starter pack.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/creating-animations): Open the Animation Editor via Avatar tab > Animation; it appears at the bottom of the screen.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation): Base body must meet the 15-MeshPart avatar spec; single-mesh bodies can use Avatar Auto Setup to add required components.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation): Match imported base body scales to HumanoidDescription scales (BodyTypeScale, HeadScale, HeightScale, WidthScale, ProportionScale).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/change-the-animation): To swap the walk animation, search Explorer for 'walk', select WalkAnimationID, and paste your animation asset ID into its Value field.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/change-the-animation): Save the animation in the Animation Editor after every change, or updates will not appear in-game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/change-the-animation): Re-copy the asset ID from Creator Dashboard > Development Items > Animations > Configure > Copy Asset ID if paste fails.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup): Pick either the mesh template or the cage template before starting; you cannot switch templates after you begin makeup work.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup): Prefer the mesh template for most creators: it mimics real-world makeup application, with higher-fidelity mesh and UVs and minimal distortion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup): Use the cage template if you want fewer texture transfers (one vs two), which can reduce artifacts in makeup projection.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup): Export layers as separate PNGs and keep PBR texture names specific per component (e.g. Lips-Color, Lips-Metallic, Lips-Roughness) for easy reassignment.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup): In Procreate, export the color texture with transparency by disabling the background layer before sharing the PNG.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup): Test makeup by dropping Decal assets into a character's Makeup or Accessories folder in the reference place, then press Play to preview.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-4): Keep the Tool script with nested if statements open as a reference while students code, since it is the most helpful example.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/export-requirements): Blender: set Path Mode to Copy and enable Embed Textures so textures travel with the .fbx.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/export-requirements): Maya: enable Embed Media if textures must be imported as .png.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/use-case-tutorial-overview): Use Studio solid modeling (union and negate basic parts) for complex shapes; use Blender for meshes and import them via the Importer.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/use-case-tutorial-overview): Export meshes from Blender following the mesh and texture specifications and export requirements before importing into Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using): Humanoid rigs need an Animator child inside the Humanoid; non-Humanoid rigs need an AnimationController with a child Animator before LoadAnimation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using): To replace default character animations, put a server Script in ServerScriptService, hook CharacterAppearanceLoaded, stop playing tracks, then set Animate script AnimationId values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using): Multiple idle animations are picked randomly; set Weight values so the chance equals animation weight divided by total weight of all state animations (e.g. 5 and 10 gives 1/3 and 2/3).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using): Catalog avatar animation bundles (Ninja, Knight, Zombie, etc.) provide run, walk, jump, idle, fall, swim, swim idle and climb IDs you can reuse as replacements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor): Set Default Rotation Type to Quaternions before opening the Curve Editor; quaternion-to-Euler conversion is irreversible and can alter animation near gimbal lock.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor): Converting a KeyframeSequence to a CurveAnimation is one-way for the rotation track; verify rotation type first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor): Use Cubic interpolation mode when you need eased in/out motion; it is the only mode that allows tangent editing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor): Use Generate Curve > Bounce or Elastic on selected keyframes to mimic easing styles unavailable for curve animations.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor): Right-click an unset or set tangent and choose Set Tangent to Zero to reset it to default interpolation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/curve-editor): Changing Euler order on an existing Euler track can change the animation because keyframe values are preserved; set order before authoring keys.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/island-terrain): Customise map edges with Terrain Editor tools so the map reads as a natural island rather than a square.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/head-objects): Join and remove the extra head objects (Head_Geo, UpperTeeth_Geo, LowerTeeth_Geo, Tongue_Geo, RightLash_Geo, RightEye_Geo, LeftLash_Geo, LeftEye_Geo) during cleanup before exporting to avoid validation errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/laser-traps-with-beams): Reuse assets from your inventory via Toolbox Inventory tab after clicking Get Model on the Creator Store page.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/exporting): Unhide all objects and make them selectable in the Outliner, then select everything, before exporting so nothing is skipped.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/emotes): Use Studio's Animation Capture tool to convert a full-body video into a keyframe sequence for an emote.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/specifications): Keep rigid accessories to a single mesh with at most 4k triangles; make all geometry watertight with no holes or backfaces.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/specifications): Use quads wherever possible and avoid faces with 5 or more sides.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories/specifications): Keep Marketplace accessory textures at or below 2048x2048; larger textures are downscaled automatically.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/assets): Use Right-click > Copy Asset ID on Creator Store audio and paste into an AudioPlayer, not a Sound, for dynamic playback.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/assets): Keep imports to a single track in mp3, ogg, wav or flac, under 20 MB and 7 minutes, sample rate at most 48 kHz.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/assets): If Studio rejects an import, update your audio tool (older tools write invalid headers) and re-check the requirements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/photo-booth): Supply background images at 16:9 (1024×576) for the best result; 1 to 4 backgrounds can be passed to setBackgrounds().
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes): Enable "Uses Cages" in the 3D Importer when importing a model with cage meshes so Studio creates the cage objects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes): All targets and layers that pair with each other must share identical UV maps; cages pair by UV coordinates, not position.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes): A WrapLayer or WrapTarget can only be parented to a MeshPart, since cages behave as components of their parent.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/facial-animation/create-basic-heads): Plan the full range of facial poses before rigging so you avoid re-adjusting head joints later.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/facial-animation/create-basic-heads): Export FBX with Add Leaf Bones disabled, Custom Properties enabled, and NLA Strips, All Actions and Force Start/End Keyframes unchecked.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/publish-eyebrows-eyelashes): Import the .fbx face accessory and body, set Rig Type to Rthro for the reference body, then run Accessory Fitting Tool with Accessory > Eyelash or Eyebrow asset type.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/publish-eyebrows-eyelashes): Rename face accessories to EyelashAccessory or EyebrowAccessory if required before upload.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/texture-painting): Adjust brush color, size and falloff per modification; settings vary with the change you intend to make.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/texture-painting): For more unique looks on meshes, consider alpha transparencies, unique seams, PBR textures, or other Blender texturing techniques.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/exporting): Before exporting an accessory, delete extra objects like lights, cameras, and mannequins so only the accessory mesh is exported.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/exporting): Apply all modifiers to the mesh object before exporting from Blender.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/accessory-fitting-tool): Pick Clothing for layered accessories (inner/outer cage wraps body and clothing) and Accessory for rigid items attached to a fixed attachment point.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/accessory-fitting-tool): Set body type to the scale used while sculpting; it only affects rigid accessories unless the body part has a different AvatarPartScaleType StringValue.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/accessory-fitting-tool): Test accessories on multiple default bodies, layered items, and animations before generating, since fit issues show up across combinations.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/accessory-fitting-tool): For minor cage tweaks use the AFT cage editor; for major fit or sculpting changes, edit cage meshes in third-party modeling software and reimport.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/accessory-fitting-tool): Disable Move snapping when editing individual cage vertices for detailed positional changes; use Falloff Distance to control how many nearby vertices follow.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/accessory-fitting-tool): Keep rigid accessories inside the bounding box; the box turns red when the accessory is outside the valid placement space.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/accessory-fitting-tool): Toggle auto-skinning between EnabledPreserved and EnabledOverride; override can give better results depending on asset and skinning quality.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation): Set AnchorPoint to (0.5, 0.5) before tweening position or size so the object grows or moves around its center.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-player-tools): A tool must contain a part named Handle; without it the tool drops to the ground when equipped.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-player-tools): Set GripPos (and GripForward/GripRight/GripUp) on the tool to fix how it is held rather than leaving it gripped at the center.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Build UI with relative sizes and positions plus UISizeConstraint; develop for lower resolutions first so it scales up to large TVs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Keep important UI inside TV-safe areas, since some TVs crop content at the edges.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Use ScrollingFrame to reduce on-screen clutter as UI scales up on large displays.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Add shortcuts or supplemental buttons for essential in-game actions to speed up console navigation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Show button icons matching the player's device; use InputActionLabel for Input Action System bindings, or GetImageForKeyCode/GetStringForKeyCode otherwise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Use haptic feedback for explosions, collisions, attention events and UI notifications, but avoid overuse since constant vibration annoys players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines): Add sound effects to confirm selections and signal menu traversal for controller-driven UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/design-the-world): Use Terrain Paint to change a level's look (snow, water, lava) without altering terrain shape; paint changes appearance only.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/design-the-world): Recolor terrain materials via Workspace.Terrain Properties > MaterialColors to create unique palettes from default materials like Snow.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/model-generation): Use PredefinedSchema "Car5" for a car with body and four wheels; functional vehicles need at least five separate MeshParts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/model-generation): Scale generated models with GetExtentsSize and ScaleTo to a target size, using the largest axis as the reference.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/model-generation): Anchor all BaseParts of a generated model so separate meshes do not fall apart when spawned.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/project-files): Start layered accessory work from Roblox's Clothing_Cage_Template and Rig_and_Attachments_Template files rather than from scratch, for correct naming and attachment points.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/project-files): Pick the mannequin matching the target body type (Classic, Rthro Normal, or Rthro Slender) so caging fits the intended avatar body.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/grouping-the-parts): Position the camera above the build and drag-select only the intended parts to avoid grouping stray objects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/grouping-the-parts): Verify grouping by moving the model; if pieces stay behind or extra objects move, undo (Ctrl/Cmd+Z) and regroup.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace): Create complex meshes in Blender or Maya and import as MeshParts for far more detail than Studio solid modeling allows.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/adding-wings): Driftspeeder must have only one body, but can have as many wings and other parts as needed for race vehicles.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/adding-wings): Enable Rotate snapping at 45° before positioning wings to align them quickly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/adding-wings): Press Ctrl+R (or ⌘+R) to rotate a selected part 90 degrees.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/adding-wings): Turn Move snapping off if the wing won't move smoothly when dragging into the work area.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications): Upload textures as .png, .jpg, .tga, or .bmp; these are the only formats accepted for separately uploaded textures in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications): Size textures to surface area: 256x256 for 5x5 stud objects, 512x512 for 10x10, 1024x1024 for 20x20. Manual sizing can reduce memory use.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications): Keep mesh objects to one material; a mesh can only have one material assigned.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications): Use a single UV set per component (humanoid or accessory); Studio does not allow multiple UV sets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes/apply-deformation): Confirm the target MeshPart has a WrapTarget and the deforming MeshPart has a WrapLayer before applying a deformation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes/apply-deformation): Duplicate the layered mesh before testing so you keep an original copy for later deformations.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes/apply-deformation): Parent target and layered MeshParts in one Model, then add a WeldConstraint with Part0 as the layered mesh and Part1 as the target.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes/apply-deformation): Change WrapLayer.Order to trigger deformation; adjust it anytime to fix stacking order between layers such as a T-shirt over a sweater.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes/apply-deformation): After deformation computes it is retained, so you can delete the duplicate layer and weld to leave one deformed mesh and simplify the workspace.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes/apply-deformation): Every layered mesh added to a target must share the same UV layout as the target, because Roblox pairs layers to the target by UV.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/cage-meshes/apply-deformation): When stacking layers, weld each new layer to the previous layered mesh (Part0 = previous layer, Part1 = new layer) and set WrapLayer.Order so the later layer renders on top.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/surface-art): Install SurfaceArt from Creator Store Packages, move the SurfaceArt model into ReplicatedStorage; module starts automatically on run.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/surface-art): Any BasePart tagged SurfaceCanvas works as a canvas; set its SurfaceCanvasFace attribute (Front/Back/Right/Left/Top/Bottom). Default is Right.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/surface-art): Custom art assets currently apply to all tagged canvases; you cannot give different canvases different asset sets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/speech-to-text): Set VoiceChatService.UseAudioApi to Enabled to use the latest voice API required for AudioDeviceInput.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art): Replace greybox with polished assets made in third-party modeling tools once layout and gameplay are validated.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art): Import and configure assets in a central asset library inside the game for easy access and reuse across the world.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-assets): Use Importer instead of the API for custom 3D models when you need a 3D preview, error checks and import settings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-assets): Don't edit .rbxm/.rbxmx files outside Roblox Studio; they may fail to upload or function.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-assets): Meshes are accepted only if downloaded from the Asset Delivery API; otherwise use Importer for meshes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/sculpting): Use only sculpting tools that preserve vertices (Non-Destructive Modeling); other Blender sculpt tools may destroy the mesh topology needed for Roblox avatars.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/assemble-an-asset-library): Anchor every static environment asset; anchored parts skip physics simulation, freeing GPU for low-end devices. Exception: moving parts like doors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/assemble-an-asset-library): Organise imported assets into folders by data type (trim sheets, decals, modular assets, props) before importing into Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/assemble-an-asset-library): Create MaterialVariants from the base material closest to your texture so it inherits physical properties; enable Set as Override to apply to terrain.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/assemble-an-asset-library): Use Organic pattern on MaterialVariant tiling to randomise output and reduce visible tiling on organic surfaces.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/assemble-an-asset-library): Store trim sheet texture maps in one SurfaceAppearance package and duplicate it onto meshes; edits to the package propagate to all instances.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/assemble-an-asset-library): Convert assets to packages and enable AutoUpdate on PackageLink so edits propagate across instances and projects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/validation-tool): Install the Validation Tool add-on in Blender 3.5.1+; earlier versions may behave unexpectedly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/validation-tool): Set the Target Armature via eyedropper on the armature object (often named Joints or Armature) before running checks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/validation-tool): Freeze geometry location, rotation and scale to 0,0,0 before export; auto-fix may alter your rig, so back up first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/validation-tool): Keep body UVs within the 0-1 range; this must be fixed manually.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/validation-tool): Avoid N-gons, non-manifold geometry and vertex color data; keep meshes watertight and within polycount budgets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/validation-tool): Remove extra layers, orphan data and unused materials from the scene before export.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/validation-tool): Check that cage meshes do not intersect character geometry; use a fresh template cage if cage UV auto-fix fails.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Choose an art style first and use it as visual ground rules for every asset decision; a mood/theme board of reference images helps establish it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Make tileable textures seamless and give all elements equal visible distribution; avoid a single distinctive patch that reveals repetition.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Keep tileable textures at or below 1024x1024; performance cost rises as size approaches the maximum.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Avoid highly contextual details on trim sheets; they must serve many objects and specific stains or marks become obvious when repeated.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Make trim sheets equal in length on X and Y, and keep them at or below 1024x1024.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Make each modular asset's max width and height divisible by the kit's smallest dimension so pieces rotate and move in clean increments.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Make modular assets at least 5 studs tall and wide and snap on 5-stud grid increments to prevent clashing geometry.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Split large modular pieces (e.g. hallways) into named child meshes (UpperTrim, LowerTrim, Wall) to customize material, collision and rendering per part.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Test modular kits from multiple angles often; clashing geometry usually means a pivot point is inconsistent.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Give props logical pivots (back for wall-mounted, bottom for floor items) so they place universally regardless of surface.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/develop-polished-assets): Add only enough prop geometry that the object is recognizable from its silhouette, to protect performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories): Export all avatar components in a single .fbx or .gltf file for import into Studio when creating a rigid accessory in Blender or Maya.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories): A rigid accessory requires a single MeshPart nested under a single Model; textures are applied via a child SurfaceAppearance or the MeshPart TextureID property.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories): Use the Accessory Fitting Tool to automatically generate attachment points for rigid accessories instead of placing them manually.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/validation-tool): Don't rely solely on the validation tool; verify general mesh requirements and Roblox layered accessory specs manually before importing into Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/validation-tool): Select the accessory object and click Check Assets; red (Blender) or yellow (Maya) results mark issues, grey/green mean passed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/validation-tool): Freezing transforms via the Transforms auto-fix may change your rig; check the rig after applying it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/validation-tool): Intersection warnings on sleeve holes, neck and torso edges are default behavior and can be ignored.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/validation-tool): Remove extra layers, unused materials, orphan data (Blender) or construction history (Maya), and unnecessary keyframes before export.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/validation-tool): Name attachment points using the correct convention; the auto-fix parents unexpected attachment points.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/validation-tool): Check texture resolution against Roblox texture requirements; use the output messages to resolve texture issues.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads): Export all avatar components (head, body, cages, attachments) in a single .fbx or .gltf for import into Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/converting): Judge rigid accessory fit using the AFT preview window and default character; the clothing mannequin in the viewport does not accurately show how rigid accessories attach.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/converting): Set the asset type to Accessory and choose the attachment location (e.g. Back) before fitting, since this determines where the accessory attaches to the body.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/converting): If you accidentally select another object while adjusting, click back into the AFT panel to reselect the accessory and resume with Move, Scale, and Rotate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/converting): Test the generated accessory by equipping it to a character with the Avatar Setup tool before publishing or saving it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/converting): A generated Class.Accessory can be uploaded to the Marketplace, used in your game via HumanoidDescription or by placing it under a character Model, or saved to the Toolbox.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/properties): Use Edit > Save as Default [Instance] to make a configured instance (e.g. anchored MeshPart) the default for new inserts of that type.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/properties): Defaults are stored locally in DefaultInstances.rbxm, not on your account; change the folder via Settings > Studio > Instance Defaults Dir.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/properties): Reset defaults via Edit > Reset Default [Instance]; existing instances keep their properties, only new inserts change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/properties): Group as Model and Group as Folder ignore instance defaults; Position/CFrame/Origin/WorldPivot are not governed by defaults.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-studio): Use packages rather than loose assets where possible; packages propagate changes to all copies across games and offer versioning.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-studio): Use basic parts for greyboxing or prototyping; they are performant and work well with native physics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-studio): Use the Material Generator and Texture Generator in Studio to create materials and textures from natural-language prompts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-studio): Use PBR textures when you need realistic surfaces, for assets destined for the Creator Store, Marketplace or your game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/creating-engaging-experiences): When using your own project, base the tutorial on your game's main player actions (e.g. collecting wheat and selling at a shop booth in a farming game).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/creating-engaging-experiences): Use Beams with Attachments on the player and goal objects to draw a visible guiding path; Beams also suit lasers or force fields.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor): Keep clip authoring in the Animation Editor and use the Animation Graph Editor only to organize clips into logic trees driving behaviour.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/toolbox): Right-click an inserted asset in Explorer and choose Disable Scripts to use it without its bundled scripts running.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/toolbox): Use the Creations tab to find and configure assets you published or that groups you belong to published; Edit Asset lets you restore earlier versions.
