- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/toolbox): Use the advanced filter to narrow results by verified creator, specific creator, plugin sale price, or audio file length.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup/specifications): Name head mesh and cage with the TransferTarget_ prefix, and TransferTexture_Eyes_01, TransferTexture_Face_01, TransferTexture_Lips_01 (increment the number for extra regions).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/makeup/specifications): Check all accessory texture specs against Roblox's texture requirements, and read the PBR textures guidance before exporting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/from-existing): Apply all transforms (Object > Apply > All Transforms) before export; unapplied scale/rotation causes unexpected orientation when importing into Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/from-existing): Face the accessory -Y forward and center it at 0,0,0 in Blender so it imports at the center of the Studio camera.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/from-existing): Remove lights, cameras, and mannequin meshes and apply modifiers so only the accessory mesh is exported.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/from-existing): PBR textures are optional for accessories but add realism; verify them in Material Preview mode.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/script-editor): Use Find All / Replace All (Ctrl+Shift+F) to refactor names across all scripts in the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/importing): Use Studio's Importer (Home tab > Import) for .fbx or .gltf files; it previews the asset and error-checks it against Studio's 3D requirements before import.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/importing): Imported assets populate as a Model with MeshParts whose TextureID carries the textures automatically applied during import.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/importing): If textures fail to load, upload the image via Asset Manager, wait for moderation to clear, then paste its asset ID into the MeshPart's TextureID property.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-2D-audio): Set SoundService.DefaultListenerLocation to Character so a default listener follows the player; the engine then auto-creates an AudioDeviceOutput under SoundService.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/atmosphere): Balance Offset with Density: low Offset can show skybox through terrain, high Offset can show too much distant detail.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/atmosphere): Use high Offset to create a horizon silhouette; use low Offset to blend distant objects into sky for open worlds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/atmosphere): Combine Color with a high Haze value to make the Color effect visible, e.g. smoky or foggy moods.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/atmosphere): Glare only shows when Haze is above 0; Decay only shows when Glare and Haze are both above 0.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/publish-to-marketplace): Upload accessories as Accessory objects (use Accessory Fitting Tool to convert Models); shoes need LeftShoeAccessory and RightShoeAccessory grouped as a Model.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/skin-a-humanoid-model): Set armature Display As to Sticks in Object Data Properties to keep bones from obstructing weight painting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/skin-a-humanoid-model): Export the skinned model as FBX using Blender export settings for use in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): Keep cage geometry equidistant where possible to reduce texture and layered accessory distortion; note this isn't always achievable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): Align vertex-colored areas to lips, eyebrows and hairline so makeup transfer and hair/facial hair/eyelash accessories fit correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): Align non-vertex-colored areas (nose, eyes, ears) carefully for accurate makeup transfer and accessory fit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): Keep the cage middle axis at origin when possible for consistency.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): Line neck cage parts up close to render mesh so Hidden Surface Removal hides the underlying surface under layered accessories; HSR is disabled on head but not body.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): Make the cage barely cover the render mesh without intersecting it; close fit improves layered accessory fit and makeup accuracy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): For eyes, use three verts on upper eyelid and three on lower eyelid, equidistant, and enclose the open eye edge up to the eye mesh.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): For eyebrows, establish head and tail first, then place three upper and three lower verts equidistant.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): For missing eyebrows, use facial proportion rules and adjust for stylization and face shape.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): Outline lips inside and outside; upper lip on green vertex color, lower lip on purple, equidistant spacing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): Start the nose bridge at inner eyes and end two faces above the lips.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): For ears, use the triangle portion for the top and the quad for the bottom; keep hairline shape around the ear.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): For sideburns, define shape in front of and around ears and decide where hairline ends at neck base to guide layered hair fit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/caging-best-practices): For faces missing features, use proportion guides: face thirds (hairline-brows, brows-nose bottom, nose bottom-chin), eyes halfway down head, head about five eyes wide, pupils aligned with mouth corners.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/global-wind): Set Workspace.GlobalWind in Studio's Properties window (X, Y, Z) to control direction and strength for grass and clouds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/global-wind): Use the Wind Direction widget (View menu) to tune Speed, Yaw and Pitch visually with a wind sock model.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/post-processing-effects): Add effects to Lighting for global looks (e.g. sun rays) and to Camera for per-player effects (e.g. blur when a UI menu is open).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/export): Apply or delete all modifiers and remove non-Roblox-supported objects from the model before exporting a layered accessory.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/export): If Maya textures must import as .png, enable Embed Media in the FBX export options.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects): Use Packages for repeated objects (e.g. a tree duplicated many times); edit once in the package and the change propagates to every instance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects): Start environments with graybox assets in packages, then swap in final art later; replaced assets keep original positions and orientations.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/go-beyond-the-challenge): Add new models from the asset library to change the scenery and keep the simulator world visually fresh.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/play-background-music): Parent background music Sound objects to SoundService so they play at the same volume everywhere, unlike sounds parented to parts which are positional.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/exporting-animations): Publish animations via Animation Editor > ... > Publish to Roblox, give a title and description, then Submit before using them in-game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/exporting-animations): Copy the exported animation ID (green 'ID Copied!' confirmation) and use it in game scripts so the game knows which animation to play.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/exporting-animations): If the Animation Editor is blank after reopening Studio, go to Avatar tab, click Animation, then reselect the Figure on the pedestal.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Use built-in materials where possible to save memory budget for the custom textures central to your game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Convert assets into packages during creation to avoid duplicate assets with different IDs, which hurt performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Reuse meshes and textures instead of importing duplicates; resize, rotate and overlap them to build varied environments with few draw calls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs): Author all animation clips on a standard R15 Block Avatar rig so they play on every supported avatar appearance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs): Convert clips to CurveAnimation before saving; conversion is irreversible and may break some third-party animation plugins, so verify data first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs): Keep every clip except Jump looping; enable Loop in Properties under ServerStorage > RBX_ANIMSAVES > [rig].
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs): Trim clips to 10 seconds or less and keep body parts within 1.5 studs of their start position per frame, or validation fails.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs): Use the identical Parent model name string on every clip in a pack, or Studio creates separate models and validation fails.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design): Use responsive layout patterns: collapse less-important buttons into a menu button on small screens, show them in full on desktop; adapt to landscape and portrait if supported.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design): Build UI panels that react to parent size, e.g. split-view side panels that collapse on narrow screens and expand on wide ones.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design): Use content-driven sizing and automatic text wrapping so labels don't overflow or become unreadable as screen size changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design): Tie UI sizing to user text/UI scaling accessibility settings so larger preferences produce larger, more readable UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design): Choose font sizes readable at the smallest expected mobile size; test on real devices; avoid decorative or thin fonts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/adaptive-design): Use high contrast between text/buttons and backgrounds, and strong hierarchy via size, color, or placement, for varied in-game lighting and TV viewing distances.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/accelerated-workflows): Use the mesh generator or MCP command to get a textured MeshPart from a text description.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/accelerated-workflows): Use the material generator for seamless tiling MaterialVariants on large surfaces like terrain, floors and walls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/accelerated-workflows): Use Avatar Auto-Setup to automatically rig, cage and segment 3D models into animatable avatars.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/appearance): Body scale properties (height, width, head, body type, proportion) have no effect on R6 body types.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/appearance): Use SetAccessories with Order values for layered accessories rather than setting individual accessory strings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/appearance): Custom layered accessory cage implementations cannot be published to the Marketplace; use custom cage meshes only for in-game assets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/appearance): For non-R15 or Humanoid-less targets, add a Weld with Part0 as the accessory and Part1 as the parent part for layered accessories.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/appearance): Layered accessory cages need matching UV maps: target outer cage and layer inner/outer cages must share corresponding vertex UVs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/templates): Baseplate uses a 4x4 stud-aligned grid texture; use it as the reference floor for sizing parts to stud measurements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/colors-and-textures): Import textures via a part's TextureID property in Properties; click Add Image, then Choose File and Create. Game must be published first or Add Image won't appear.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/colors-and-textures): Select multiple parts in Explorer with Ctrl/Cmd-click, or click one part then Shift-click another to select the range, to apply a texture to many parts at once.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/in-experience-asset-creation): Generate images procedurally with AssetService:CreateEditableImage, DrawRectangle and DrawImageTransformed, then destroy temporary EditableImages after compositing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/project-files): Edit the full-body std_cage_deformable helper mesh to propagate vertex changes to all 15 body-part cages; remove the helper mesh after importing the FBX into Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/project-files): Lola.fbx has no inner/outer cage mesh data, so it cannot equip layered accessories; use a caged model if layered clothing is needed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/project-files): Caged mannequin files may not import into Studio correctly until unconfigured cage objects are modified.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/intro-to-roblox): Use the Explorer panel as a core foundation for organizing and selecting objects in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/create-an-environment-with-terrain): Use Material Overrides to replace default terrain textures with custom texture assets for a more distinctive look.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting): Set Lighting.LightingStyle to Realistic instead of the default Soft so local light sources cast precise shadows.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting): Tint Ambient, OutdoorAmbient and ColorShift_Top together to pull the whole scene toward one palette rather than tuning them separately.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting): Set ShadowSoftness to 0 for crisp shadow edges when you want dramatic transitions, such as stepping from outside into a tunnel.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting): Set only one of ClockTime or TimeOfDay; they are linked and changing one updates the other.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting): Use Atmosphere Density to hide the edge of terrain so players do not see where the world ends.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting): Keep Atmosphere Offset mid-range: lower values blend distant objects into the sky for endless-feeling worlds, higher values keep clear silhouettes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/customize-global-lighting): Adjust Ambient, ClockTime and Density by eye in the viewport after applying prompts; the values are starting points, not final.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/tutorials): Use Blender 3.0+ for Roblox avatar asset tutorials; the official tutorial content targets that version.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/material-reference): Start PBR surfaces from baseline values: glossy plastic R 0.1/M 0.0, matte plastic R 0.4/M 0.0, pure metals R 0.2-0.3/M 1.0.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/material-reference): Use Metalness 0.0 for non-metal materials; only set Metalness near 1.0 for true metals, since fabrics like silk and velvet use 0.4-0.6.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/material-reference): Combine roughness and metalness values with PBR textures to render most real-world surfaces; compare results against Substance Painter renders to calibrate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/events): Use animation event markers (e.g. FootStep) to trigger sounds or effects at exact animation frames instead of relying on timed waits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/events): Events are available across the whole animation once created; copy and paste a marker to reuse it, e.g. FootStep for both left and right foot.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/events): Enable Show Animation Events from the timeline settings icon to reveal the Animation Events bar.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/modeling-setup): 
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/modeling-setup): Keep accessory geometry within the polycount budget and avoid IP that infringes other creators' work, inside or outside Roblox.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/modeling-setup): After bevelling, run Merge Vertices by Distance at 0.01 so the mesh shares vertices instead of leaving near-duplicate verts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/modeling-setup): Use Clamp Overlap when bevelling to avoid self-intersecting geometry on rounded edges.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house): Track all furniture and props in one planning document, prioritized by room type, to avoid making too few or too many assets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house): Reuse tileable textures and transparent textures with color tint to stay within memory budget instead of making unique albedo maps.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house): Use packages for shared objects, room states and SurfaceAppearance materials so one edit updates every instance; set parent packages to auto-update.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/emotes/export): Enable Embed Media in Maya export only if you need textures imported as .png.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/test-studio): Test custom character bodies in both Avatar Setup and the Avatar test place regularly while iterating, not only once at the end.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/test-studio): If skin tone or textures look wrong under different lighting, re-adjust texture components in the modeling tool.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/foundational-gameplay-systems): Keep a visual cue for the player's new state after a transition, such as the corrupt-room look, so they know they are in a different location.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/project-files): Start avatar and clothing work from the R15 rig template and cage templates rather than building rigs from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/project-files): Use the Blender Calisthenics tool to check skinning data on characters and clothing before upload.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/project-files): Apply UV textures to the skinned tree model with Class.SurfaceAppearance; the maple tree packs soil, branches and leaves as separate meshes on one armature.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/modeling-tips): In the Blender Outliner, hide objects you aren't editing using the Hide In Viewport icon to avoid unintentional changes to other objects or collections.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/modeling-tips): Shift-click the Hide In Viewport icon on a parent object (e.g. Cage or Joints) to bulk hide or unhide its child objects that are not disabled.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/modeling-tips): Switch between X-Ray and Wireframe viewport options while modeling to better see vertices and shapes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/adaptive-animation): Import custom characters via 3D Importer with Rig Type set to Custom, then click Create in Remap Rig panel to generate HumanoidRigDescription and per-hand DigitsRigDescription objects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core): Use solid modeling to plan platform shapes first, then replace simple parts with polished imported models at the end.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/procedural-models): Use Size for structural changes (regenerates content) and Scale for uniform resizing (ScaleTo on existing results) to avoid unnecessary regeneration.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/specifications): Keep layered accessories a single mesh, at most 4k triangles, watertight with no exposed holes or backfaces; prefer quads and avoid faces with 5+ sides.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/specifications): Keep layered clothing within 8 x 8 x 8 studs (centered on attachment point); eyebrows and eyelashes within 1.5 x 0.5 x 0.5 studs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/specifications): Keep Marketplace layered accessory textures at or below 2048x2048; higher resolutions are auto-downscaled.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/specifications): Use Studio's Accessory Fitting Tool to auto-generate correct attachment names; manually use the names listed per accessory type (e.g. BodyFrontAttachment for tops, WaistCenterAttachment for bottoms).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/layered-accessories/specifications): Set MeshPart Material to Plastic, Transparency to 0, and VertexColor to 1,1,1; remove scripts and extra parts from the Accessory before Marketplace upload.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility): Pick text and background colors with sufficient contrast; avoid light-on-light or dark-on-dark text.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility): Don't rely on color alone; add symbols alongside colors so colorblind players can tell differences in gameplay and UI.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility): Don't convey game events only through sound; pair audio cues with visual indicators for hearing-impaired players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility): Respect GuiService.PreferredTransparency by tagging UI backgrounds 'TransparentBack' and multiplying BackgroundTransparency by the preferred value.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility): Respect GuiService.PreferredTextSize; avoid TextScaled and fixed sizes where text should grow, and use AutomaticSize/TextWrapped so layouts adapt.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility): Check GuiService.ReducedMotionEnabled and set tween time to 0 or switch to a fade-in instead of positional motion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/models): Set a PrimaryPart on models joined by WeldConstraints or Motor6Ds so the pivot and bounding box move with the correct part.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/models): Use Alt (Windows) or Option (Mac) click selection cycling to pick hidden child parts without moving the camera.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/models): Keep Creator Store model dependencies within 15,000 and each mesh under 20,000 triangles; set reasonable scale and orientation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/models): Distribute any child audio asset on the Creator Store before parenting it to the model, or the audio will not be audible at runtime.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/designing-your-speeder): Turn off Collisions in the Geometric toolbar and turn off Move snapping before positioning parts so they pass through each other and move freely.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/designing-your-speeder): Use F to focus the camera on a selected part; use WASD, right-drag, and scroll to move, rotate, and zoom while building.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/designing-your-speeder): Click inside the game world if the camera stops responding to WASD.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/basic-particle-effects): Set ParticleEmitter Lifetime to a short Min,Max range (e.g. 0.5, 1) for sparkles; the default 5-10s is too long for that effect.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/avatar-settings): Set Clothing Scale and Accessory Scale limit bounds to restrict oversized user items; 0% means strictly inside the body bounding box.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/texturing): Unwrap with Smart UV Project and set Island Margin to .02 to keep UV islands from bleeding into each other.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/texturing): Name texture image files with an affix like _TXT to keep texture files organized.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/texturing): Mark the target areas on the 3D object in Texture Paint mode before painting the 2D atlas, since Smart UV layouts make it hard to see which island maps where.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/texturing): Set the brush Falloff to the flattest icon so painted edges stay sharp.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/texturing): Use Ctrl/Cmd+Z to undo accidental brushstrokes and hold F while dragging to resize the brush.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/texturing): PBR textures are optional for accessories; adding them adds visual flair but requires additional third-party software.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Check the .fbx/.gltf/.obj against Studio's mesh requirements before importing to reduce errors or unexpected behavior.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Set Scale Unit to match the modelling software's units so the mesh imports at the correct size.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Disable Upload to Roblox while iterating on a model's design to avoid creating multiple copies in Toolbox and Asset Manager.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Use Import as Package for assets reused or redistributed; packages auto-update and can be shared.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Enable Merge Meshes to combine all MeshParts into one MeshPart, reducing instance count, instead of a Model of many parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Enable Make Double Sided only when needed; double-sided rendering is more performance intensive.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Leave Keep Zero Influence Bones disabled unless needed; the importer removes them by default.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Use presets (Studio Default, Last Imported, or custom saved presets) and Apply settings to all to speed up bulk imports from the queue.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/test): Face accessories import as MeshParts; convert them with the Accessory Fitting Tool so they're compatible with the dynamic head.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/sharing-animations): Animations saved to .RBXM can be shared with friends and groups without re-uploading, per Roblox asset privacy changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/create-volcanoes): Break a real-world reference into distinct components, each with its own visual and behavioural traits, before choosing which VFX objects to use for each.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/create-volcanoes): Make emitter host parts invisible (Transparency 1) and attach ParticleEmitters to them to control emission area without visible geometry.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/sharing-your-speeders): Newly uploaded models may show a placeholder thumbnail until Roblox moderators review them; this can take some time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/skybox): Make each skybox face seamless along all edges of neighbouring faces when folded into a cube, or the panorama will show visible seams.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/skybox): Import custom skybox images into Roblox before assigning them to a Sky object's face properties.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/skybox): Set SunAngularSize or MoonAngularSize to 0 to hide only the sun or moon while keeping stars; set CelestialBodiesShown false to hide all celestial bodies.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/gltf-export): Enable glTF export via File > Beta Features > glTF Export before use; the feature is still in beta and specs may change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/gltf-export): Export only assets uploaded to your own or your group's account, or mesh/image assets explicitly shared with you, to avoid permission conflicts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-dcc): Uploaded textures must follow Studio's texture specifications; Asset Manager accepts .png, .jpg, .gif, .tga, .bmp and supports bulk import.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-dcc): Uploaded sounds must meet Roblox's audio asset requirements; Asset Manager accepts .ogg, .mp3, .flac, .wav with bulk import.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/overview-dcc): Video assets can be imported as .mp4 or .mov, but only if all Roblox video requirements are met.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization): Set AutoLocalize to false on GuiBase2d objects that should never be translated, such as NPC names or street signs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization): Use the translation table programmatically to show language-specific images or assets, or contextual translations.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/viewport-frames): To show a workspace object in a ViewportFrame, duplicate it and parent the duplicate; the original stays in Workspace for gameplay.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/viewport-frames): For reflections inside a ViewportFrame, add a Sky child with all six Skybox textures set, and give parts Reflectance > 0 or Glass/Foil material.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/viewport-frames): For MeshParts to reflect in a ViewportFrame, apply a SurfaceAppearance with a properly configured MetalnessMap.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive): Use MaterialService with custom materials and SurfaceAppearance to make each room's normal and corrupted states look distinct.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/test): Check textures against Roblox texture specifications when texturing the character.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/artist/work-with-particle-emitters): Set ParticleEmitter Size to between 1 and 10 for best results; different textures look better at different sizes, so tune per texture.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/artist/work-with-particle-emitters): Set ParticleEmitter Speed between 10 (slow) and 100 (fast) to control whether particles burst out quickly or drift out slowly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/importing): Import layered accessory .fbx files via Home > Import in Studio; the Importer previews the layers before you select Import.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating/importing): If textures fail to load, upload the image in Asset Manager and set the MeshPart's TextureID to the image asset ID.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Use fewer, larger meshes instead of many small pieces to reduce frustum culling cost. Reduce visible Beams, ParticleEmitters and Humanoids.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Disable CastShadow on complex meshes, highly transparent parts, and unimportant instances; fully transparent parts without decals should have it disabled.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Reduce visual changes to models with Humanoids or skinned MeshParts; use Bone.Transform over CFrame/Size changes to avoid bounding box updates.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art): Use alpha masks in PBR textures to customize colors instead of duplicating textures; staff article says this saves time and optimizes performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art): Check General mesh specifications and Blender/Maya FBX export settings before importing third-party models to avoid import problems.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art): Follow Texturing requirements and PBR texture guidance so materials render correctly in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art): Use the Importer, Accessory Fitting Tool, Material Generator and Texture Generator Studio tools to speed up asset pipelines.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design): Document the visual language in a Style Guide so the whole team applies it consistently.
- 2026-10-30 Xaiyne [0:40](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=40): Use a reference image with a plain white background for AI 3D generation; it gives cleaner results for hats.
- 2026-10-30 Xaiyne [1:21](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=81): Keep generated meshes under Roblox limits: texture at 2K max and under 4,000 triangles, or upload will be rejected.
- 2026-10-30 Xaiyne [3:28](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=208): Ungroup the imported model, rename the part to exactly 'Handle', and place it inside an Accessory before testing fit.
- 2026-10-30 Xaiyne [4:09](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=249): Use Avatar > Accessory tool to test fit; scale until the box turns blue, then raise it so it doesn't clip the head.
